### Task 6: 재사용 관문 (가)와 AD 입력 manifest 쓰기 (AD.2 (가)/(나), AD.5 6, AD.6 3)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/reuse.py`, `scripts/write_ad_inputs.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_reuse.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 5 `inputs.inputs_doc`, `input_paths`, `input_manifest`; `flymon.ac.manifest.MODEL_MANIFEST`; `blocks.read_jsonl`.
- Produces:
  - `CONFIRM_DIGEST`, `EVAL_DIGEST`, `COMPARED`(재개 · 분석이 다시 비교하는 필드)
  - `stage1_paths(spec) -> dict(result, init, points)`, `coff_records(init_path, flies) -> list`, `stage1_fly_records(points_path, flies, point) -> list`
  - `reuse_fields(confirm_digest, eval_digest, spec=SPEC, model_path=MODEL_MANIFEST) -> dict`, `reuse_problems(fields, spec=SPEC) -> list[str]`, `changed_since(old, new) -> list[str]`
  - `scripts/write_ad_inputs.py [--smoke | --bench N]` — 종료 0 OK, 2 `STOP_REUSE` · `STOP_SET`

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_reuse.py`:

```python
"""AD.2 (가) / AD.5 6: the model manifest, the confirmation and evaluation digests and the six C-off point-0 records
reused from stage 1 must be stage 1's; any mismatch is STOP_REUSE. Fake stage-1 trees in tmp_path."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import reuse, store
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL, SPEC
from flymon.rescope import blocks

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def fake_stage1(root: Path, *, frozen=True, model_status="FROZEN"):
    (root / "results/summary").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    mp = root / "results/summary/ac_model_manifest.json"
    mp.write_text(json.dumps({"status": model_status, "recovery_per_pulse": 0.001}))
    b = root / "results/m4/stage1/BRAIN"
    (b / "logs").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (b / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps(dict(model=dict(sha256=store.sha256_file(mp)),
                                                   confirm_digest=reuse.CONFIRM_DIGEST, eval_digest=reuse.EVAL_DIGEST)))
    recs = [dict(kind="situation_eval", fly=g, point=0, arm="FLY" if g < 12 else "COFF", switched=[0] * 20,
                 frozen=frozen or g < 12, label=LABEL) for g in range(18)]
    (b / "logs/situations_init.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in recs))


def fields():
    return reuse.reuse_fields(reuse.CONFIRM_DIGEST, reuse.EVAL_DIGEST, SPEC)


def test_matching_stage1_has_no_problem(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    fake_stage1(tmp_path)
    f = fields()
    assert reuse.reuse_problems(f) == [] and f["coff_flies"] == [12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17] and f["coff_arm"] == ["COFF"]


def test_each_mismatch_is_named(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    fake_stage1(tmp_path, frozen=False)
    assert reuse.reuse_problems(fields()) == ["coff_frozen"]
    fake_stage1(tmp_path)
    (tmp_path / "results/summary/ac_model_manifest.json").write_text("{}")
    assert reuse.reuse_problems(fields()) == ["model_status", "model_sha256"]
    fake_stage1(tmp_path)
    f = reuse.reuse_fields("0" * 64, reuse.EVAL_DIGEST, SPEC)
    assert reuse.reuse_problems(f) == ["confirm_digest"]


def test_changed_since_compares_the_hashes(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    fake_stage1(tmp_path)
    old = fields()
    blocks.append_jsonl(tmp_path / "results/m4/stage1/BRAIN/logs/situations_init.jsonl", {"fly": 99, "point": 0})
    assert reuse.changed_since(old, fields()) == ["coff_file_sha256"]


def load_script():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("wai", ROOT / "scripts/write_ad_inputs.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(mod); return mod


def test_script_smoke_and_run(tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    w = load_script()
    assert w.main(["--smoke"]) == 0
    man = json.loads((tmp_path / "results/m4-smoke/ad/inputs_manifest.json").read_text())
    assert man["status"] == "OK" and man["reuse"] is None
    fake_stage1(tmp_path)
    assert w.main([]) == 0
    man = json.loads((tmp_path / "results/summary/ad_inputs_manifest.json").read_text())
    assert man["status"] == "OK" and man["reuse"]["coff_content_hash"] and man["reuse_problems"] == []
    fake_stage1(tmp_path, model_status="STOP_NO_RECOVERY")
    assert w.main([]) == 2
    assert json.loads((tmp_path / "results/summary/ad_inputs_manifest.json").read_text())["status"] == "STOP_REUSE"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/reuse.py`:

```python
"""Spec AD.2 (가) / AD.5 6: what AD reuses from stage 1 must be stage 1's, else STOP_REUSE - the FROZEN model manifest
(its sha256 equal to the one stage 1's BRAIN result recorded), the confirmation-set digest 71a10cbc..., the
evaluation-schedule digest 03e209ea... (both recomputed and as stage 1 recorded them), and the six C-off point-0
situation records (global flies 12-17 of results/m4/stage1/BRAIN/logs/situations_init.jsonl: the file's sha256, the
records' content hash, frozen, AC.5 label). COMPARED fields are compared again at every run / resume and by the
analysis (a later change is STOP_REUSE)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from ..ac.manifest import MODEL_MANIFEST
from ..rescope.blocks import read_jsonl
from .spec import LABEL, SPEC
from .store import digest, sha256_file

CONFIRM_DIGEST = "71a10cbcabc67d551d9db9834c3436a10ce45e4172d44aba2c1b5c829bfec038"
EVAL_DIGEST = "03e209ea65d38d461ba58befe3b893a9f54d301edd58cf4d5abb6518a91a8dfa"
COMPARED = ("model_sha256", "confirm_digest", "eval_digest", "coff_file_sha256", "coff_content_hash")


def stage1_paths(spec=SPEC) -> dict:
    root = Path(spec.stage1_root) / "BRAIN"
    return dict(result=root / "result.json", init=root / "logs" / "situations_init.jsonl",
                points=root / "logs" / "situations.jsonl")


def coff_records(init_path, flies) -> list:
    flies = [int(f) for f in flies]
    return sorted((r for r in read_jsonl(init_path) if r.get("fly") in flies and r.get("point") == 0),
                  key=lambda r: r["fly"])


def stage1_fly_records(points_path, flies, point: int) -> list:
    flies = [int(f) for f in flies]
    return sorted((r for r in read_jsonl(points_path) if r.get("fly") in flies and r.get("point") == int(point)),
                  key=lambda r: r["fly"])


def reuse_fields(confirm_digest, eval_digest, spec=SPEC, model_path=MODEL_MANIFEST) -> dict:
    p = stage1_paths(spec)
    for k in ("result", "init"):
        if not p[k].exists():
            raise SystemExit(f"STOP_REUSE: {p[k]} does not exist (AD reuses stage 1's records, AD.1)")
    res = json.loads(p["result"].read_text())
    model = json.loads(Path(model_path).read_text())
    co = coff_records(p["init"], spec.coff_flies)
    return dict(model_path=str(model_path), model_status=model.get("status"), model_sha256=sha256_file(model_path),
                stage1_model_sha256=(res.get("model") or {}).get("sha256"),
                confirm_digest=confirm_digest, stage1_confirm_digest=res.get("confirm_digest"),
                eval_digest=eval_digest, stage1_eval_digest=res.get("eval_digest"),
                coff_path=str(p["init"]), coff_file_sha256=sha256_file(p["init"]), coff_content_hash=digest(co),
                coff_flies=[r["fly"] for r in co], coff_arm=sorted({str(r.get("arm")) for r in co}),
                coff_frozen=bool(co) and all(r.get("frozen") is True for r in co),
                coff_label=bool(co) and all(r.get("label") == LABEL for r in co))


def reuse_problems(f: dict, spec=SPEC) -> list:
    bad = []
    if f["model_status"] != "FROZEN":
        bad.append("model_status")
    if f["model_sha256"] != f["stage1_model_sha256"]:
        bad.append("model_sha256")
    if f["confirm_digest"] != CONFIRM_DIGEST or f["stage1_confirm_digest"] != CONFIRM_DIGEST:
        bad.append("confirm_digest")
    if f["eval_digest"] != EVAL_DIGEST or f["stage1_eval_digest"] != EVAL_DIGEST:
        bad.append("eval_digest")
    if f["coff_flies"] != list(spec.coff_flies) or f["coff_arm"] != ["COFF"]:
        bad.append("coff_records")
    if not f["coff_frozen"]:
        bad.append("coff_frozen")
    if not f["coff_label"]:
        bad.append("coff_label")
    return bad


def changed_since(old: dict, new: dict) -> list:
    return [k for k in COMPARED if (old or {}).get(k) != (new or {}).get(k)]
```

`scripts/write_ad_inputs.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Spec AD.6 3 (and the smoke / bench inputs): the AD input document (reused confirmation set and evaluation schedule,
learning schedule 306 with its prefix digests, new 20 pairs 308, layout, tags) and the AD input manifest with the
stage-1 reuse fields (가) and the AD-fixed fields (나).

    uv run python scripts/write_ad_inputs.py            # results/summary/ad_inputs.json + ad_inputs_manifest.json
    uv run python scripts/write_ad_inputs.py --smoke    # results/m4-smoke/ad/inputs.json + inputs_manifest.json
    uv run python scripts/write_ad_inputs.py --bench 1  # results/m4-bench/ad-1/...

Exit 0 (OK) or 2 (STOP_REUSE: a reused stage-1 field differs; STOP_SET). A run-mode document already on disk with other
content is never overwritten."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys

from flymon.ad import inputs, reuse, store
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC, bench, smoke


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    g = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    g.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true")
    g.add_argument("--bench", type=int, default=None, metavar="N")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    spec = smoke() if a.smoke else bench(a.bench) if a.bench is not None else SPEC
    doc_p, man_p = inputs.input_paths(spec)
    doc = inputs.inputs_doc(spec)
    if spec.mode == "run" and doc_p.exists():
        if json.loads(doc_p.read_text()) != json.loads(json.dumps(doc, sort_keys=True, default=float)):
            raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {doc_p} exists with other content (AD.2 freeze)")
    store.write_json(doc_p, doc)
    fields, problems = None, []
    if spec.mode == "run":
        fields = reuse.reuse_fields(doc["digests"]["confirm"], doc["digests"]["eval"], spec)
        problems = reuse.reuse_problems(fields, spec)
    man = inputs.input_manifest(doc, doc_p, fields, problems)
    store.write_json(man_p, man)
    print(f"{man['status']}: digests {({k: (v or '')[:12] for k, v in doc['digests'].items()})}; new set from "
          f"{doc['new']['n_rest']} left; reuse problems {problems}; wrote {doc_p}, {man_p}; {doc['label']}", flush=True)
    return 0 if man["status"] == "OK" else 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

### Task 7: 상황 쌍 평가 종류와 배틀 훅 — 본 · 짝지은 OS · 새 20쌍, 시점, 재시도 기록, 두 장부 (AD.2, AD.3, AD.7)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/hooks.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_hooks.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 `situations.evaluate(..., seed_fly, seed_key)`, Task 4 `os_encoder.os_pair_odours`, Task 5 `inputs.battle_index`, `flymon.ac.stage1.SituationLog`, `flymon.ac.invalid.InvalidBook`, `flymon.ac.diag.weight_summary`, `flymon.agent.checkpoint.filter_log`, `CheckpointStore`.
- Produces:
  - `OS_KEY: dict[(set, mode), str]`, `SEED_KEY: dict[set, str]`
  - `ADSituationLog(logs_dir)`: `has_init(fly, set_="confirm", mode="own")`, `add_init`, `add`, `add_retry(rec)`, `retries() -> list`, `filter(completed) -> int`(점 기록과 재시도 기록), `records() -> list`, `repair_init()`
  - `committed_all(out, key) -> set`(학습 ∪ 평가 체크포인트의 끝난 배틀 id; 다른 키의 체크포인트면 `SystemExit("STOP_MANIFEST: …")`)
  - `make_evaluate(pool, swarm, cfg, spec, lay, sets: dict, grid) -> evaluate(i, point, set_, mode) -> dict`
  - `ADHooks(spec, T, new_set, lay, books: dict, sitlog, evaluate)`: `kinds(i, point) -> list[(set, mode)]`, `initial() -> int`(쓴 0 시점 기록 수), `after_battle(block, sb, rec)`
  - `skipping(attempt_for, books) -> attempt_for`
  - 상황 기록 필드(`situations.evaluate`의 것에 더해): `fly`(전역), `pool_index`, `arm`, `point`, `set`, `mode`, `battle_id`, `floor_contact`, `weights`(`diag.weight_summary`), FLY-OS · 짝지은 OS면 `os`(쪽마다 pair, side, true, drawn, same_types), `label`

`tests/ad/test_ad_hooks.py`:

```python
"""AD's situation-pair hooks (AD.2, AD.3, AD.7) on the fake pool: the kinds per arm and point, records keyed by the
global fly, the resume dropping uncommitted records of every kind, the two InvalidBooks, the retry log, the frozen
check, and make_evaluate's OS odours and global seeds."""
from types import SimpleNamespace

import numpy as np
import pytest

from flymon.ac.invalid import InvalidBook
from flymon.ad import hooks, inputs, os_encoder
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL, smoke
from flymon.agent.policy import derive_seed
from flymon.rescope import blocks
from tests.ac.ac_fakes import FakePool, run_fake, stub_grid
from tests.ac.test_ac_situations import FakePool as SitPool
from tests.ac.test_ac_situations import _counts

S = smoke()
LAY = S.layout()
DOC = {"learn": blocks.schedule_rows(inputs.learn_canonical(S)), "eval": blocks.schedule_rows(inputs.eval_canonical(S))}
LEARN, EVAL = inputs.schedules_for(S, DOC, S.learn_battles)
KEY = "h" * 64


def fake_eval(i, point, set_, mode, frozen=True):
    return dict(kind="situation_eval", fly=i, point=point, rate=0.5, switched=[1, 0], frozen=frozen)


def setup(out, new_set=True, evaluate=fake_eval):
    arm_of = {i: arm for i, (arm, _, _) in enumerate(LAY)}
    books = {"L": InvalidBook(out / "invalid.json", arm_of, S.arm_sizes(), S.min_valid_of()),
             "E": InvalidBook(out / "invalid_eval.json", arm_of, S.arm_sizes(), S.min_valid_of())}
    log = hooks.ADSituationLog(out / "logs")
    return books, log, hooks.ADHooks(S, S.learn_battles, new_set, LAY, books, log, evaluate)


def go(out, books, log, hk, **kw):
    if kw.get("resume"):
        log.filter(hooks.committed_all(out, KEY))
    hk.initial()
    return run_fake(out, learn=LEARN, eval_=EVAL, n=len(LAY), pool=FakePool(len(LAY)), after_battle=hk.after_battle,
                    wrap=lambda af: hooks.skipping(af, books), **kw)


def kinds_of(log):
    return sorted((r["fly"], r["point"], r["set"], r["mode"]) for r in log.records())


def test_kinds_per_arm_and_point(tmp_path):
    _, _, hk = setup(tmp_path)
    assert hk.kinds(0, 0) == [("confirm", "own"), ("confirm", "os_paired"), ("new", "own")]
    assert hk.kinds(2, 1) == [("confirm", "own"), ("new", "own")]
    _, _, hk2 = setup(tmp_path / "b", new_set=False)
    assert hk2.kinds(0, 2) == [("confirm", "own"), ("confirm", "os_paired")]


def test_records_at_every_point_keyed_by_the_global_fly(tmp_path):
    books, log, hk = setup(tmp_path)
    run, _ = go(tmp_path, books, log, hk)
    assert run["complete"]
    recs = log.records()
    assert len(recs) == 3 * (2 * 3 + 2 * 2)                       # points 0 / 1 / 2; FLY-L 3 kinds, FLY-OS 2
    r = next(x for x in recs if x["fly"] == 26 and x["point"] == 2 and x["set"] == "new")
    assert (r["pool_index"], r["arm"], r["battle_id"], r["label"]) == (2, "FLYOS", "XL-f26-b001", LABEL)
    assert {x["fly"] for x in recs} == {24, 25, 26, 27}


def test_resume_drops_uncommitted_records_of_every_kind(tmp_path):
    full = tmp_path / "full"
    b, l, h = setup(full)
    go(full, b, l, h)
    part = tmp_path / "part"
    b, l, h = setup(part)
    run, _ = go(part, b, l, h, stop_after=4)                     # only the four b000 battles are committed
    assert not run["complete"]
    stray = "XL-f27-b001"                                         # a battle that was never committed
    for s, m in (("confirm", "own"), ("new", "own")):
        l.add(dict(fake_eval(3, 2, s, m), fly=27, set=s, mode=m, battle_id=stray, label=LABEL))
    l.add_retry(dict(fly=27, pool_index=3, battle_id=stray, block="L", attempts=2, invalid=False))
    b, l, h = setup(part)
    go(part, b, l, h, resume=True)
    assert kinds_of(l) == kinds_of(setup(full)[1]) and l.retries() == []


def test_failed_learning_battle_marks_the_learning_book_and_skips_eval(tmp_path):
    books, log, hk = setup(tmp_path)
    run, _ = go(tmp_path, books, log, hk, fail={("XL-f24-b000", n) for n in range(4)})
    assert books["L"].invalid[0] == "XL-f24-b000: unfinished after 3 retries"
    assert books["E"].invalid[0] == "learn_invalid" and 0 not in books["L"].stopped
    assert not any(r["fly"] == 24 and r["point"] > 0 for r in log.records())
    assert any(r["fly"] == 24 and r["point"] == 0 for r in log.records())       # point 0 stays readable
    rl = [r for r in log.retries() if r["battle_id"] == "XL-f24-b000"]
    assert rl == [dict(fly=24, pool_index=0, battle_id="XL-f24-b000", block="L", attempts=4, invalid=True)]


def test_failed_eval_battle_marks_only_the_eval_book(tmp_path):
    books, log, hk = setup(tmp_path)
    go(tmp_path, books, log, hk, fail={("XE-f26-b000", n) for n in range(4)})
    assert books["E"].invalid == {2: "XE-f26-b000: unfinished after 3 retries"} and books["L"].invalid == {}


def test_retried_battle_is_logged(tmp_path):
    books, log, hk = setup(tmp_path)
    go(tmp_path, books, log, hk, fail={("XL-f25-b001", 0)})
    assert [r for r in log.retries()] == [dict(fly=25, pool_index=1, battle_id="XL-f25-b001", block="L", attempts=2,
                                               invalid=False)]


def test_unfrozen_record_stops(tmp_path):
    books, log, hk = setup(tmp_path, evaluate=lambda i, p, s, m: fake_eval(i, p, s, m, frozen=p == 0))
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_SMOKE"):
        go(tmp_path, books, log, hk)


PAIRS = [dict(me="Lapras", o1="Charizard", o2="Exeggutor", cands=["Surf", "Psychic", "Thunderbolt"],
              best1="Surf", best2="Psychic")]


class Pool(SitPool):
    def __init__(self):
        super().__init__({(0, 0): _counts(0), (0, 1): _counts(1)})
        self.w = {f: np.ones(4, np.float32) for f in range(4)}
        self.w0 = {None: np.ones(4, np.float32)}
        self.flies = [SimpleNamespace(shuffle_seed=None)] * 4


def test_make_evaluate_uses_os_odours_and_global_seeds():
    pool, grid = Pool(), stub_grid()
    mask = np.array([True, True, False, False])
    sw = SimpleNamespace(a_idx=np.array([0]), p_idx=np.array([1]), kc=np.array([2, 3]),
                         _edge_masks={"PAM08": mask, "PPL105": mask})
    cfg = SimpleNamespace(z={"A": (0.0, 1.0), "P": (0.0, 1.0)}, strength=1.0, settle_ms=800.0, read_ms=600.0,
                          reward_type="PAM08", punish_type="PPL105")
    ev = hooks.make_evaluate(pool, sw, cfg, S, LAY, {"confirm": PAIRS, "new": PAIRS}, grid)
    r = ev(2, 0, "confirm", "own")                                 # FLY-OS fly 26
    want = [os_encoder.SPECIES[os_encoder.draw_index(derive_seed(307, "os-sit", 26, 0, s))] for s in (0, 1)]
    assert [d["drawn"] for d in r["os"]] == want
    assert [s for _, _, s in pool.calls[-1]] == [derive_seed(305, "sit", 26, 0, s) for s in (0, 1)]
    assert "floor_contact" in r and r["weights"]["n_changed"] == 0
    r = ev(0, 0, "confirm", "own")                                 # FLY-L fly 24: the FLY odours, no draw
    assert "os" not in r and pool.calls[-1][0][1] == [os_encoder.odour_for_species(grid, grid.move_info[m][0], "Charizard")
                                                     for m in PAIRS[0]["cands"]]
    r = ev(0, 0, "confirm", "os_paired")
    assert [d["drawn"] for d in r["os"]] == [
        os_encoder.SPECIES[os_encoder.draw_index(derive_seed(307, "os-sit-paired", 24, 0, s))] for s in (0, 1)]
    assert [s for _, _, s in pool.calls[-1]] == [derive_seed(305, "sit", 24, 0, s) for s in (0, 1)]   # paired seeds
    ev(3, 0, "new", "own")
    assert [s for _, _, s in pool.calls[-1]] == [derive_seed(305, "sit-new", 27, 0, s) for s in (0, 1)]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/hooks.py`:

```python
"""AD's situation-pair evaluations and per-battle hooks (spec AD.2, AD.3, AD.7). Records carry the global fly (`fly`),
the pool index, the arm, the point, the set ("confirm" | "new") and the mode ("own" | "os_paired"), floor contact,
the plastic-weight summary, FLY-OS's draws (`os`) and AC.5's label.
- Kinds per point: (confirm, own) every fly - FLY-L on the FLY odours, FLY-OS on OS odours keyed "os-sit";
  (confirm, os_paired) FLY-L flies on OS odours keyed "os-sit-paired" with the SAME decision seeds as (confirm, own)
  (within-fly pairing, AD.2); (new, own) every fly at the new-set points (0 / 40 / T), decision key "sit-new" (FLY-OS
  OS key "os-sit-new"), only if the run manifest includes the set.
- Point 0: initial() evaluates every fly lacking a point-0 record of a kind on w0, into logs/situations_init.jsonl, kept
  across resumes (deterministic from w0 and the seeds).
- Points 20 / 40 / ...: after_battle evaluates a fly right after its learning battle point - 1 settles and before that
  battle's checkpoint commit, into logs/situations.jsonl keyed by that battle id; a resume filters the file (and the
  retry log) to committed battles first, so an evaluation whose battle was not committed is redone.
- A battle that needed a retry is written to logs/retry_log.jsonl (global fly, battle id, attempts, invalid); a battle
  still unfinished after the retries marks the fly in the learning or the evaluation InvalidBook (AD.7: a learning
  failure drops the fly from the later points and from the evaluation block; an evaluation failure drops it from
  criteria 2-3 only). A fly skipped in the evaluation block because it is invalid in learning is "learn_invalid".
- A record whose weights changed during the evaluation stops the run: STOP_SMOKE in the smoke, STOP_INFRA otherwise."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from ..ac import diag, situations
from ..ac.stage1 import SituationLog
from ..agent.checkpoint import CheckpointStore, filter_log
from ..rescope import blocks
from . import os_encoder
from .inputs import battle_index
from .spec import LABEL

OS_KEY = {("confirm", "own"): "os-sit", ("confirm", "os_paired"): "os-sit-paired", ("new", "own"): "os-sit-new"}
SEED_KEY = {"confirm": "sit", "new": "sit-new"}


class ADSituationLog(SituationLog):
    def __init__(self, logs_dir):
        super().__init__(logs_dir)
        self.retry_path = Path(logs_dir) / "retry_log.jsonl"

    def has_init(self, fly: int, set_: str = "confirm", mode: str = "own") -> bool:
        return any(r.get("fly") == int(fly) and r.get("set") == set_ and r.get("mode") == mode
                   for r in blocks.read_jsonl(self.init_path))

    def add_retry(self, rec: dict) -> None:
        blocks.append_jsonl(self.retry_path, rec)

    def retries(self) -> list:
        return blocks.read_jsonl(self.retry_path)

    def filter(self, completed: set) -> int:
        return super().filter(completed) + filter_log(self.retry_path, set(completed))


def committed_all(out, key: str) -> set:
    done = set()
    for block in ("learn", "eval"):
        try:
            ck = CheckpointStore(Path(out) / "checkpoints" / block, key).load()
        except ValueError as e:
            raise SystemExit(f"STOP_MANIFEST: {out}/checkpoints/{block}: {e} (another run manifest, AD.4)")
        if ck:
            done |= set(ck["completed"])
    return done


def make_evaluate(pool, swarm, cfg, spec, lay, sets: dict, grid):
    cache: dict = {}

    def odours(i: int, set_: str, mode: str):
        arm, _, g = lay[i]
        os_kind = arm == "FLYOS" or mode == "os_paired"
        key = (i if os_kind else arm, set_, mode)
        if key not in cache:
            if os_kind:
                cache[key] = os_encoder.os_pair_odours(grid, sets[set_], g, spec.os_seed, OS_KEY[(set_, mode)])
            else:
                cache[key] = (situations.pair_odours(grid, sets[set_]), None)
        return cache[key]

    def evaluate(i: int, point: int, set_: str, mode: str) -> dict:
        ods, draws = odours(i, set_, mode)
        rec = situations.evaluate(pool, i, point, sets[set_], ods, cfg, swarm.a_idx, swarm.p_idx, swarm.kc,
                                  spec.tie_seed, seed_fly=lay[i][2], seed_key=SEED_KEY[set_])
        rec["floor_contact"] = situations.floor_contact(pool, i, swarm._edge_masks, (cfg.reward_type, cfg.punish_type),
                                                        spec.floor_ratio)
        rec["weights"] = diag.weight_summary(pool.w[i], pool.w0[pool.flies[i].shuffle_seed], spec.floor_ratio)
        if draws is not None:
            rec["os"] = draws
        return rec
    return evaluate


class ADHooks:
    def __init__(self, spec, T: int, new_set: bool, lay, books: dict, sitlog: ADSituationLog, evaluate):
        self.spec, self.T, self.new_set, self.lay = spec, int(T), bool(new_set), list(lay)
        self.books, self.sitlog, self.evaluate = books, sitlog, evaluate

    def kinds(self, i: int, point: int) -> list:
        out = [("confirm", "own")]
        if self.lay[i][0] == "FLYL":
            out.append(("confirm", "os_paired"))
        if self.new_set and int(point) in self.spec.new_points(self.T):
            out.append(("new", "own"))
        return out

    def _record(self, i: int, point: int, set_: str, mode: str, battle_id) -> dict:
        arm, _, g = self.lay[i]
        rec = self.evaluate(i, point, set_, mode)
        if not rec.get("frozen", False):
            code = "STOP_SMOKE" if self.spec.mode == "smoke" else "STOP_INFRA"
            raise SystemExit(f"{code}: fly {g}: weights changed during the point-{point} {set_}/{mode} situation "
                             f"evaluation (AD.7)")
        return dict(rec, fly=g, pool_index=i, arm=arm, point=int(point), set=set_, mode=mode, battle_id=battle_id,
                    label=LABEL)

    def initial(self) -> int:
        self.sitlog.repair_init()
        n = 0
        for i, (_, _, g) in enumerate(self.lay):
            if self.books["L"].skip(i):
                continue
            for set_, mode in self.kinds(i, 0):
                if not self.sitlog.has_init(g, set_, mode):
                    self.sitlog.add_init(self._record(i, 0, set_, mode, None))
                    n += 1
        return n

    def after_battle(self, block: str, sb, rec: dict) -> None:
        i = int(sb.fly_id)
        g = self.lay[i][2]
        book = self.books[block]
        skipped = book.skip(i) or (block == "E" and self.books["L"].skip(i))
        if int(rec.get("retries", 0)) > 0 and not skipped:
            self.sitlog.add_retry(dict(fly=g, pool_index=i, battle_id=sb.battle_id, block=block,
                                       attempts=int(rec["retries"]) + 1, invalid=bool(rec["invalid"])))
        if rec["invalid"]:
            if block == "E" and self.books["L"].skip(i):
                book.mark(i, "learn_invalid")
            else:
                book.mark(i, "arm_stopped" if book.skip(i) else
                          f"{sb.battle_id}: unfinished after {rec['retries']} retries")
            return
        if block != "L" or self.books["L"].skip(i):
            return
        point = battle_index(sb.battle_id) + 1
        if point in self.spec.points(self.T):
            for set_, mode in self.kinds(i, point):
                self.sitlog.add(self._record(i, point, set_, mode, sb.battle_id))


def skipping(attempt_for, books: dict):
    def wrapped(block):
        attempt = attempt_for(block)

        async def att(sb, n):
            i = int(sb.fly_id)
            if books["L"].skip(i) or (block == "E" and books["E"].skip(i)):
                return {"finished": False, "won": None, "skipped": True}
            return await attempt(sb, n)
        return att
    return wrapped
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:
  - `records(sits, *, point, arm=None, set_="confirm", mode="own") -> dict[g, rec]` — 그 시점 · 그 종류의 동결 검사를 통과한 기록(마리당 하나; 둘이면 ValueError)
  - `right_direction(rec, pairs) -> float`, `share(recs: dict, pairs) -> float | None`
  - `futility(sits, pairs, spec) -> dict(status: "CONTINUE" | "STOP_FUTILE" | "NO_FLYL", point, share_at_point, share_at_0, n_at_point, n_at_0, label)`, `persist_futility(path, fut) -> dict`, `futility_line(fut) -> str`
  - `reached(sits) -> dict[g, int]`, `latest_common_point(sits, lay, invalid: dict) -> int | None`, `final_point(spec, T, futility_status, run_status, sits, lay, invalid) -> dict(point, reason)`
  - `dropouts(invalid: dict, lay, sits) -> list[dict(fly, arm, reason, last_point)]`, `n_valid_by_point(sits, lay, points, set_="confirm", mode="own") -> dict`

`tests/ad/test_ad_validity.py`:

```python
"""Point-wise validity, the futility check at 40 and the final reading point (AD.2, AD.4, AD.7) on synthetic records."""
import json

import pytest

from flymon.ad import validity as va
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC

LAY = SPEC.layout()
PAIRS = [dict(cands=["m0", "m1"], best1="m0", best2="m1") for _ in range(4)]


def rec(g, point, pos, arm=None, set_="confirm", mode="own", frozen=True):
    sits = []
    for i, p in enumerate(pos):
        sits.append(dict(pair=i, side=0, v=[1.0, 0.0] if p else [0.0, 1.0]))
        sits.append(dict(pair=i, side=1, v=[0.0, 1.0] if p else [1.0, 0.0]))
    return dict(fly=g, point=point, arm=arm or ("FLYL" if g < 36 else "FLYOS"), set=set_, mode=mode, frozen=frozen,
                situations=sits, switched=[int(p) for p in pos])


def test_records_and_share():
    sits = [rec(24, 0, [1, 0, 0, 0]), rec(25, 0, [1, 1, 0, 0]), rec(36, 0, [1, 1, 1, 1]),
            rec(26, 0, [1, 1, 1, 1], frozen=False), rec(24, 0, [1, 1, 1, 1], mode="os_paired")]
    r = va.records(sits, point=0, arm="FLYL")
    assert sorted(r) == [24, 25] and va.share(r, PAIRS) == pytest.approx(0.375)
    assert va.share({}, PAIRS) is None
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        va.records(sits + [rec(24, 0, [0, 0, 0, 0])], point=0)


def test_futility_rule():
    base = [rec(24, 0, [1, 1, 0, 0]), rec(25, 0, [1, 0, 0, 0])]                 # share at 0 = 0.375
    same = base + [rec(24, 40, [1, 0, 0, 0]), rec(25, 40, [1, 1, 0, 0])]        # 0.375 <= 0.375
    f = va.futility(same, PAIRS, SPEC)
    assert f["status"] == "STOP_FUTILE" and f["share_at_point"] == f["share_at_0"] == pytest.approx(0.375)
    up = base + [rec(24, 40, [1, 1, 1, 0]), rec(25, 40, [1, 0, 0, 0])]
    assert va.futility(up, PAIRS, SPEC)["status"] == "CONTINUE"
    osonly = base + [rec(36, 40, [1, 1, 1, 1])]
    assert va.futility(osonly, PAIRS, SPEC)["status"] == "NO_FLYL"
    line = va.futility_line(f)
    assert line == "futility check at 40: FLY-L right-direction share 0.375 vs point 0 0.375 -> STOP_FUTILE"


def test_futility_decision_is_kept_across_resume(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    p = "results/m4/ad/futility.json"
    f = va.futility([rec(24, 0, [1, 0, 0, 0]), rec(24, 40, [1, 1, 0, 0])], PAIRS, SPEC)
    assert va.persist_futility(p, f) == f
    again = va.persist_futility(p, dict(f))
    assert again == json.loads((tmp_path / p).read_text())
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_INFRA"):
        va.persist_futility(p, dict(f, status="STOP_FUTILE"))


def test_budget_stop_reads_the_latest_common_point():
    sits = ([rec(24, p, [1, 0, 0, 0]) for p in (0, 20, 40, 60)] + [rec(25, p, [1, 0, 0, 0]) for p in (0, 20, 40, 60, 80)]
            + [rec(36, p, [0, 0, 0, 0]) for p in (0, 20, 40, 60)] + [rec(37, p, [0, 0, 0, 0]) for p in (0, 20)])
    invalid = {13: "DL-f37-b030: unfinished after 3 retries"}                   # pool index 13 = fly 37
    assert va.reached(sits) == {24: 60, 25: 80, 36: 60, 37: 20}
    lay = [x for x in LAY if x[2] in (24, 25, 36, 37)]
    assert va.latest_common_point(sits, LAY[:2] + LAY[12:14], {3: invalid[13]}) == 60
    fp = va.final_point(SPEC, 120, "CONTINUE", "STOP_BUDGET", sits, LAY[:2] + LAY[12:14], {3: invalid[13]})
    assert fp == {"point": 60, "reason": "STOP_BUDGET"}
    assert va.final_point(SPEC, 120, "STOP_FUTILE", "COMPLETE", sits, lay, {}) == {"point": 40, "reason": "STOP_FUTILE"}
    assert va.final_point(SPEC, 120, "CONTINUE", "COMPLETE", sits, lay, {}) == {"point": 120, "reason": "complete"}
    assert va.final_point(SPEC, 80, "CONTINUE", "COMPLETE", sits, lay, {}) == {"point": 80, "reason": "fallback"}


def test_dropouts_and_valid_counts():
    sits = [rec(24, p, [1, 0, 0, 0]) for p in (0, 20, 40)] + [rec(36, p, [0, 0, 0, 0]) for p in (0, 20)]
    d = va.dropouts({12: "DL-f36-b025: unfinished after 3 retries"}, LAY, sits)
    assert d == [dict(fly=36, arm="FLYOS", reason="DL-f36-b025: unfinished after 3 retries", last_point=20)]
    n = va.n_valid_by_point(sits, LAY, (0, 20, 40))
    assert n == {0: {"FLYL": 1, "FLYOS": 1}, 20: {"FLYL": 1, "FLYOS": 1}, 40: {"FLYL": 1, "FLYOS": 0}}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/validity.py`:

```python
"""Point-wise validity, the futility check and the final reading point (spec AD.2, AD.4, AD.7).
- A fly enters the reading at point t iff its point-t situation record exists and passed the frozen check: the record
  is written only after learning battles 0 .. t-1 all finished (Task 7), so an INVALID fly keeps its earlier points.
- Futility check (AD.2, the only interim analysis): once every fly has its point-40 evaluation, FLY-L's right-direction
  share (per fly the share of pairs with diag.best_contrast > 0, averaged over the valid FLY-L flies at that point) at
  40 is compared with point 0; 40 <= 0 is STOP_FUTILE (learning stops at 40, every reading at 40). It reads only these
  two numbers. The decision is written once (futility.json); a resume recomputes it and must get the same.
- Final reading point: 40 under STOP_FUTILE; under STOP_BUDGET in the run, the last point every still-valid fly reached
  (the latest common point); else T ("complete" for 120, "fallback" for 80)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from ..ac import diag
from .spec import LABEL
from .store import write_json


def records(sits, *, point: int, arm=None, set_: str = "confirm", mode: str = "own") -> dict:
    out: dict = {}
    for r in sits:
        if r.get("set") != set_ or r.get("mode") != mode or r.get("point") != int(point) or r.get("frozen") is not True:
            continue
        if arm is not None and r.get("arm") != arm:
            continue
        if r["fly"] in out:
            raise ValueError(f"two {set_}/{mode} records of fly {r['fly']} at point {point}")
        out[r["fly"]] = r
    return out


def right_direction(rec: dict, pairs) -> float:
    return float(np.mean([d > 0 for d in diag.best_contrast(rec, pairs)]))


def share(recs: dict, pairs):
    return float(np.mean([right_direction(r, pairs) for r in recs.values()])) if recs else None


def futility(sits, pairs, spec) -> dict:
    fp = spec.futility_point
    r0, rc = records(sits, point=0, arm="FLYL"), records(sits, point=fp, arm="FLYL")
    b, a = share(r0, pairs), share(rc, pairs)
    status = "NO_FLYL" if a is None or b is None else ("STOP_FUTILE" if a <= b else "CONTINUE")
    return dict(status=status, point=fp, share_at_point=a, share_at_0=b, n_at_point=len(rc), n_at_0=len(r0),
                label=LABEL)


def futility_line(fut: dict) -> str:
    f = lambda x: "none" if x is None else f"{x:.3f}"
    return (f"futility check at {fut['point']}: FLY-L right-direction share {f(fut['share_at_point'])} vs point 0 "
            f"{f(fut['share_at_0'])} -> {fut['status']}")


def persist_futility(path, fut: dict) -> dict:
    p = Path(path)
    if p.exists():
        old = json.loads(p.read_text())
        keys = ("status", "share_at_point", "share_at_0", "n_at_point", "n_at_0")
        if any(old.get(k) != fut.get(k) for k in keys):
            raise SystemExit(f"STOP_INFRA: the futility check changed across a resume ({old} -> {fut})")
        return old
    write_json(p, fut)
    return fut


def reached(sits) -> dict:
    out: dict = {}
    for r in sits:
        if r.get("set") == "confirm" and r.get("mode") == "own" and r.get("frozen") is True:
            out[r["fly"]] = max(out.get(r["fly"], -1), int(r["point"]))
    return out


def latest_common_point(sits, lay, invalid: dict):
    rp = reached(sits)
    alive = [g for i, (_, _, g) in enumerate(lay) if int(i) not in {int(k) for k in invalid}]
    pts = [rp.get(g) for g in alive]
    if not pts or any(p is None for p in pts):
        return None
    return min(pts)


def final_point(spec, T: int, futility_status, run_status, sits, lay, invalid: dict) -> dict:
    if futility_status == "STOP_FUTILE":
        return dict(point=int(spec.futility_point), reason="STOP_FUTILE")
    if run_status == "STOP_BUDGET":
        return dict(point=latest_common_point(sits, lay, invalid), reason="STOP_BUDGET")
    return dict(point=int(T), reason="fallback" if int(T) == spec.fallback_battles else "complete")


def dropouts(invalid: dict, lay, sits) -> list:
    rp = reached(sits)
    out = []
    for i, reason in sorted((int(k), v) for k, v in invalid.items()):
        arm, _, g = lay[i]
        out.append(dict(fly=g, arm=arm, reason=reason, last_point=rp.get(g)))
    return out


def n_valid_by_point(sits, lay, points, set_: str = "confirm", mode: str = "own") -> dict:
    arms = list(dict.fromkeys(a for a, _, _ in lay))
    out = {}
    for p in points:
        recs = records(sits, point=p, set_=set_, mode=mode)
        out[int(p)] = {a: sum(1 for r in recs.values() if r.get("arm") == a) for a in arms}
    return out
```

(시험의 `latest_common_point` 호출에서 `lay`는 풀 순번이 0–3인 네 마리 목록이다 — `LAY[:2] + LAY[12:14]` = 24, 25, 36, 37 — 그리고 `invalid`는 그 목록의 순번 3(= 37)을 가리킨다.)

### Task 9: 배틀별 시작 검사와 두 단계 학습 실행 골격 `run_ad_arm` (AD.2 무익 점검, AD.4 예약 · STOP_MANIFEST)

**Files:**
- Modify: `flymon/agent/runner.py:29-56` (`run_cohort`에 `should_stop_battle=None`)
- Modify: `flymon/rescope/blocks.py:282-318` (`run_block`에 `should_stop_battle=None`, `run_cohort`로 전달)
- Create: `flymon/ad/runner.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_runner.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `flymon.rescope.blocks`(`run_block`, `ids_of`, `enter_eval`, `weights_sha`), `CheckpointStore`, Task 5 `inputs.battle_index`; `tests/ac/ac_fakes.py`의 `FakePool` · `fake_attempt_for` · `snapshot`.
- Produces:
  - `run_cohort(..., should_stop_battle=None)` — 배틀마다 시작 직전(잠금 안, `should_stop` 다음, `stop_after` 예약 전) `should_stop_battle(sb)`가 참이면 그 마리 루프를 끝낸다.
  - `run_block(..., should_stop_battle=None)`.
  - `flymon.ad.runner.run_ad_arm(out, *, learn, eval_, attempt_for, cfg_hash, retry_max, resume, pool, swarm, reset_player=None, stop_after=None, on_block_end=None, after_battle=None, should_stop=None, should_stop_battle=None, futility_point=None, decide_futility=None) -> dict(complete, played{L, E}, before, after, futility, learn_ids)` — `should_stop_battle(block, sb)`, `decide_futility() -> dict(status, ...)`. 체크포인트 키 불일치는 `SystemExit("STOP_MANIFEST: …")`. `eval_`가 비면(벤치) 학습이 끝났을 때 `complete=True`.

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_runner.py`:

```python
"""run_ad_arm (AD.2, AD.4) on the fake pool: learning in two phases split at the futility point, one decision per
session, STOP_FUTILE going straight to the evaluation block, resume equivalence across the split, the per-battle start
check, a changed checkpoint key refused as STOP_MANIFEST, and the bench's eval-less completion."""
import asyncio
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from flymon.ad import inputs
from flymon.ad.runner import run_ad_arm
from flymon.ad.spec import smoke
from flymon.rescope import blocks
from tests.ac.ac_fakes import FakePool, fake_attempt_for, snapshot

S = smoke()
DOC = {"learn": blocks.schedule_rows(inputs.learn_canonical(S)), "eval": blocks.schedule_rows(inputs.eval_canonical(S))}
LEARN, EVAL = inputs.schedules_for(S, DOC, S.learn_battles)
N = S.n_flies()


def go(out, *, status="CONTINUE", calls=None, key="h" * 64, resume=False, stop_after=None, gate=None, eval_=EVAL,
       pool=None):
    calls = calls if calls is not None else []

    def decide():
        calls.append(1)
        return {"status": status}
    pool = pool or FakePool(N)
    run = asyncio.run(run_ad_arm(out, learn=LEARN, eval_=eval_, attempt_for=fake_attempt_for(out, pool), cfg_hash=key,
                                 retry_max=3, resume=resume, pool=pool, swarm=SimpleNamespace(mode="learn"),
                                 reset_player=lambda b, f: None, stop_after=stop_after, should_stop_battle=gate,
                                 futility_point=S.futility_point, decide_futility=decide))
    return run, calls, pool


def learned(out):
    return sorted(r["battle_id"] for r in blocks.read_jsonl(out / "logs" / "battles.jsonl"))


def test_continue_plays_every_learning_battle_then_eval(tmp_path):
    run, calls, _ = go(tmp_path)
    assert run["complete"] and calls == [1] and run["futility"] == {"status": "CONTINUE"}
    assert learned(tmp_path) == sorted(sb.battle_id for sb in LEARN) and len(run["played"]["E"]) == len(EVAL)


def test_stop_futile_skips_the_rest_and_runs_eval(tmp_path):
    run, calls, _ = go(tmp_path, status="STOP_FUTILE")
    assert run["complete"] and calls == [1]
    assert learned(tmp_path) == sorted(sb.battle_id for sb in LEARN if inputs.battle_index(sb.battle_id) < 1)
    assert run["learn_ids"] == [sb.battle_id for sb in LEARN if sb.battle_id.endswith("-b000")]
    assert len(run["played"]["E"]) == len(EVAL)


def test_resume_across_the_split_equals_one_session(tmp_path):
    full = tmp_path / "full"
    go(full)
    part = tmp_path / "part"
    calls = []
    run, _, pool = go(part, stop_after=6, calls=calls)              # phase 1 (4) + 2 of phase 2
    assert not run["complete"] and calls == [1]
    run, _, _ = go(part, resume=True, calls=calls, pool=pool)
    assert run["complete"] and calls == [1, 1]
    assert snapshot(full) == snapshot(part)


def test_per_battle_gate_stops_at_a_batch_boundary(tmp_path):
    seen = []

    def gate(block, sb):
        seen.append((block, inputs.battle_index(sb.battle_id)))
        return block == "L" and inputs.battle_index(sb.battle_id) >= 1
    run, calls, _ = go(tmp_path, gate=gate)
    assert not run["complete"] and calls == [1]
    assert learned(tmp_path) == sorted(sb.battle_id for sb in LEARN if sb.battle_id.endswith("-b000"))
    assert ("L", 1) in seen and run["played"]["E"] == []


def test_changed_checkpoint_key_is_stop_manifest(tmp_path):
    go(tmp_path, stop_after=3)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_MANIFEST"):
        go(tmp_path, resume=True, key="g" * 64)


def test_refuses_existing_checkpoints_without_resume(tmp_path):
    go(tmp_path, stop_after=3)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="--resume"):
        go(tmp_path)


def test_bench_without_eval_block_completes(tmp_path):
    run, _, _ = go(tmp_path, eval_=[])
    assert run["complete"] and run["played"]["E"] == [] and run["after"] == run["before"]
```

`flymon/ad/runner.py`:

```python
"""run_ad_arm: rescope's run_arm for AD (spec AD.2, AD.4). The learning block runs in two phases split at the futility
point: phase 1 = learning battles with index < 40 (every fly), then decide_futility() once per session (it persists
the decision, Task 8), then phase 2 = the rest - or nothing under STOP_FUTILE - and the evaluation block after the
effective learning end. Phase boundaries are a barrier: a fly ahead waits for the slowest before battle 40, so the
check reads every fly at 40 and no fly learns past 40 before the decision. should_stop_battle(block, sb) is asked at
every battle start (AD.4's per-batch reservation). A checkpoint written under another key (the run manifest's sha256 is
part of it) is STOP_MANIFEST. Without an evaluation schedule (the bench) the run is complete when learning is."""
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from ..agent.checkpoint import CheckpointStore
from ..rescope import blocks
from .inputs import battle_index


def _complete(ck, sched) -> bool:
    return ck is not None and set(blocks.ids_of(sched)) <= set(ck["completed"])


def _load(store):
    try:
        return store.load()
    except ValueError as e:
        raise SystemExit(f"STOP_MANIFEST: {store.root}: {e} (the checkpoint key holds the run manifest's sha256, AD.4)")


# copied from flymon/rescope/blocks.py:run_arm (+ two learning phases split at the futility point, the per-battle start
# check, STOP_MANIFEST on a foreign checkpoint key, an eval-less bench; no yoked queues)
async def run_ad_arm(out, *, learn, eval_, attempt_for, cfg_hash: str, retry_max: int, resume: bool, pool, swarm,
                     reset_player=None, stop_after=None, on_block_end=None, after_battle=None, should_stop=None,
                     should_stop_battle=None, futility_point=None, decide_futility=None) -> dict:
    out = Path(out)
    stores = {"L": CheckpointStore(out / "checkpoints" / "learn", cfg_hash),
              "E": CheckpointStore(out / "checkpoints" / "eval", cfg_hash)}
    cks = {b: _load(s) for b, s in stores.items()}
    if not resume and any(cks.values()):
        raise SystemExit(f"{out} already has checkpoints; pass --resume")
    budget = {"left": stop_after}
    played: dict = {"L": [], "E": []}
    state = {"fut": None, "learn_ids": None}

    def snapshot(sb):
        return dict(w=np.array(pool.w[sb.fly_id], copy=True))

    def rollback_for(block):
        def rollback(sb, snap, i, res):
            if reset_player:
                reset_player(block, sb.fly_id)              # AD: retires the attempt token before the restore
            pool.w[sb.fly_id] = snap["w"].copy()
        return rollback

    def after_for(block):
        def after(sb, rec):
            if after_battle is not None:
                after_battle(block, sb, rec)
        return after

    async def block_run(block, sched, logs_dir):
        gate = None if should_stop_battle is None else (lambda sb: should_stop_battle(block, sb))
        r = await blocks.run_block(sched, stores[block], logs_dir, attempt_for(block), pool.state, retry_max,
                                   block=block, snapshot=snapshot, rollback=rollback_for(block),
                                   after=after_for(block), stop_after=budget["left"], should_stop=should_stop,
                                   should_stop_battle=gate)
        played[block] += r["played"]
        if on_block_end is not None:
            await on_block_end(block)
        if budget["left"] is not None:
            budget["left"] -= len(r["played"])
        return _load(stores[block])

    def partial(before=None) -> dict:
        return dict(complete=False, played=played, before=before or {}, after={}, futility=state["fut"],
                    learn_ids=state["learn_ids"])

    ck = cks["L"]
    if ck:
        pool.load_state(ck["pool_state"])
    phase1 = learn if futility_point is None else [sb for sb in learn
                                                   if battle_index(sb.battle_id) < int(futility_point)]
    eff = phase1
    if not _complete(ck, phase1):
        if cks["E"]:
            raise SystemExit(f"{out}: an eval checkpoint exists but the learning block is incomplete")
        ck = await block_run("L", phase1, out / "logs")
        if not _complete(ck, phase1):
            return partial()
    if len(phase1) < len(learn):
        state["fut"] = decide_futility() if decide_futility is not None else {"status": "CONTINUE"}
        if state["fut"]["status"] != "STOP_FUTILE":
            eff = learn
            if not _complete(ck, eff):
                if cks["E"]:
                    raise SystemExit(f"{out}: an eval checkpoint exists but the learning block is incomplete")
                ck = await block_run("L", eff, out / "logs")
                if not _complete(ck, eff):
                    return partial()
    state["learn_ids"] = [sb.battle_id for sb in eff]
    pool.load_state(ck["pool_state"])                    # the committed end of learning (== the live pool)
    before = {f: blocks.weights_sha(e["w"]) for f, e in enumerate(ck["pool_state"]["flies"])}
    if not eval_:
        return dict(complete=True, played=played, before=before, after=dict(before), futility=state["fut"],
                    learn_ids=state["learn_ids"])
    if cks["E"]:
        pool.load_state(cks["E"]["pool_state"])
    blocks.enter_eval(swarm, pool)
    if not _complete(cks["E"], eval_) and ((should_stop is not None and should_stop())
                                          or (budget["left"] is not None and budget["left"] <= 0)):
        return partial(before)
    eck = await block_run("E", eval_, out / "logs" / "eval")
    after = {f: blocks.weights_sha(pool.w[f]) for f in range(pool.n_flies)}
    return dict(complete=_complete(eck, eval_), played=played, before=before, after=after, futility=state["fut"],
                learn_ids=state["learn_ids"])
```

(`FakePool`에는 `w.gen`이 없지만 `run_ad_arm`은 세대를 읽지 않는다 — 세대는 `ADSwarm`과 `LeverFlyPool`의 몫이다.)

### Task 10: 예산 — 세션 청구, 벤치 중앙값, 경우별 외삽과 T 결정, 배치 예약 (AD.4, AD.8 11)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/budget.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_budget.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `ADSpec`(`budget_limit_h`, `sit_evals`, `sit_evals_at`, `batches`, `learn_battles`, `fallback_battles`, `rescope_s_per_batch_battle`, `margin`, `bench_valid`, `bench_max_attempts`, `worst_factor`, `session_cap_h`), Task 5 `inputs.battle_index`, Task 1 `store.write_json`.
- Produces:
  - `BUDGET = "results/summary/ad_budget.json"`
  - `close_stale(out, now: float) -> int` — "running"으로 남은 세션을 "killed"로 닫고 다음 세션 시작(없으면 `now`)까지 청구
  - `spent_s(out) -> float`, `remaining_s(spec, spent) -> float`, `session_cap_s(spec, spent) -> float`
  - `hours(spec, s_batch, s_eval, T, new_set) -> float`, `choose(spec, s_batch, s_eval) -> dict(status, T, new_set, case, hours, cases, limit_h, ...)`
  - `bench_summary(attempts: list[dict], spec) -> dict(status: "OK" | "MORE" | "STOP_BENCH", used, s_per_batch_battle, sit_eval_s, values)`
  - `BatchGate(spec, T, new_set, *, session_end, budget_end, bench_batch_s, bench_eval_s, times_path, now=time.time)`: `__call__(block, sb) -> bool`(참 = 시작하지 않음), `finished(block, sb)`, `worst(block, index) -> float`, `refused: None | "STOP_BUDGET" | "session_cap"`, `refusal: dict | None`, `longest: float`

`tests/ad/test_ad_budget.py`:

```python
"""AD's budget (AD.4): sessions charged to the next start, the remaining budget and the session cap, the case-wise
extrapolation and T, the 3-valid-of-6 bench median, and the per-batch worst-case reservation."""
import datetime as dt
import json
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from flymon.ad import budget, store
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC


def test_reference_values_choose_120_with_the_new_set():
    c = budget.choose(SPEC, 838.9, 27.2)
    assert {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in c["cases"].items()} == {"T120_new": 45.59, "T120": 44.89, "T80_new": 32.77,
                                                                "T80": 32.06}
    assert (c["status"], c["T"], c["new_set"], c["case"]) == ("OK", 120, True, "T120_new")
    assert round(c["limit_h"], 3) == 47.418


@pytest.mark.parametrize("s_batch,s_eval,want", [
    (730.0, 100.0, ("OK", 120, False, "T120")),
    (900.0, 27.2, ("OK", 80, True, "T80_new")),
    (1300.0, 27.2, ("STOP_BUDGET", None, None, None))])
def test_choose_follows_the_pre_registered_order(s_batch, s_eval, want):
    c = budget.choose(SPEC, s_batch, s_eval)
    assert (c["status"], c["T"], c["new_set"], c["case"]) == want


def test_rescope_floor():
    assert budget.hours(SPEC, 300.0, 0.0, 120, False) == pytest.approx(431.1 * 140 * 1.3 / 3600)


def test_close_stale_charges_to_the_next_start(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    out = tmp_path / "results/m4/ad"
    iso = lambda t: dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(t, dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()
    store.write_json(out / "wall_clock.json", {"sessions": [
        dict(started_utc=iso(1000), ended_utc=None, seconds=None, status="running"),
        dict(started_utc=iso(5000), ended_utc=iso(5100), seconds=100.0, status="ok"),
        dict(started_utc=iso(9000), ended_utc=None, seconds=None, status="running")]})
    assert budget.close_stale(out, now=12000.0) == 2
    s = json.loads((out / "wall_clock.json").read_text())["sessions"]
    assert [x["seconds"] for x in s] == [4000.0, 100.0, 3000.0] and s[0]["status"] == "killed"
    assert s[0]["charged"] == "to_next_start" and s[2]["charged"] == "to_detection"
    assert budget.spent_s(out) == 7100.0 and budget.close_stale(out, now=13000.0) == 0


def test_remaining_and_session_cap():
    assert budget.remaining_s(SPEC, 10 * 3600) == pytest.approx((47.41797 - 10) * 3600, abs=1)
    assert budget.session_cap_s(SPEC, 10 * 3600) == 24 * 3600
    assert budget.session_cap_s(SPEC, 30 * 3600) == pytest.approx(budget.remaining_s(SPEC, 30 * 3600))


def att(n, valid, b, e):
    return dict(attempt=n, valid=valid, s_per_batch_battle=b, sit_eval_s_per_eval=e)


def test_bench_summary():
    a = [att(1, True, 800.0, 27.0), att(2, False, 2900.0, 90.0), att(3, True, 850.0, 28.0), att(4, True, 820.0, 26.0)]
    s = budget.bench_summary(a, SPEC)
    assert s["status"] == "OK" and s["used"] == [1, 3, 4] and s["s_per_batch_battle"] == 820.0
    assert s["sit_eval_s"] == 27.0 and s["values"]["s_per_batch_battle"] == [800.0, 850.0, 820.0]
    assert budget.bench_summary(a[:2], SPEC)["status"] == "MORE"
    six = [att(i, i < 3, 800.0, 27.0) for i in range(1, 7)]
    assert budget.bench_summary(six, SPEC)["status"] == "STOP_BENCH"


class Clock:
    def __init__(self):
        self.t = 0.0

    def __call__(self):
        return self.t


def sb(bid):
    return SimpleNamespace(battle_id=bid)


def gate(tmp_path, clock, *, session_end=1e9, budget_end=1e9, new_set=True):
    return budget.BatchGate(SPEC, 120, new_set, session_end=session_end, budget_end=budget_end, bench_batch_s=100.0,
                            bench_eval_s=10.0, times_path=tmp_path / "results/m4/ad/batch_times.json", now=clock)


def test_gate_refuses_a_batch_the_budget_cannot_cover(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    clock = Clock()
    g = gate(tmp_path, clock, budget_end=1000.0)
    assert g.worst("L", 0) == 200.0
    assert g("L", sb("DL-f24-b000")) is False and g("L", sb("DL-f25-b000")) is False
    clock.t = 850.0
    assert g("L", sb("DL-f24-b001")) is True and g.refused == "STOP_BUDGET"
    assert g("L", sb("DL-f26-b000")) is False                      # batch 0 was reserved: the lagging fly plays it
    assert g("L", sb("DL-f25-b001")) is True
    assert g.refusal == dict(block="L", index=1, worst_s=200.0, left_s=150.0, reason="STOP_BUDGET")


def test_gate_session_cap_is_not_a_budget_stop(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    clock = Clock()
    g = gate(tmp_path, clock, session_end=150.0)
    assert g("L", sb("DL-f24-b000")) is True and g.refused == "session_cap"


def test_gate_uses_the_longest_batch_and_point_evaluations(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    clock = Clock()
    g = gate(tmp_path, clock)
    g("L", sb("DL-f24-b000")); clock.t = 30.0; g("L", sb("DL-f25-b000"))
    clock.t = 400.0; g.finished("L", sb("DL-f24-b000"))
    clock.t = 450.0; g.finished("L", sb("DL-f25-b000"))
    assert g.longest == 450.0
    assert g.worst("L", 5) == 900.0                                 # no point after battle 5
    assert g.worst("L", 19) == 900.0 + 10.0 * 36                    # point 20: 24 own + 12 paired
    assert g.worst("L", 39) == 900.0 + 10.0 * 60                    # point 40: + 24 new-set
    assert g.worst("E", 3) == 900.0
    again = gate(tmp_path, Clock())
    assert again.longest == 450.0                                   # persisted across sessions
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/budget.py`:

```python
"""AD's budget (spec AD.4, AD.8 11). Total 60 h; stage 1 spent 41 695.3 s; AD's limit is 60 h - 11.582 h - 1 h reserve =
47.418 h. Bench and smoke time are not charged.
- Sessions live in <out>/wall_clock.json (AC's open_session / close_session format). A session hard-killed before its
  own close is left "running"; close_stale() (called at the next session's start) closes it as "killed" and charges it
  from its start to the next recorded session's start, or to `now` (the session opening now = detection) - AD's rule,
  not stage 1's last-mtime rule.
- Session cap = min(24 h, the limit minus every AD session so far); a resume reserves nothing again.
- Extrapolation: hours = (max(bench median s/batch-battle, 431.1) x (T + 20) + bench median s/evaluation x the
  situation evaluations of (T, new set)) x 1.3 / 3600. T = 120 with the new set if it fits, else 120 without it, else 80
  (with the set, else without), else STOP_BUDGET. Futility shortening is not counted.
- Bench median: the first 3 valid attempts (validity: Task 11), each of s/batch-battle and s/evaluation; fewer than 3
  valid after 6 attempts is STOP_BENCH.
- BatchGate (AD.4 '상한 근처의 배치'): a batch-battle (block, schedule index k) starts only if both the session and the
  total budget left cover its worst case = worst_factor x the longest batch-battle so far in the run (first: x the bench
  median; persisted in batch_times.json) + the bench median s/evaluation x the situation evaluations at point k + 1 (a
  learning batch ending on a point). Once a batch is open its other flies' battles are not checked again. A refusal by
  the total budget is STOP_BUDGET (the run stops; the reading uses the latest common point); by the session cap only, the
  session closes at a batch boundary for --resume. A batch-battle's span runs from its first start to its last finish."""
from __future__ import annotations

import datetime as dt
import json
import time
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from .inputs import battle_index
from .spec import LABEL
from .store import write_json

BUDGET = "results/summary/ad_budget.json"


def _ts(iso: str) -> float:
    return dt.datetime.fromisoformat(iso).timestamp()


def close_stale(out, now: float) -> int:
    p = Path(out) / "wall_clock.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return 0
    d = json.loads(p.read_text())
    starts = [_ts(s["started_utc"]) for s in d["sessions"]]
    n = 0
    for i, s in enumerate(d["sessions"]):
        if s.get("status") != "running":
            continue
        nxt = i + 1 < len(starts)
        end = starts[i + 1] if nxt else float(now)
        sec = max(0.0, end - starts[i])
        s.update(status="killed", seconds=round(sec, 2), charged="to_next_start" if nxt else "to_detection",
                 ended_utc=dt.datetime.fromtimestamp(starts[i] + sec, dt.timezone.utc).isoformat())
        n += 1
    if n:
        d["label"] = LABEL
        write_json(p, d)
    return n


def spent_s(out) -> float:
    p = Path(out) / "wall_clock.json"
    if not p.exists():
        return 0.0
    return float(sum(float(s["seconds"]) for s in json.loads(p.read_text())["sessions"] if s.get("seconds") is not None))


def remaining_s(spec, spent: float) -> float:
    return spec.budget_limit_h() * 3600.0 - float(spent)


def session_cap_s(spec, spent: float) -> float:
    return min(spec.session_cap_h * 3600.0, remaining_s(spec, spent))


def hours(spec, s_batch: float, s_eval: float, T: int, new_set: bool) -> float:
    used = max(float(s_batch), spec.rescope_s_per_batch_battle)
    return (used * spec.batches(T) + float(s_eval) * spec.sit_evals(T, new_set)) * spec.margin / 3600.0


def choose(spec, s_batch: float, s_eval: float) -> dict:
    T, F = spec.learn_battles, spec.fallback_battles
    order = ((f"T{T}_new", T, True), (f"T{T}", T, False), (f"T{F}_new", F, True), (f"T{F}", F, False))
    cases = {name: hours(spec, s_batch, s_eval, t, new) for name, t, new in order}
    limit = spec.budget_limit_h()
    base = dict(cases=cases, limit_h=limit, s_per_batch_battle_used=max(float(s_batch), spec.rescope_s_per_batch_battle),
                s_per_batch_battle_bench=float(s_batch), sit_eval_s=float(s_eval),
                rescope_s_per_batch_battle=spec.rescope_s_per_batch_battle, margin=spec.margin,
                sit_evals={name: spec.sit_evals(t, new) for name, t, new in order},
                batches={name: spec.batches(t) for name, t, _ in order})
    for name, t, new in order:
        if cases[name] <= limit:
            return dict(base, status="OK", T=t, new_set=new, case=name, hours=cases[name])
    return dict(base, status="STOP_BUDGET", T=None, new_set=None, case=None, hours=None)


def bench_summary(attempts: list, spec) -> dict:
    valid = [a for a in attempts if a["valid"]][: spec.bench_valid]
    vals = dict(s_per_batch_battle=[float(a["s_per_batch_battle"]) for a in valid],
                sit_eval_s=[float(a["sit_eval_s_per_eval"]) for a in valid])
    if len(valid) == spec.bench_valid:
        return dict(status="OK", used=[a["attempt"] for a in valid], values=vals,
                    s_per_batch_battle=float(np.median(vals["s_per_batch_battle"])),
                    sit_eval_s=float(np.median(vals["sit_eval_s"])), n_attempts=len(attempts))
    status = "STOP_BENCH" if len(attempts) >= spec.bench_max_attempts else "MORE"
    return dict(status=status, used=[a["attempt"] for a in valid], values=vals, s_per_batch_battle=None,
                sit_eval_s=None, n_attempts=len(attempts))


class BatchGate:
    def __init__(self, spec, T: int, new_set: bool, *, session_end: float, budget_end: float, bench_batch_s: float,
                 bench_eval_s: float, times_path, now=time.time):
        self.spec, self.T, self.new_set = spec, int(T), bool(new_set)
        self.session_end, self.budget_end = float(session_end), float(budget_end)
        self.bench_batch_s, self.bench_eval_s = float(bench_batch_s), float(bench_eval_s)
        self.times_path, self.now = Path(times_path), now
        d = json.loads(self.times_path.read_text()) if self.times_path.exists() else {}
        self.longest = float(d.get("longest_s", 0.0))
        self.opened: set = set()
        self.spans: dict = {}
        self.refused, self.refusal = None, None

    def worst(self, block: str, index: int) -> float:
        base = self.spec.worst_factor * (self.longest if self.longest > 0 else self.bench_batch_s)
        n = self.spec.sit_evals_at(int(index) + 1, self.T, self.new_set) if block == "L" else 0
        return base + self.bench_eval_s * n

    def __call__(self, block: str, sb) -> bool:
        key = (block, battle_index(sb.battle_id))
        if key in self.opened:
            return False
        if self.refused is not None:
            return True
        t, w = self.now(), self.worst(*key)
        for reason, end in (("STOP_BUDGET", self.budget_end), ("session_cap", self.session_end)):
            if end - t < w:
                self.refused = reason
                self.refusal = dict(block=block, index=key[1], worst_s=w, left_s=end - t, reason=reason)
                return True
        self.opened.add(key)
        self.spans[key] = [t, None]
        return False

    def finished(self, block: str, sb) -> None:
        key = (block, battle_index(sb.battle_id))
        span = self.spans.get(key)
        if span is None:
            return
        t = self.now()
        span[1] = t if span[1] is None else max(span[1], t)
        dur = span[1] - span[0]
        if dur > self.longest:
            self.longest = dur
            write_json(self.times_path, dict(longest_s=dur, block=block, index=key[1], label=LABEL))
```

```python
"""Load logging (AD.4): uptime / ps parsing, the ancestry of busy processes, a bench invalidated by an outside process
above 50% CPU for 3 consecutive samples, the quiet start condition, the run summary, and write_ad_budget's statuses."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import load, store

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
MAC = "10:00  up 1 day, 10:13, 32 users, load averages: 4.01 5.33 5.80"
PS = """    1     0   0.0 /sbin/launchd
   10     1  10.0 python scripts/run_ad.py --bench 1
   11    10 180.0 python -c spawn_main
   12    10  60.0 node pokemon-showdown
   99     1  95.0 /usr/bin/other-job
"""


def s(t, busy):
    return dict(kind="sample", t=float(t), load=[1.0 + t, 1.0, 1.0], uptime="", busy=busy)


def p(pid, anc=(1,), cpu=90.0):
    return dict(pid=pid, ppid=anc[0] if anc else 0, cpu=cpu, cmd=f"p{pid}", ancestors=list(anc))


def test_parse_uptime_and_ps():
    assert load.parse_uptime(MAC) == [4.01, 5.33, 5.80]
    assert load.parse_uptime(" 10:00:00 up 3 days,  load average: 0.10, 0.20, 0.30") == [0.10, 0.20, 0.30]
    rows = load.parse_ps(PS)
    assert rows[2] == dict(pid=11, ppid=10, cpu=180.0, cmd="python -c spawn_main")
    assert load.ancestors(rows, 11) == [10, 1]


def test_sample_lists_busy_processes_with_ancestry():
    x = load.sample(MAC, PS, 100.0, 50.0)
    assert x["load"] == [4.01, 5.33, 5.80] and [b["pid"] for b in x["busy"]] == [11, 12, 99]
    assert x["busy"][0]["ancestors"] == [10, 1]


def test_outside_process_three_consecutive_samples_invalidates():
    run = [s(0, []), s(60, [p(99)]), s(120, [p(99)]), s(180, [p(99)]), s(240, [])]
    v = load.bench_validity(run, 0, 300, own_pid=10, busy_samples=3)
    assert not v["valid"] and v["offenders"][0]["pid"] == 99 and v["offenders"][0]["n_consecutive"] == 3
    gap = [s(0, []), s(60, [p(99)]), s(120, [p(99)]), s(180, []), s(240, [p(99)])]
    assert load.bench_validity(gap, 0, 300, own_pid=10, busy_samples=3)["valid"]


def test_the_bench_own_processes_never_count():
    run = [s(t, [p(11, (10, 1), 180.0), p(12, (10, 1))]) for t in (0, 60, 120, 180)]
    assert load.bench_validity(run, 0, 300, own_pid=10, busy_samples=3)["valid"]


def test_only_samples_inside_the_window_count_and_none_is_invalid():
    run = [s(t, [p(99)]) for t in (0, 60, 120)] + [s(400, [])]
    assert load.bench_validity(run, 300, 500, own_pid=10, busy_samples=3)["valid"]
    v = load.bench_validity(run, 1000, 2000, own_pid=10, busy_samples=3)
    assert not v["valid"] and v["reason"] == "no load samples in the bench window"


def test_quiet_needs_three_clean_samples():
    assert not load.quiet([s(0, []), s(60, [])], set(), 3)
    assert load.quiet([s(0, [p(99)]), s(60, []), s(120, []), s(180, [])], set(), 3)
    assert not load.quiet([s(0, []), s(60, [p(99)]), s(120, [])], set(), 3)
    assert load.quiet([s(0, [p(5)]), s(60, [p(5)]), s(120, [p(5)])], {5}, 3)


def test_summary():
    run = [s(0, []), s(60, [p(99)]), s(120, [p(99)]), s(180, [p(99)])]
    x = load.summary(run, 0, 200, own_pid=10, busy_samples=3)
    assert x["load1_median"] == 91.0 and x["load1_max"] == 181.0       # load1 = 1 + t in these samples
    assert x["n_samples"] == 4 and [o["pid"] for o in x["offenders"]] == [99]


def load_script():
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("wab", ROOT / "scripts/write_ad_budget.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(mod); return mod


def bench_dir(tmp_path, n, start, b, e):
    store.write_json(tmp_path / f"results/m4-bench/ad-{n}/bench.json",
                     dict(s_per_batch_battle=b, sit_eval_s_per_eval=e, pid=10, t_started=start, t_ended=start + 500))


def test_write_ad_budget_statuses(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    log = tmp_path / "load.log"
    lines = [s(t, [p(99)] if 1000 <= t < 1500 else []) for t in range(0, 4000, 60)]
    log.write_text("".join(json.dumps(x) + "\n" for x in lines))
    w = load_script()
    bench_dir(tmp_path, 1, 0, 830.0, 27.0)
    bench_dir(tmp_path, 2, 1000, 2900.0, 90.0)                     # an outside process: invalid
    assert w.main(["--load-log", str(log)]) == 3                   # one valid so far
    bench_dir(tmp_path, 3, 2000, 850.0, 28.0)
    bench_dir(tmp_path, 4, 3000, 840.0, 26.0)
    assert w.main(["--load-log", str(log)]) == 0
    d = json.loads((tmp_path / "results/summary/ad_budget.json").read_text())
    assert d["status"] == "OK" and d["T"] == 120 and d["new_set"] is True and d["bench"]["used"] == [1, 3, 4]
    assert d["bench"]["s_per_batch_battle"] == 840.0 and [a["valid"] for a in d["attempts"]] == [True, False, True, True]
    assert d["label"].startswith("M2 정식 판정 없음") and d["load_log_sha256"]
```

`flymon/ad/load.py`:

```python
"""Load logging around AD's re-bench and run (spec AD.4). Every minute a sample: uptime's three load averages and every
process above busy_cpu % CPU (ps -A -o pid=,ppid=,pcpu=,command=) with its ancestor pid chain, so a bench's own
processes (the bench python, its spawn workers, its Showdown node) are recognised by ancestry. A bench is invalid if a
process outside it is busy in busy_samples (3) consecutive samples inside the bench window - with one sample a minute
this calls a 2-to-4-minute burst invalid, the conservative side of AD.4's "3 minutes". A bench window with no sample
is invalid (the logger was not running). The start condition is the last 3 samples free of busy processes (any
process, not only python, AD.4). During the run the samples are recorded; the result carries load1 median / max and
the outside offenders (not a STOP)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

UPTIME = re.compile(r"load averages?:\s*([\d.]+),?\s+([\d.]+),?\s+([\d.]+)")


def parse_uptime(text: str):
    m = UPTIME.search(text or "")
    return [float(m.group(i)) for i in (1, 2, 3)] if m else None


def parse_ps(text: str) -> list:
    rows = []
    for line in (text or "").splitlines():
        parts = line.split(None, 3)
        if len(parts) < 4:
            continue
        try:
            rows.append(dict(pid=int(parts[0]), ppid=int(parts[1]), cpu=float(parts[2]), cmd=parts[3].strip()))
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return rows


def ancestors(rows, pid: int) -> list:
    parent = {r["pid"]: r["ppid"] for r in rows}
    out, cur = [], parent.get(int(pid))
    while cur and cur not in out:
        out.append(cur)
        cur = parent.get(cur)
    return out


def sample(uptime_text: str, ps_text: str, t: float, busy_cpu: float) -> dict:
    rows = parse_ps(ps_text)
    busy = [dict(r, cmd=r["cmd"][:160], ancestors=ancestors(rows, r["pid"])) for r in rows if r["cpu"] > busy_cpu]
    return dict(kind="sample", t=float(t), load=parse_uptime(uptime_text), uptime=(uptime_text or "").strip(),
                busy=busy)


def take(busy_cpu: float) -> dict:
    import time
    up = subprocess.run(["uptime"], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    ps = subprocess.run(["ps", "-A", "-o", "pid=,ppid=,pcpu=,command="], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    return sample(up, ps, time.time(), busy_cpu)


def read(path) -> list:
    p = Path(path)
    if not p.exists():
        return []
    out = []
    for line in p.read_text().splitlines():
        try:
            rec = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
        if rec.get("kind") in ("sample", "wait"):
            out.append(rec)
    return sorted(out, key=lambda r: r["t"])


def window(samples, start: float, end: float) -> list:
    return [x for x in samples if float(start) <= x["t"] <= float(end)]


def _own(proc: dict, own_pids) -> bool:
    own = {int(p) for p in own_pids}
    return proc["pid"] in own or bool(own & set(proc.get("ancestors", [])))


def offenders(samples, own_pids, busy_samples: int) -> list:
    runs: dict = {}
    best: dict = {}
    for x in samples:
        now = {b["pid"]: b for b in x["busy"] if not _own(b, own_pids)}
        for pid in list(runs):
            if pid not in now:
                runs.pop(pid)
        for pid, b in now.items():
            r = runs.setdefault(pid, dict(pid=pid, cmd=b["cmd"], n_consecutive=0, first_t=x["t"], last_t=x["t"]))
            r["n_consecutive"] += 1
            r["last_t"] = x["t"]
            if r["n_consecutive"] > best.get(pid, {}).get("n_consecutive", 0):
                best[pid] = dict(r)
    return [best[pid] for pid in sorted(best) if best[pid]["n_consecutive"] >= int(busy_samples)]


def bench_validity(samples, start: float, end: float, own_pid: int, busy_samples: int) -> dict:
    w = window(samples, start, end)
    if not w:
        return dict(valid=False, reason="no load samples in the bench window", offenders=[], n_samples=0)
    off = offenders(w, {own_pid}, busy_samples)
    return dict(valid=not off, reason=None if not off else "a process outside the bench above the CPU limit",
                offenders=off, n_samples=len(w))


def quiet(samples, exclude_pids, busy_samples: int) -> bool:
    last = list(samples)[-int(busy_samples):]
    return len(last) == int(busy_samples) and all(not [b for b in x["busy"] if not _own(b, exclude_pids)] for x in last)


def summary(samples, start: float, end: float, own_pid: int, busy_samples: int) -> dict:
    w = window(samples, start, end)
    l1 = [x["load"][0] for x in w if x.get("load")]
    return dict(n_samples=len(w), load1_median=float(np.median(l1)) if l1 else None,
                load1_max=float(max(l1)) if l1 else None, offenders=offenders(w, {own_pid}, busy_samples))
```

`scripts/ad_load_log.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""AD.4: one load sample a minute (uptime + processes above 50% CPU with their ancestry) appended to a JSONL log, until
--until-pid exits (or forever). The first line records --own-pid: the process tree whose CPU is the measured work.

    uv run python scripts/ad_load_log.py --out .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs/ad_bench_load.log --own-pid 0
    uv run python scripts/ad_load_log.py --out .../ad_run_load.log --own-pid $RUN_PID --until-pid $RUN_PID"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import load
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC


def alive(pid) -> bool:
    if not pid:
        return True
    try:
        os.kill(int(pid), 0)
        return True
    except OSError:
        return False


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--own-pid", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--until-pid", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--interval", type=float, default=60.0)
    ap.add_argument("--count", type=int, default=0, help="stop after N samples (0 = no limit)")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    out = Path(a.out)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out.open("a") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps(dict(kind="start", t=time.time(), own_pid=a.own_pid, until_pid=a.until_pid)) + "\n")
    n = 0
    while alive(a.until_pid):
        x = load.take(SPEC.busy_cpu)
        with out.open("a") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(x) + "\n")
        n += 1
        if a.count and n >= a.count:
            break
        time.sleep(a.interval)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`scripts/ad_wait_quiet.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""AD.4's start condition: wait until no process (other than this one) has been above 50% CPU for 3 consecutive
one-minute samples; every sample is appended to --log (the uptime log). Exit 0 when quiet, 3 after --max-minutes.

    uv run python scripts/ad_wait_quiet.py --log .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs/ad_bench_load.log"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import load
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--log", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--samples", type=int, default=SPEC.busy_samples)
    ap.add_argument("--interval", type=float, default=60.0)
    ap.add_argument("--max-minutes", type=float, default=240.0)
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    log = Path(a.log)
    log.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    me, seen, t0 = {os.getpid()}, [], time.time()
    while time.time() - t0 <= a.max_minutes * 60:
        x = dict(load.take(SPEC.busy_cpu), kind="wait")
        with log.open("a") as f:
            f.write(json.dumps(x) + "\n")
        seen.append(x)
        if load.quiet(seen, me, a.samples):
            print(f"quiet: {a.samples} samples without a process above {SPEC.busy_cpu}% CPU; load {x['load']}", flush=True)
            return 0
        time.sleep(a.interval)
    print(f"not quiet after {a.max_minutes} min", flush=True)
    return 3


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`scripts/write_ad_budget.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Spec AD.6 4: the re-bench median and AD's extrapolation. Reads every attempt results/m4-bench/ad-<n>/bench.json (an
attempt directory without bench.json counts as an invalid attempt), judges each attempt valid from the load log
(AD.4: no outside process above 50% CPU in 3 consecutive samples inside its window), takes the median of the first 3
valid attempts and chooses T (120 with / without the new set, 80, or STOP_BUDGET).

    uv run python scripts/write_ad_budget.py --load-log .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs/ad_bench_load.log

Writes results/summary/ad_budget.json. Exit 0 OK, 2 STOP_BUDGET or STOP_BENCH (6 attempts without 3 valid), 3 fewer
than 3 valid attempts so far (run the next attempt)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import budget, load, store
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL, SPEC


def attempts(root: Path, samples) -> list:
    out = []
    dirs = sorted((d for d in root.glob("ad-*") if d.is_dir() and d.name[3:].isdigit()), key=lambda d: int(d.name[3:]))
    for d in dirs:
        n, p = int(d.name[3:]), d / "bench.json"
        if not p.exists():
            out.append(dict(attempt=n, valid=False, reason="no bench.json", s_per_batch_battle=None,
                            sit_eval_s_per_eval=None, offenders=[]))
            continue
        b = json.loads(p.read_text())
        v = load.bench_validity(samples, b["t_started"], b["t_ended"], b["pid"], SPEC.busy_samples)
        out.append(dict(attempt=n, valid=v["valid"], reason=v["reason"], offenders=v["offenders"],
                        n_samples=v["n_samples"], s_per_batch_battle=float(b["s_per_batch_battle"]),
                        sit_eval_s_per_eval=float(b["sit_eval_s_per_eval"]), bench_sha256=store.sha256_file(p)))
    return out


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--root", default="results/m4-bench")
    ap.add_argument("--load-log", required=True)
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    samples = load.read(a.load_log)
    att = attempts(Path(a.root), samples)
    summ = budget.bench_summary(att, SPEC)
    info = dict(bench=summ, attempts=att, load_log=a.load_log,
                load_log_sha256=store.sha256_file(a.load_log) if Path(a.load_log).exists() else None, label=LABEL)
    if summ["status"] != "OK":
        store.write_json(budget.BUDGET, dict(info, status=summ["status"], T=None, new_set=None))
        print(f"{summ['status']}: {len(summ['used'])} valid of {len(att)} attempts; wrote {budget.BUDGET}", flush=True)
        return 3 if summ["status"] == "MORE" else 2
    c = budget.choose(SPEC, summ["s_per_batch_battle"], summ["sit_eval_s"])
    store.write_json(budget.BUDGET, dict(c, **info))
    cases = {k: round(v, 2) for k, v in c["cases"].items()}
    print(f"{c['status']}: T {c['T']}, new set {c['new_set']}; cases {cases} h (limit {c['limit_h']:.3f} h); "
          f"bench medians {summ['s_per_batch_battle']:.1f} s/batch-battle, {summ['sit_eval_s']:.1f} s/evaluation from "
          f"attempts {summ['used']}; wrote {budget.BUDGET}", flush=True)
    return 0 if c["status"] == "OK" else 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```
