### Task 12: 실행 manifest와 체크포인트 키 (AD.4 "실행 manifest", AD.2 (나), `STOP_MANIFEST`)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/run_manifest.py`, `scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_run_manifest.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `store`(`write_json`, `digest`, `sha256_file`, `head_commit`, `code_changes_since`), Task 5 `inputs`(`learn_canonical`, `prefix`, `input_paths`), Task 10 `budget.BUDGET`(스크립트가 읽는다).
- Produces:
  - `RUN_MANIFEST = "results/summary/ad_run_manifest.json"`, `CODE_PATHS = ("flymon", "scripts/run_ad.py")`
  - `manifest_path(spec) -> Path`(본 실행은 `RUN_MANIFEST`, 스모크는 `results/m4-smoke/ad/run_manifest.json`)
  - `build(spec, *, T, new_set, budget_path, inputs_manifest_path, commit) -> dict(status, mode, T, new_set, points, new_points, futility_point, learn_prefix_digest, budget_path, budget_sha256, inputs_manifest_path, inputs_manifest_sha256, code_commit, code_paths, label)`
  - `check(man, spec, *, check_code=True) -> list[str]` — 어긋난 필드 이름([] = 맞음)
  - `checkpoint_key(*, cfg_hash, layout, input_digests, mode, lever_sha, run_manifest_sha256) -> str`
  - `scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py [--smoke]` — 본 실행은 `ad_budget.json` 상태 OK · 입력 manifest 상태 OK · 코드 경로가 커밋된 상태여야 쓴다

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_run_manifest.py`:

```python
"""The run manifest (AD.4): T, the points, the learning-prefix digest used, the new-set flag, the budget and input
manifest sha256 and the code commit, verified at every run and resume (STOP_MANIFEST); its sha256 is part of the
checkpoint key."""
import importlib.util
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from flymon.ad import budget, inputs, run_manifest as rm, store
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC, smoke

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def repo(tmp_path):
    run = lambda *a: subprocess.run(["git", *a], cwd=tmp_path, check=True, capture_output=True)
    run("init", "-q"); run("config", "user.email", "t@t"); run("config", "user.name", "t")
    (tmp_path / "flymon").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "flymon/x.py").write_text("x = 1\n")
    (tmp_path / "scripts").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "scripts/run_ad.py").write_text("# runner\n")
    run("add", "."); run("commit", "-qm", "code")
    return run


def files(tmp_path, spec, T=120):
    _, man_p = inputs.input_paths(spec)
    store.write_json(man_p, {"status": "OK", "fixed": {}})
    bp = Path(budget.BUDGET) if spec.mode == "run" else None
    if bp is not None:
        store.write_json(bp, {"status": "OK", "T": T, "new_set": True})
    return bp, man_p


def test_build_and_check(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    repo(tmp_path)
    bp, man_p = files(tmp_path, SPEC)
    m = rm.build(SPEC, T=120, new_set=True, budget_path=bp, inputs_manifest_path=man_p, commit=store.head_commit())
    assert m["points"] == [0, 20, 40, 60, 80, 100, 120] and m["new_points"] == [0, 40, 120]
    assert m["learn_prefix_digest"] == "4a19075eacf8a74904b5e41d1e0221d3cc7cf3d49006c883ce6aeff9f4374867"
    assert rm.check(m, SPEC) == []
    m80 = rm.build(SPEC, T=80, new_set=False, budget_path=bp, inputs_manifest_path=man_p, commit=store.head_commit())
    assert m80["learn_prefix_digest"].startswith("6036ac80bae4") and m80["new_points"] == []


def test_check_detects_changed_budget_code_and_points(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    run = repo(tmp_path)
    bp, man_p = files(tmp_path, SPEC)
    m = rm.build(SPEC, T=120, new_set=True, budget_path=bp, inputs_manifest_path=man_p, commit=store.head_commit())
    assert rm.check(dict(m, points=[0, 40, 120]), SPEC) == ["points"]
    assert rm.check(dict(m, T=100), SPEC) == ["T", "points", "new_points", "learn_prefix_digest", "budget_decision"]
    store.write_json(bp, {"status": "OK", "T": 120, "new_set": False})
    assert rm.check(m, SPEC) == ["budget_sha256", "budget_decision"]
    store.write_json(bp, {"status": "OK", "T": 120, "new_set": True})
    (tmp_path / "flymon/x.py").write_text("x = 2\n")
    assert rm.check(m, SPEC) == ["code_changed: flymon/x.py"]
    assert rm.check(m, SPEC, check_code=False) == []
    run("commit", "-qam", "edit")
    assert rm.check(m, SPEC) == ["code_changed: flymon/x.py"]


def test_checkpoint_key_moves_with_the_manifest():
    k = dict(cfg_hash="c", layout=[["FLYL", 0, 24]], input_digests={"learn": "a"}, mode="run", lever_sha="l")
    assert rm.checkpoint_key(**k, run_manifest_sha256="1") != rm.checkpoint_key(**k, run_manifest_sha256="2")


def load_script():
    s = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("warm", ROOT / "scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(s); s.loader.exec_module(mod); return mod


def test_script_refuses_without_ok_budget_or_with_dirty_code(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    repo(tmp_path)
    w = load_script()
    _, man_p = inputs.input_paths(SPEC)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="ad_inputs_manifest"):
        w.main([])
    store.write_json(man_p, {"status": "OK", "fixed": {}})
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="ad_budget"):
        w.main([])
    bp = Path(budget.BUDGET)
    store.write_json(bp, {"status": "STOP_BUDGET", "T": None})
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_BUDGET"):
        w.main([])
    store.write_json(bp, {"status": "OK", "T": 80, "new_set": True})
    (tmp_path / "flymon/x.py").write_text("x = 3\n")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="uncommitted"):
        w.main([])
    subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-qam", "c"], cwd=tmp_path, check=True)
    assert w.main([]) == 0
    m = json.loads((tmp_path / rm.RUN_MANIFEST).read_text())
    assert m["T"] == 80 and m["points"] == [0, 20, 40, 60, 80] and m["new_points"] == [0, 40, 80]


def test_smoke_manifest(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    repo(tmp_path)
    files(tmp_path, smoke())
    assert load_script().main(["--smoke"]) == 0
    m = json.loads((tmp_path / "results/m4-smoke/ad/run_manifest.json").read_text())
    assert (m["T"], m["points"], m["budget_path"], m["mode"]) == (2, [0, 1, 2], None, "smoke")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/run_manifest.py`:

```python
"""The run manifest (spec AD.4): committed after the re-bench and the extrapolation, before the run. It fixes T, the
point list, the new-set flag (and its points), the digest of the learning-schedule prefix actually used, the sha256 of
ad_budget.json and of the AD input manifest, and the code commit. Every run and resume verifies it (check(); a
mismatch is STOP_MANIFEST) and its sha256 is part of the checkpoint key, so a checkpoint written under another manifest
cannot be resumed (run_ad_arm raises STOP_MANIFEST). The code check lists every file under CODE_PATHS that differs from
the manifest's commit, committed later or not. STOP_FUTILE and a budget stop do not change the manifest; they go in
the result. The smoke writes its own manifest (no budget) under results/m4-smoke/ad/."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path

from ..rescope import blocks
from . import inputs
from .spec import LABEL
from .store import code_changes_since, digest, sha256_file

RUN_MANIFEST = "results/summary/ad_run_manifest.json"
CODE_PATHS = ("flymon", "scripts/run_ad.py")


def manifest_path(spec) -> Path:
    return Path(RUN_MANIFEST) if spec.mode == "run" else Path(spec.out_root) / "run_manifest.json"


def _prefix_digest(spec, T: int) -> str:
    return blocks.schedule_digest(inputs.prefix(inputs.learn_canonical(spec), T))


def build(spec, *, T: int, new_set: bool, budget_path, inputs_manifest_path, commit: str) -> dict:
    return dict(status="OK", mode=spec.mode, T=int(T), new_set=bool(new_set), points=list(spec.points(T)),
                new_points=list(spec.new_points(T)) if new_set else [], futility_point=spec.futility_point,
                learn_prefix_digest=_prefix_digest(spec, T),
                budget_path=None if budget_path is None else str(budget_path),
                budget_sha256=None if budget_path is None else sha256_file(budget_path),
                inputs_manifest_path=str(inputs_manifest_path), inputs_manifest_sha256=sha256_file(inputs_manifest_path),
                code_commit=str(commit), code_paths=list(CODE_PATHS), label=LABEL)


def check(man: dict, spec, *, check_code: bool = True) -> list:
    bad = []
    T = int(man.get("T") or -1)
    if man.get("status") != "OK" or man.get("mode") != spec.mode:
        bad.append("status")
    if T not in (spec.learn_battles, spec.fallback_battles):
        bad.append("T")
    if man.get("points") != list(spec.points(T)):
        bad.append("points")
    if man.get("new_points") != (list(spec.new_points(T)) if man.get("new_set") else []):
        bad.append("new_points")
    if man.get("learn_prefix_digest") != _prefix_digest(spec, max(T, 0)):
        bad.append("learn_prefix_digest")
    if man.get("budget_path") is not None:
        bp = Path(man["budget_path"])
        if not bp.exists() or sha256_file(bp) != man.get("budget_sha256"):
            bad.append("budget_sha256")
        b = json.loads(bp.read_text()) if bp.exists() else {}
        if b.get("status") != "OK" or b.get("T") != man.get("T") or b.get("new_set") != man.get("new_set"):
            bad.append("budget_decision")
    ip = Path(man.get("inputs_manifest_path") or "")
    if not ip.is_file() or sha256_file(ip) != man.get("inputs_manifest_sha256"):
        bad.append("inputs_manifest_sha256")
    if check_code:
        changed = code_changes_since(man["code_commit"], man.get("code_paths") or CODE_PATHS)
        if changed:
            bad.append("code_changed: " + ", ".join(changed))
    return bad


def checkpoint_key(*, cfg_hash: str, layout, input_digests: dict, mode: str, lever_sha: str,
                   run_manifest_sha256: str) -> str:
    return digest(dict(cfg=cfg_hash, layout=[list(x) for x in layout], inputs=input_digests, mode=mode,
                       lever=lever_sha, run_manifest=run_manifest_sha256))
```

(테스트 `test_check_detects_changed_budget_code_and_points`의 `T=100`은 `T`(허용 값 아님) · `points` · `new_points` · `learn_prefix_digest`와, 예산 파일의 T(120)와 달라 `budget_decision`까지 어긋나게 한다.)

`scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Spec AD.6 4, last step: the run manifest, from results/summary/ad_budget.json (status OK: T and the new-set flag)
and the AD input manifest (status OK), at the current commit; the code under flymon/ and scripts/run_ad.py must be
committed. --smoke writes the smoke's manifest (T = 2, no budget) under results/m4-smoke/ad/.

    uv run python scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py
    uv run python scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py --smoke"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import inputs, run_manifest as rm, store
from flymon.ad.budget import BUDGET
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC, smoke


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--allow-dirty", action="store_true", help="smoke only: skip the uncommitted-code refusal")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    spec = smoke() if a.smoke else SPEC
    _, man_p = inputs.input_paths(spec)
    if not man_p.exists() or json.loads(man_p.read_text()).get("status") != "OK":
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {man_p} missing or not OK (scripts/write_ad_inputs.py"
                         f"{' --smoke' if a.smoke else ''}; STOP_REUSE stops AD)")
    if a.smoke:
        T, new_set, bp = spec.learn_battles, True, None
    else:
        bp = Path(BUDGET)
        if not bp.exists():
            raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {bp} does not exist (scripts/write_ad_budget.py)")
        b = json.loads(bp.read_text())
        if b.get("status") != "OK":
            raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {bp} status {b.get('status')!r} (STOP_BUDGET / STOP_BENCH stop AD)")
        T, new_set = int(b["T"]), bool(b["new_set"])
    commit = store.head_commit()
    dirty = store.code_changes_since(commit, rm.CODE_PATHS)
    if dirty and not (a.smoke and a.allow_dirty):
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: uncommitted code {dirty} (the run manifest pins the code commit)")
    m = rm.build(spec, T=T, new_set=new_set, budget_path=bp, inputs_manifest_path=man_p, commit=commit)
    out = rm.manifest_path(spec)
    store.write_json(out, m)
    print(f"run manifest: T {T}, points {m['points']}, new set {new_set} at {m['new_points']}, commit {commit[:12]}; "
          f"wrote {out}", flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

### Task 13: Showdown 배선과 AD 실행기 `scripts/run_ad.py` (본 실행 · 스모크 · 벤치)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/battles.py`, `scripts/run_ad.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_run.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 `ADAgentPlayer` · `ADEvalPlayer` · `AttemptBook` · `ADSwarm`, Task 3 `LVSwarm(seed_fly)`, Task 4 `OSEncoder`, Task 5 `inputs`, Task 6 `reuse`, Task 7 `hooks`, Task 8 `validity`, Task 9 `run_ad_arm`, Task 10 `budget`, Task 12 `run_manifest`; `flymon.ac.battles.ACBattles` · `team` · `on_player_loop`; `flymon.ac.budget.open_session` · `close_session`; `flymon.ac.manifest.check_model` · `MODEL_MANIFEST`; `flymon.ac.config.load_lv_config` · `lv_codebook`; `flymon.ac.invalid.InvalidBook`; `flymon.ac.schedules.check_names`.
- Produces:
  - `flymon.ad.battles.ADBattles(lay, out, srv_cfg, swarm, encoders, attempts)`: `player(block, sb)`, `attempt_for(block)`, `reset_player(block, fly)`(시도 토큰을 먼저 거둔다), `close_block`, `close`
  - `scripts/run_ad.py` 함수: `parse_args(argv)`, `spec_for(a)`, `load_inputs(spec) -> (doc, info)`, `load_model() -> (doc, r)`, `load_run_manifest(spec, a) -> (man | None, T, new_set)`, `load_budget(spec) -> dict | None`, `session_window(spec, out, a, t0) -> (cap_s, remaining_s)`, `fresh_bench(out)`, `files() -> list`, `main(argv) -> int`
  - 결과 파일 `<out>/result.json`: `status`("COMPLETE" | "STOP_BUDGET"), `mode`, `T`, `T_effective`, `new_set`, `futility`, `layout`, `per_fly`(완료 때), `books`{L, E}, `inputs`, `eval_digest`, `learn_prefix_digest`, `run_manifest`{path, sha256}, `model`{path, sha256}, `recovery_per_pulse`, `lever_sha`, `config_hash`, `gate`, `stale_pulses`, `wall_clock_s`, `workers`, `complete`, `label`, `provenance`
  - 벤치 파일 `<out>/bench.json`: `s_per_batch_battle`, `sit_eval_s_per_eval`, `n_init`, `pid`, `t_started`, `t_ended`, `attempt`, `flies`, `workers`, `battles`, `timing`, `git`, `label`

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_run.py`:

```python
"""scripts/run_ad.py without Showdown: arguments, the refusals that run before any pool or server (inputs ->
STOP_MANIFEST, stage-1 reuse -> STOP_REUSE, model manifest, run manifest -> STOP_MANIFEST, a used bench directory,
a spent budget), the session window, and the id / name lengths."""
import importlib.util
import json
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from flymon.ac import schedules as ac_sched
from flymon.ad import inputs, run_manifest as rm, store
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL, SPEC, smoke
from tests.ad.test_ad_reuse import fake_stage1

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def load():
    s = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("rad", ROOT / "scripts/run_ad.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(s); s.loader.exec_module(mod); return mod


def smoke_inputs():
    s = smoke()
    doc_p, man_p = inputs.input_paths(s)
    d = inputs.inputs_doc(s)
    store.write_json(doc_p, d)
    store.write_json(man_p, inputs.input_manifest(d, doc_p))
    return doc_p, man_p


def frozen_model(tmp_path):
    p = tmp_path / "results/summary/ac_model_manifest.json"
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text(json.dumps({"status": "FROZEN", "recovery_per_pulse": 0.001}))


def test_parse_args():
    ra = load()
    a = ra.parse_args(["--smoke"])
    assert a.workers == 4 and ra.spec_for(a).out_root == "results/m4-smoke/ad"
    a = ra.parse_args(["--bench", "2"])
    assert a.workers == 16 and ra.spec_for(a).out_root == "results/m4-bench/ad-2"
    assert ra.spec_for(ra.parse_args([])) == SPEC
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        ra.parse_args(["--bench", "1", "--resume"])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        ra.parse_args(["--bench", "1", "--smoke"])


def test_refuses_missing_or_changed_inputs(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ra = load()
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="does not exist"):
        ra.main(["--smoke"])
    doc_p, _ = smoke_inputs()
    d = json.loads(doc_p.read_text())
    d["eval"][0]["my_team"] = list(reversed(d["eval"][0]["my_team"]))
    doc_p.write_text(json.dumps(d))
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_MANIFEST"):
        ra.main(["--smoke"])


def test_refuses_without_a_frozen_model(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ra = load()
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    smoke_inputs()
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="ac_model_manifest"):
        ra.main(["--smoke"])
    store_p = tmp_path / "results/summary/ac_model_manifest.json"
    store_p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    store_p.write_text(json.dumps({"status": "STOP_NO_RECOVERY"}))
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="not FROZEN"):
        ra.main(["--smoke"])


def test_resume_refuses_a_changed_run_manifest(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ra = load()
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    _, man_p = smoke_inputs()
    frozen_model(tmp_path)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="run_manifest"):
        ra.main(["--smoke", "--allow-dirty"])
    m = rm.build(smoke(), T=2, new_set=True, budget_path=None, inputs_manifest_path=man_p, commit="0" * 40)
    store.write_json(rm.manifest_path(smoke()), dict(m, points=[0, 2]))
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_MANIFEST"):
        ra.main(["--smoke", "--resume", "--allow-dirty"])


def test_run_mode_refuses_a_changed_stage1_record(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ra = load()
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    fake_stage1(tmp_path)
    w = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("wai", ROOT / "scripts/write_ad_inputs.py")
    mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(w); w.loader.exec_module(mod)
    assert mod.main([]) == 0
    with (tmp_path / "results/m4/stage1/BRAIN/logs/situations_init.jsonl").open("a") as f:
        f.write(json.dumps({"fly": 12, "point": 0, "arm": "COFF", "frozen": True, "label": LABEL}) + "\n")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_REUSE"):
        ra.main([])


def test_bench_refuses_a_used_directory(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ra = load()
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    (tmp_path / "results/m4-bench/ad-1/logs").mkdir(parents=True)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="earlier bench attempt"):
        ra.fresh_bench(Path("results/m4-bench/ad-1"))
    (tmp_path / "results/m4-bench/ad-2").mkdir(parents=True)
    (tmp_path / "results/m4-bench/ad-2/inputs.json").write_text("{}")
    ra.fresh_bench(Path("results/m4-bench/ad-2"))                 # inputs alone are not an earlier attempt


def test_session_window(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    ra = load()
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    out = Path("results/m4/ad")
    a = ra.parse_args([])
    cap, left = ra.session_window(SPEC, out, a, 0.0)
    assert cap == 24 * 3600 and left == pytest.approx(SPEC.budget_limit_h() * 3600)
    store.write_json(out / "wall_clock.json", {"sessions": [dict(started_utc="1970-01-01T00:00:00+00:00",
                                                                seconds=47.5 * 3600, status="ok")]})
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="STOP_BUDGET"):
        ra.session_window(SPEC, out, a, 0.0)
    cap, left = ra.session_window(smoke(), Path("results/m4-smoke/ad"), ra.parse_args(["--smoke", "--session-hours",
                                                                                         "0.5"]), 0.0)
    assert cap == 1800.0 and left == float("inf")


def test_run_ids_and_names_fit():
    doc = inputs.inputs_doc(SPEC)
    learn, ev = inputs.schedules_for(SPEC, doc, 120)
    ac_sched.check_names(learn + ev, SPEC.retry_max)
    assert {inputs.account(a, b, g) for a, _, g in SPEC.layout() for b in "LE"} and len(learn) == 2880
```

`flymon/ad/battles.py`:

```python
"""Showdown plumbing for AD (spec AD.1, AD.5 2): AC's ACBattles with AD's players (ADAgentPlayer in learning,
ADEvalPlayer in evaluation; both carry the global fly, the attempt token and the generation reader), AD's account names,
one encoder per fly (FLY-L the FLY encoder, FLY-OS its own OSEncoder). Every attempt begins its token (and the
encoder's schedule id) before it plays; the rollback of an unfinished attempt retires the token before the weights are
restored (reset_player is called first in run_ad_arm's rollback)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from ..ac.battles import ACBattles, on_player_loop, team
from . import inputs


class ADBattles(ACBattles):
    def __init__(self, lay, out, srv_cfg, swarm, encoders, attempts):
        super().__init__({i: arm for i, (arm, _, _) in enumerate(lay)}, out, srv_cfg, swarm=swarm, encoders=encoders,
                         phase="ad")
        self.lay, self.attempts = list(lay), attempts

    # copied from flymon/ac/battles.py:ACBattles.player (AD players and account names; brain arms only)
    def player(self, block: str, sb):
        p = self.players[block].get(sb.fly_id)
        if p is not None:
            return p
        from poke_env.ps_client import AccountConfiguration
        from ..agent.screen import ScreenTable
        from ..battle.coach import Coach
        from .player import ADAgentPlayer, ADEvalPlayer
        i = int(sb.fly_id)
        arm, _, g = self.lay[i]
        logs = self.out / "logs" if block == "L" else self.out / "logs" / "eval"
        dbar, rbar = self.bars[block]
        kw = dict(fly=i, encoder=self.encoders[i], table=ScreenTable.allow_all(), rbarrier=rbar, barrier=dbar, gfly=g,
                  attempts=self.attempts, gen_of=self.swarm.gen_of, log_path=logs / f"fly{i:02d}.jsonl", coach=Coach(),
                  account_configuration=AccountConfiguration(inputs.account(arm, block, g), None),
                  battle_format="gen1ou", server_configuration=self.srv_cfg, team=team(sb.my_team),
                  max_concurrent_battles=1)
        p = ADEvalPlayer(**kw) if block == "E" else ADAgentPlayer(**kw)
        self.players[block][i] = p
        return p

    def reset_player(self, block: str, fly: int) -> None:
        self.attempts.retire(fly)
        super().reset_player(block, fly)

    # copied from flymon/ac/battles.py:ACBattles.attempt_for (+ start_attempt: the attempt token, the encoder's id)
    def attempt_for(self, block: str):
        async def attempt(sb, n):
            from poke_env.ps_client import AccountConfiguration
            from ..ac import schedules as ac_sched
            from ..battle.opponents import KINDS
            p = self.player(block, sb)
            opp = KINDS[sb.opponent](account_configuration=AccountConfiguration(
                ac_sched.opponent_name(sb.battle_id, n), None), battle_format="gen1ou",
                server_configuration=self.srv_cfg, team=team(sb.opp_team), max_concurrent_battles=1)
            p.update_team(team(sb.my_team))
            p.start_attempt(sb.battle_id, inputs.battle_index(sb.battle_id), n)
            before = set(p.battles)
            try:
                await p.battle_against(opp, n_battles=1)
                if getattr(p, "fatal", None) is not None:
                    raise p.fatal
                new = sorted(set(p.battles) - before)
                if len(new) != 1:
                    return {"finished": False, "won": None, "battle_tag": None, "new_battles": new}
                tag = new[0]
                st = p.battle_stats(tag)
                res = dict(st, battle_tag=tag, turns=int(p.battles[tag].turn))
                if not st["finished"]:
                    return res
                await on_player_loop(p, p.drain())
                if p.fatal is not None:
                    raise p.fatal
                p.write_battle_summary(tag, self.swarm.compartment_fracs(sb.fly_id))
                return res
            finally:
                await opp.ps_client.stop_listening()
        return attempt
```

`scripts/run_ad.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""AD's run (spec AD.1-AD.7): FLY-L 12 + FLY-OS 12 on one L_V LeverFlyPool through a local Showdown server.

    uv run python scripts/run_ad.py                    # the run under results/m4/ad/ (a session cap -> --resume)
    uv run python scripts/run_ad.py --resume
    uv run python scripts/run_ad.py --smoke --stop-after 3 ; ... --smoke --resume    # AD.5 1 smoke
    uv run python scripts/run_ad.py --bench N          # AD.4 re-bench attempt N under results/m4-bench/ad-N/

Learning (T from the run manifest; softmax; own pulses with attempt tokens) in two phases split at the futility check
(40), situation-pair evaluations at 0 / 20 / ... / T (FLY-L also on OS odours; the new 20 pairs at 0 / 40 / T if the
manifest includes them), then the evaluation block (the shared 20-battle schedule, argmax with the seeded tie rule,
plasticity off). Refusals run before any pool or server: inputs (STOP_MANIFEST), stage-1 reuse (STOP_REUSE), the model
manifest (FROZEN), the run manifest (STOP_MANIFEST), the budget left (STOP_BUDGET), uncommitted code. Each battle starts
only if its batch's worst case fits the session and the total budget (a total-budget refusal is STOP_BUDGET in the run:
result.json records it and the reading uses the latest common point)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import dataclasses
import datetime as dt
import glob
import json
import os
import socket
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ac import budget as ac_budget
from flymon.ac import schedules as ac_sched
from flymon.ac.manifest import MODEL_MANIFEST
from flymon.ad import budget, inputs, reuse, run_manifest as rm, store
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL, SPEC, bench, smoke
from flymon.rescope import blocks

NPZ = "data/malecns.npz"
POOL_TIMEOUT_S = 7200


def spec_for(a):
    return smoke() if a.smoke else bench(a.bench) if a.bench is not None else SPEC


def parse_args(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    g = ap.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    g.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true")
    g.add_argument("--bench", type=int, default=None, metavar="N")
    ap.add_argument("--resume", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--stop-after", type=int, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--session-hours", type=float, default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--npz", default=NPZ)
    ap.add_argument("--allow-dirty", action="store_true")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if a.bench is not None and a.resume:
        ap.error("--bench is one fresh session")
    if a.smoke:
        a.workers = 4
    if a.bench is not None:
        a.workers = SPEC.bench_workers
    return a


def load_inputs(spec) -> tuple:
    doc_p, man_p = inputs.input_paths(spec)
    for p in (doc_p, man_p):
        if not p.exists():
            raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {p} does not exist (scripts/write_ad_inputs.py"
                             f"{'' if spec.mode == 'run' else ' --' + spec.mode})")
    doc, man = json.loads(doc_p.read_text()), json.loads(man_p.read_text())
    if man.get("status") != "OK":
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {man_p} status {man.get('status')!r} (STOP_REUSE / STOP_SET stop AD)")
    bad = inputs.check_inputs(doc, man, spec, doc_p)
    if bad:
        raise SystemExit(f"STOP_MANIFEST: {doc_p} does not match its seeds / manifest: {bad}")
    if spec.mode == "run":
        now = reuse.reuse_fields(doc["digests"]["confirm"], doc["digests"]["eval"], spec)
        bad = reuse.changed_since(man.get("reuse"), now) + reuse.reuse_problems(now, spec)
        if bad:
            raise SystemExit(f"STOP_REUSE: stage-1 fields changed or wrong since {man_p}: {bad}")
    return doc, dict(path=str(doc_p), manifest=str(man_p), manifest_sha256=store.sha256_file(man_p),
                     digests=doc["digests"])


def load_model() -> tuple:
    p = Path(MODEL_MANIFEST)
    if not p.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {p} does not exist (AC.7 2a)")
    d = json.loads(p.read_text())
    if d.get("status") != "FROZEN":
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {p} status {d.get('status')!r} is not FROZEN")
    return d, float(d["recovery_per_pulse"])


def load_run_manifest(spec, a) -> tuple:
    if spec.mode == "bench":
        return None, spec.learn_battles, True
    p = rm.manifest_path(spec)
    if not p.exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {p} does not exist (scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py"
                         f"{' --smoke' if spec.mode == 'smoke' else ''})")
    man = json.loads(p.read_text())
    bad = rm.check(man, spec, check_code=not (spec.mode == "smoke" and a.allow_dirty))
    if bad:
        raise SystemExit(f"STOP_MANIFEST: {p} does not match this run: {bad}")
    return man, int(man["T"]), bool(man["new_set"])


def load_budget(spec):
    if spec.mode != "run":
        return None
    d = json.loads(Path(budget.BUDGET).read_text())          # the run manifest already checked it exists and is OK
    return d


def session_window(spec, out, a, t0: float) -> tuple:
    if spec.mode == "run":
        spent = budget.spent_s(out)
        cap, left = budget.session_cap_s(spec, spent), budget.remaining_s(spec, spent)
    else:
        cap, left = spec.session_cap_h * 3600.0, float("inf")
    if a.session_hours is not None:
        cap = min(cap, a.session_hours * 3600.0)
    if cap <= 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"STOP_BUDGET: the {spec.budget_limit_h():.3f} h AD budget is spent (AD.4)")
    return cap, left


def fresh_bench(out) -> None:
    out = Path(out)
    if any((out / x).exists() for x in ("checkpoints", "logs", "bench.json", "wall_clock.json")):
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {out} holds an earlier bench attempt; every attempt is one fresh session in its "
                         f"own directory (use the next N)")


def files() -> list:
    return (sorted(glob.glob("flymon/ad/*.py")) + sorted(glob.glob("flymon/ac/*.py"))
            + ["flymon/brain/lv_pool.py", "flymon/rescope/blocks.py", "flymon/agent/runner.py",
               "flymon/agent/policy.py", "scripts/run_ad.py"])


def _port():
    with socket.socket() as s:
        s.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0)); return s.getsockname()[1]


def srv_config(srv):
    from poke_env.ps_client import ServerConfiguration
    return ServerConfiguration(srv.url_ws, "https://play.pokemonshowdown.com/action.php?")


async def drive(a, out, key, spec, learn, eval_, pool, swarm, lay, encoders, books, attempts, after, srv_cfg, gate,
                decide_futility):
    from flymon.ad import hooks
    from flymon.ad.battles import ADBattles
    from flymon.ad.runner import run_ad_arm
    b = ADBattles(lay, out, srv_cfg, swarm, encoders, attempts)
    try:
        return await run_ad_arm(out, learn=learn, eval_=eval_, attempt_for=hooks.skipping(b.attempt_for, books),
                                cfg_hash=key, retry_max=spec.retry_max, resume=a.resume, pool=pool, swarm=swarm,
                                reset_player=b.reset_player, stop_after=a.stop_after, on_block_end=b.close_block,
                                after_battle=after, should_stop=lambda: gate.refused is not None,
                                should_stop_battle=gate,
                                futility_point=spec.futility_point if spec.futility_checked() else None,
                                decide_futility=decide_futility)
    finally:
        await b.close()


def run_brain(a, spec, out, doc, model, r, learn, eval_, T, new_set, man_sha, gate_args) -> dict:
    import asyncio
    from flymon.ac import manifest
    from flymon.ac.config import load_lv_config, lv_codebook
    from flymon.ac.invalid import InvalidBook
    from flymon.ac.spec import SPEC as AC
    from flymon.ac.swarm import egrid_fn
    from flymon.ad import hooks, validity
    from flymon.ad.attempts import AttemptBook
    from flymon.ad.os_encoder import OSEncoder
    from flymon.ad.swarm import ADSwarm
    from flymon.agent.config import config_hash
    from flymon.agent.encode_grid import GridEncoder
    from flymon.battle.server import ShowdownServer
    from flymon.brain.circuits import Populations
    from flymon.brain.connectome import Connectome
    from flymon.brain.fly_pool import FlySpec
    from flymon.brain.h4_jobs import type_cells
    from flymon.brain.lv_pool import LeverFlyPool

    cfg = load_lv_config(r)
    cb, cb_digest = lv_codebook()
    bad = manifest.check_model(model, cfg=cfg, codebook_digest=cb_digest, spec=AC)
    if bad:
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: this run does not match {MODEL_MANIFEST}: {bad}")
    conn = Connectome.load(a.npz)
    pops = Populations.from_connectome(conn)
    lay = spec.layout()
    grid = GridEncoder(pops, cb, spec.dual_rule)
    encoders = {i: (OSEncoder(pops, cb, spec.dual_rule, g, spec.os_seed) if arm == "FLYOS" else grid)
                for i, (arm, _, g) in enumerate(lay)}
    sets = {"confirm": doc["confirm"]["pairs"], "new": doc["new"]["pairs"]}
    arm_of = {i: arm for i, (arm, _, _) in enumerate(lay)}
    mins = {k: v for k, v in spec.min_valid_of().items() if k in spec.arm_sizes()}
    books = {"L": InvalidBook(out / "invalid.json", arm_of, spec.arm_sizes(), mins),
             "E": InvalidBook(out / "invalid_eval.json", arm_of, spec.arm_sizes(), mins)}
    key = rm.checkpoint_key(cfg_hash=config_hash(cfg), layout=lay, input_digests=doc["digests"], mode=spec.mode,
                            lever_sha=spec.lever_sha, run_manifest_sha256=man_sha)
    sitlog = hooks.ADSituationLog(out / "logs")
    with LeverFlyPool(a.npz, cfg.params, [FlySpec() for _ in lay], edit=spec.lever_edit, p_type=spec.p_type,
                      workers=a.workers, timeout_s=POOL_TIMEOUT_S) as pool:
        sha = pool.lever_sha()
        if sha != model["lever_sha"]:
            raise SystemExit(f"refusing: the workers' L_V sha {sha[:12]} != the manifest's {model['lever_sha'][:12]}")
        attempts = AttemptBook()
        swarm = ADSwarm(pool, cfg, type_cells(conn, [cfg.readout["A"], cfg.readout["P"]]), pops.kc, attempts=attempts,
                        mode="learn", tie_seed=spec.tie_seed, kc_ratio=spec.kc_ratio, egrid=egrid_fn(grid),
                        seed_fly={i: g for i, (_, _, g) in enumerate(lay)},
                        stale_path=out / "logs" / "stale_pulses.jsonl")
        evaluate = hooks.make_evaluate(pool, swarm, cfg, spec, lay, sets, grid)
        hk = hooks.ADHooks(spec, T, new_set, lay, books, sitlog, evaluate)
        gate = budget.BatchGate(spec, T, new_set, times_path=out / "batch_times.json", **gate_args)

        def after(block, sb, rec):
            gate.finished(block, sb)                         # the battle's span, before its evaluations
            hk.after_battle(block, sb, rec)

        def decide_futility():
            fut = validity.persist_futility(out / "futility.json",
                                            validity.futility(sitlog.records(), sets["confirm"], spec))
            print(validity.futility_line(fut), flush=True)   # AD.2: only these two numbers and the decision
            return fut

        try:
            if a.resume:
                sitlog.filter(hooks.committed_all(out, key))
            t1 = time.time()
            n_init = hk.initial()
            t_sit = time.time() - t1
            with ShowdownServer(port=_port()) as srv:
                t2 = time.time()
                run = asyncio.run(drive(a, out, key, spec, learn, eval_, pool, swarm, lay, encoders, books, attempts,
                                        after, srv_config(srv), gate, decide_futility))
                t_run = time.time() - t2
            medians = {i: swarm.median_ratio(i) for i in range(len(lay))} if run["complete"] else None
        finally:
            swarm._exec.shutdown(wait=True)
    rows = None
    if run["complete"] and eval_:
        ids = set(run["learn_ids"])
        rows = blocks.arm_per_fly(out, n_flies=len(lay), eval_=eval_, run=run,
                                  learn=[sb for sb in learn if sb.battle_id in ids], spec=None, brain=True,
                                  weight_medians=medians)
        for i, row in enumerate(rows):
            arm, k, g = lay[i]
            row.update(arm=arm, k=k, gfly=g, learn_invalid=books["L"].invalid.get(i),
                       eval_invalid=books["E"].invalid.get(i))
    return dict(run=run, per_fly=rows, books={b: bk.status() for b, bk in books.items()}, lever_sha=sha,
                config_hash=config_hash(cfg), stale=len(swarm.stale),
                gate=dict(refused=gate.refused, refusal=gate.refusal, longest_s=gate.longest),
                timing=dict(t_sit_init_s=t_sit, n_init=n_init, t_run_s=t_run))


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    t0 = time.time()
    a = parse_args(argv)
    spec = spec_for(a)
    out = Path(spec.out_root)
    store.guard(out / "result.json")
    if spec.mode == "bench":
        fresh_bench(out)
    doc, info = load_inputs(spec)
    model, r = load_model()
    man, T, new_set = load_run_manifest(spec, a)
    spec = dataclasses.replace(spec, learn_battles=int(T))
    man_sha = store.sha256_file(rm.manifest_path(spec)) if man is not None else None
    bud = load_budget(spec)
    budget.close_stale(out, t0)                    # AD.4: a killed AD session is charged to this session's start
    cap, left = session_window(spec, out, a, t0)
    if (out / "result.json").exists() and not a.resume:
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {out / 'result.json'} exists; pass --resume to rewrite it")
    git = store.git_provenance(files=files())
    if git["dirty"] and not a.allow_dirty:
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: uncommitted changes in {git['dirty_files']} (commit them or pass --allow-dirty)")
    learn, eval_ = inputs.schedules_for(spec, doc, T)
    ac_sched.check_names(learn + eval_, spec.retry_max)
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    sess = ac_budget.open_session(out, t0)
    gate_args = dict(session_end=t0 + cap, budget_end=t0 + left,
                     bench_batch_s=bud["bench"]["s_per_batch_battle"] if bud else 0.0,
                     bench_eval_s=bud["bench"]["sit_eval_s"] if bud else 0.0)
    closed = {"wall": None}
    try:
        res = run_brain(a, spec, out, doc, model, r, learn, eval_, T, new_set, man_sha, gate_args)
        run = res["run"]
        wall = closed["wall"] = ac_budget.close_session(out, sess, t0, "ok", run)
        if spec.mode == "bench":
            if not run["complete"]:
                print("bench: stopped before completion; no bench.json (use the next attempt N)", flush=True)
                return 2
            t = res["timing"]
            b = dict(s_per_batch_battle=t["t_run_s"] / spec.learn_battles,
                     sit_eval_s_per_eval=t["t_sit_init_s"] / max(1, t["n_init"]), n_init=t["n_init"],
                     flies=spec.n_flies(), workers=a.workers, battles=spec.learn_battles, pid=os.getpid(),
                     t_started=t0, t_ended=time.time(), attempt=spec.bench_attempt, timing=t, git=git, label=LABEL)
            store.write_json(out / "bench.json", b)
            print(f"bench {spec.bench_attempt}: {b['s_per_batch_battle']:.1f} s/batch-battle, "
                  f"{b['sit_eval_s_per_eval']:.1f} s/evaluation ({t['n_init']}); wrote {out / 'bench.json'}", flush=True)
            return 0
        fut = run.get("futility") or {}
        base = dict(mode=spec.mode, T=T, new_set=new_set, futility=fut or None,
                    T_effective=spec.futility_point if fut.get("status") == "STOP_FUTILE" else T,
                    layout=[list(x) for x in spec.layout()], books=res["books"], inputs=info,
                    eval_digest=doc["digests"]["eval"], learn_prefix_digest=None if man is None else man["learn_prefix_digest"],
                    run_manifest=dict(path=str(rm.manifest_path(spec)), sha256=man_sha),
                    model=dict(path=MODEL_MANIFEST, sha256=store.sha256_file(MODEL_MANIFEST)), recovery_per_pulse=r,
                    lever_sha=res["lever_sha"], config_hash=res["config_hash"], gate=res["gate"],
                    stale_pulses=res["stale"], wall_clock_s=wall, workers=a.workers, label=LABEL,
                    provenance=dict(git=git, argv=list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv),
                                    finished_utc=dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat()))
        if not run["complete"]:
            if res["gate"]["refused"] == "STOP_BUDGET":
                store.write_json(out / "result.json", dict(base, status="STOP_BUDGET", complete=False, per_fly=None))
                print(f"AD: STOP_BUDGET (in run) at {res['gate']['refusal']}; wrote {out / 'result.json'}", flush=True)
                return 0
            print(f"AD: stopped before completion (played L {len(run['played']['L'])}, E {len(run['played']['E'])}; "
                  f"gate {res['gate']['refused']}); rerun with --resume", flush=True)
            return 0
        store.write_json(out / "result.json", dict(base, status="COMPLETE", complete=True, per_fly=res["per_fly"]))
        valid = sum(not row["invalid"] for row in res["per_fly"])
        print(f"AD: complete, valid flies {valid}/{len(res['per_fly'])}, futility {fut.get('status')}, T_effective "
              f"{base['T_effective']}; wrote {out / 'result.json'}", flush=True)
        return 0
    finally:
        if closed["wall"] is None:
            ac_budget.close_session(out, sess, t0, "aborted", None)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`ac_budget.open_session` · `close_session`은 `flymon.ac.store.write_json`으로 쓰고, 그 가드는 `results/m4/`, `results/m4-smoke/`, `results/m4-bench/` 아래를 허락하므로 AD 경로에 그대로 쓸 수 있다.

- [ ] **Step 4: 통과를 확인한다**

Run: `uv run pytest -q -o addopts="" tests/ad/test_ad_run.py`
Expected: PASS

- [ ] **Step 5: 커밋한다**

```bash
git add flymon/ad/battles.py scripts/run_ad.py tests/ad/test_ad_run.py
git commit -m "feat(ad): Showdown wiring and scripts/run_ad.py — run / smoke / bench, refusals before any pool or server"
```

---
### Task 14: KC 활성 보정 측정 — FLY 냄새 대 16종 OS 냄새, 결정 시드 3개 (AD.5 3, AD.8 5)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/calib.py`, `scripts/write_ad_os_calib.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_calib.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 4 `os_encoder.SPECIES` · `odour_for_species` · `total_strength_error`, `flymon.ac.situations.offline_odour`, `derive_seed`.
- Produces:
  - `CALIB = "results/summary/ad_os_kc_calib.json"`
  - `requests(grid, pairs, rep, tie_seed) -> (meta: list[dict], reqs: list[(0, odours, seed)])` — (rep, pair, side)마다 FLY 1개 + OS 16개, 같은 결정 시드
  - `kc_rows(meta, counts, n_skip: int) -> list[dict]` — 요청마다 후보별 KC 활성 수(`counts[:, n_skip:] > 0`의 합)
  - `ks(a, b) -> float`(두 표본 KS 거리), `measure(rows, mi, species_types) -> dict` — R_r · R · SD_R · 기술 타입별 · 실제 상대 단일/이중별 비 · KS · 10/50/90 백분위(관문 판단 없음)
  - `band(meas, spec) -> dict(status: "OK" | "STOP_BALANCE", R, sd_R, half_width, lo, hi)` — 스모크 검사(Task 15)가 커밋된 측정 파일에 적용한다

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_calib.py`:

```python
"""AD.5 3: the KC-activity calibration of FLY-OS on the naive brain. Requests (FLY + the 16 OS odours per situation,
one decision seed per (rep, pair, side)), the per-candidate KC counts, the measured ratios, and the pre-registered
band 1 +- max(0.15, 3 SD_R) applied after the measurement is committed."""
import numpy as np
import pytest

from flymon.ad import calib, os_encoder
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC
from flymon.agent.policy import derive_seed
from tests.ac.ac_fakes import MI, ST, stub_grid

PAIRS = [dict(me="Lapras", o1="Charizard", o2="Exeggutor", cands=["Surf", "Psychic", "Thunderbolt"])]


def test_requests_share_one_seed_per_situation_and_cover_16_species():
    meta, reqs = calib.requests(stub_grid(), PAIRS, 1, 305)
    assert len(reqs) == 2 * 17 and [m["variant"] for m in meta[:17]] == ["fly"] + list(os_encoder.SPECIES)
    assert {s for _, _, s in reqs[:17]} == {derive_seed(305, "os-calib", 1, 0, 0)}
    assert reqs[0][1] == [os_encoder.odour_for_species(stub_grid(), MI[m][0], "Charizard") for m in PAIRS[0]["cands"]]
    assert meta[17]["side"] == 1 and meta[17]["true"] == "Exeggutor"


def test_kc_rows_count_active_cells_after_the_readout():
    meta = [dict(rep=0, pair=0, side=0, variant="fly", true="Charizard", cands=["Surf", "Psychic"])]
    counts = [np.array([[9, 9, 1, 0, 2], [9, 9, 0, 0, 0]])]
    rows = calib.kc_rows(meta, counts, n_skip=2)
    assert rows[0]["kc"] == [2, 0]


def rows_for(ratio, reps=(0, 1, 2), jitter=0.0):
    out = []
    for r in reps:
        k = ratio + jitter * (r - 1)
        out.append(dict(rep=r, pair=0, side=0, variant="fly", true="Charizard", cands=["Surf", "Psychic"],
                        kc=[100, 100]))
        for sp in os_encoder.SPECIES:
            out.append(dict(rep=r, pair=0, side=0, variant=sp, true="Charizard", cands=["Surf", "Psychic"],
                            kc=[round(100 * k), round(100 * k)]))
    return out


def test_measure_and_band():
    m = calib.measure(rows_for(1.1, jitter=0.0), MI, ST)
    assert m["R_by_rep"] == [1.1, 1.1, 1.1] and m["R"] == pytest.approx(1.1) and m["sd_R"] == 0.0
    assert set(m["by_move_type"]) == {MI["Surf"][0], MI["Psychic"][0]} and "single" in m["by_true_dual"]
    assert set(m["percentiles"]) == {"fly", "os"} and m["ks"] == pytest.approx(1.0)
    b = calib.band(m, SPEC)
    assert b["status"] == "OK" and b["half_width"] == 0.15
    assert calib.band(calib.measure(rows_for(0.27), MI, ST), SPEC)["status"] == "STOP_BALANCE"   # FLY-TB's ratio
    wide = calib.measure(rows_for(1.2, jitter=0.1), MI, ST)
    assert wide["sd_R"] == pytest.approx(0.1) and calib.band(wide, SPEC)["half_width"] == pytest.approx(0.3)
    assert calib.band(wide, SPEC)["status"] == "OK"


def test_ks_distance():
    assert calib.ks([1, 2, 3], [1, 2, 3]) == 0.0
    assert calib.ks([1, 1, 1], [5, 5, 5]) == 1.0
```

`flymon/ad/calib.py`:

```python
"""AD.5 3: FLY-OS's strength balance on the naive L_V brain (w0). For every confirmation-set candidate situation (20
pairs x 2 sides x candidates) and decision seed r in {0, 1, 2}, one request with the FLY odours (true opponent) and 16
requests with the OS odours of each POOL species (uniform weights = the draw distribution), all with the seed
derive_seed(305, "os-calib", r, pair, side) (paired noise). Measured: R_r = mean OS candidate KC-active count / mean
FLY count, R = the mean of the three, SD_R (ddof 1), the ratio per move type and per true-opponent single / dual type,
the KS distance between the FLY and the pooled OS counts, and their 10 / 50 / 90 percentiles. The measurement is
committed before band() judges it: R within 1 +- max(0.15, 3 SD_R), else STOP_BALANCE (a scientific balance failure,
not a code defect)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from ..ac.situations import offline_odour
from ..agent.policy import derive_seed
from . import os_encoder
from .spec import LABEL

CALIB = "results/summary/ad_os_kc_calib.json"


def requests(grid, pairs, rep: int, tie_seed: int) -> tuple:
    meta, reqs = [], []
    for i, p in enumerate(pairs):
        for side, opp in ((0, p["o1"]), (1, p["o2"])):
            seed = derive_seed(int(tie_seed), "os-calib", int(rep), i, side)
            variants = [("fly", [offline_odour(grid, m, opp) for m in p["cands"]])]
            variants += [(sp, [os_encoder.odour_for_species(grid, grid.move_info[m][0], sp) for m in p["cands"]])
                         for sp in os_encoder.SPECIES]
            for name, ods in variants:
                meta.append(dict(rep=int(rep), pair=i, side=side, variant=name, true=opp, cands=list(p["cands"])))
                reqs.append((0, ods, seed))
    return meta, reqs


def kc_rows(meta, counts, n_skip: int) -> list:
    return [dict(m, kc=[int(x) for x in (np.asarray(c)[:, int(n_skip):] > 0).sum(1)]) for m, c in zip(meta, counts)]


def ks(a, b) -> float:
    a, b = np.sort(np.asarray(a, float)), np.sort(np.asarray(b, float))
    grid = np.union1d(a, b)
    fa = np.searchsorted(a, grid, side="right") / a.size
    fb = np.searchsorted(b, grid, side="right") / b.size
    return float(np.max(np.abs(fa - fb)))


def _ratio(rows, keep) -> float | None:
    fly = [k for r in rows if r["variant"] == "fly" for k, c in zip(r["kc"], r["cands"]) if keep(r, c)]
    os_ = [k for r in rows if r["variant"] != "fly" for k, c in zip(r["kc"], r["cands"]) if keep(r, c)]
    return float(np.mean(os_) / np.mean(fly)) if fly and os_ and np.mean(fly) > 0 else None


def measure(rows, move_info, species_types) -> dict:
    reps = sorted({r["rep"] for r in rows})
    by_rep = [_ratio([r for r in rows if r["rep"] == k], lambda r, c: True) for k in reps]
    mtypes = sorted({move_info[c][0] for r in rows for c in r["cands"]})
    by_type = {t: _ratio(rows, lambda r, c, t=t: move_info[c][0] == t) for t in mtypes}
    dual = lambda r: "dual" if len(species_types[r["true"]]) == 2 else "single"
    by_dual = {d: _ratio(rows, lambda r, c, d=d: dual(r) == d) for d in ("single", "dual")}
    fly = [k for r in rows if r["variant"] == "fly" for k in r["kc"]]
    os_ = [k for r in rows if r["variant"] != "fly" for k in r["kc"]]
    pct = lambda x: {str(q): float(np.percentile(x, q)) for q in (10, 50, 90)}
    return dict(R_by_rep=by_rep, R=float(np.mean(by_rep)), sd_R=float(np.std(by_rep, ddof=1)) if len(by_rep) > 1 else 0.0,
                by_move_type=by_type, by_true_dual=by_dual, ks=ks(fly, os_), percentiles=dict(fly=pct(fly), os=pct(os_)),
                mean_kc=dict(fly=float(np.mean(fly)), os=float(np.mean(os_))), n_fly=len(fly), n_os=len(os_),
                reps=reps, label=LABEL)


def band(meas: dict, spec) -> dict:
    half = max(spec.kc_band_min, spec.kc_band_sd * float(meas["sd_R"]))
    R = float(meas["R"])
    return dict(status="OK" if abs(R - 1.0) <= half else "STOP_BALANCE", R=R, sd_R=float(meas["sd_R"]),
                half_width=half, lo=1.0 - half, hi=1.0 + half)
```

`scripts/write_ad_os_calib.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Spec AD.5 3 / AD.6 2: the KC-activity calibration of FLY-OS on the naive L_V brain (one LeverFlyPool fly at w0),
written to results/summary/ad_os_kc_calib.json BEFORE any gate reads it (commit it, then run check_ad_smoke.py). Also
records the total-strength check |sum - k| over every (move type, species) OS odour (<= 1e-9; a failure is a code
defect, STOP_SMOKE, judged by the smoke check).

    uv run python scripts/write_ad_os_calib.py [--workers 16]"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from flymon.ad import calib, os_encoder, store
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL, SPEC


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--npz", default="data/malecns.npz")
    ap.add_argument("--inputs", default="results/summary/ac_inputs.json", help="the stage-1 confirmation set (AC.4)")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    from flymon.ac.config import load_lv_config, lv_codebook
    from flymon.ac.spec import SPEC as AC
    from flymon.agent.encode_grid import GridEncoder
    from flymon.brain.circuits import Populations
    from flymon.brain.connectome import Connectome
    from flymon.brain.fly_pool import FlySpec
    from flymon.brain.h4_jobs import type_cells
    from flymon.brain.lv_pool import LeverFlyPool
    model = json.loads(Path("results/summary/ac_model_manifest.json").read_text())
    if model.get("status") != "FROZEN":
        raise SystemExit("refusing: the model manifest is not FROZEN")
    cfg = load_lv_config(float(model["recovery_per_pulse"]))
    cb, _ = lv_codebook()
    conn = Connectome.load(a.npz)
    pops = Populations.from_connectome(conn)
    grid = GridEncoder(pops, cb, SPEC.dual_rule)
    pairs = json.loads(Path(a.inputs).read_text())["confirm"]["pairs"]
    cells = type_cells(conn, [cfg.readout["A"], cfg.readout["P"]])
    na, npp = len(cells[cfg.readout["A"]]), len(cells[cfg.readout["P"]])
    idx = np.concatenate([cells[cfg.readout["A"]], cells[cfg.readout["P"]], np.asarray(pops.kc)])
    rows = []
    with LeverFlyPool(a.npz, cfg.params, [FlySpec()], edit=AC.lever_edit, p_type=AC.p_type, workers=a.workers,
                      timeout_s=7200) as pool:
        for rep in SPEC.calib_reps:
            meta, reqs = calib.requests(grid, pairs, rep, SPEC.tie_seed)
            counts = pool.decide_batch(reqs, cfg.strength, cfg.settle_ms, cfg.read_ms, idx)
            rows += calib.kc_rows(meta, counts, na + npp)
            print(f"rep {rep}: {len(reqs)} requests", flush=True)
    meas = calib.measure(rows, grid.move_info, grid.species_types)
    strength = os_encoder.total_strength_error(grid)
    doc = dict(meas, strength_error=strength, strength_k=int(cb.k), n_pairs=len(pairs), seeds_key="os-calib",
               tie_seed=SPEC.tie_seed, rows=rows, label=LABEL)
    store.write_json(calib.CALIB, doc)
    print(f"R by rep {[round(x, 4) for x in meas['R_by_rep']]}, R {meas['R']:.4f}, SD_R {meas['sd_R']:.4f}, "
          f"KS {meas['ks']:.3f}, strength error {strength:.2e}; wrote {calib.CALIB} (no gate judged here)", flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

### Task 15: 스모크 · 마이크로 스모크 관문 (AD.5 1–6; `STOP_SMOKE` · `STOP_BALANCE` · `STOP_REUSE`)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/smoke.py`, `scripts/check_ad_smoke.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_smoke.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `flymon.ac.smoke`(`SAFETY_TESTS`, `EQUALITY_TESTS`, `schema_errors`, `log_paths`, `kc_ratio_frac`, `resumed`, `pool_response`, `response_gates`), Task 4 `os_encoder`, Task 6 `reuse`, Task 7 `hooks.ADSituationLog`, Task 14 `calib.band`, `flymon.ac.situations`.
- Produces:
  - `SMOKE = "results/summary/ad_smoke.json"`, `STALE_TESTS`, `OS_TESTS`, `SEED_TESTS`, `MANIFEST_TESTS`(pytest id 묶음)
  - `os_shadow_errors(paths, os_flies: set[int]) -> list[str]` — FLY-OS 마리(풀 순번)의 초파리 결정 레코드에 `os`(true, drawn, same_types)가 없거나 틀린 곳
  - `os_draw_record(paths, os_flies) -> dict` — 기록 전용: 실제 상대별 뽑힌 종 수, 뽑힌 타입 = 실제 비율
  - `reproduction_diff(want: list, got: list) -> list[str]` — 1단계 기록과 현재 기록의 v · a · p · kc_active · pick · tie 바이트 비교
  - `same_response(ad: dict, ac: dict) -> bool` — POOL 냄새 응답이 1단계 값과 같음
  - `status(gates: dict, groups: dict) -> str` — `STOP_SMOKE` > `STOP_REUSE` > `STOP_BALANCE` > `OK`
  - `scripts/check_ad_smoke.py --suite-exit FILE [--workers 16]` — 종료 0 OK, 2 STOP_SMOKE, 4 STOP_REUSE, 5 STOP_BALANCE

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_smoke.py`:

```python
"""AD.5's smoke gates: the gate test ids name real tests, the FLY-OS shadow schema, the record-only draw summary, the
stage-1 reproduction comparison, the POOL-response identity with stage 1, and the status precedence."""
import ast
import json
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import smoke

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


def test_gate_test_ids_name_real_tests():
    from flymon.ac import smoke as ac_smoke
    for tid in (smoke.STALE_TESTS + smoke.OS_TESTS + smoke.SEED_TESTS + smoke.MANIFEST_TESTS + ac_smoke.SAFETY_TESTS
                + ac_smoke.EQUALITY_TESTS):
        path, _, name = tid.partition("::")
        assert (ROOT / path).exists(), tid
        if name:
            tree = ast.parse((ROOT / path).read_text())
            names = {n.name for n in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef))}
            assert name in names, tid


def write(p, recs):
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    p.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in recs))


def dec(fly, os=None):
    r = dict(kind="decision", decider="fly", fly=fly, battle_id="XL-f26-b000", turn=1)
    if os is not None:
        r["os"] = os
    return r


def test_os_shadow_errors_and_draw_record(tmp_path):
    good = dict(true="Snorlax", drawn="Chansey", same_types=True)
    write(tmp_path / "logs/fly02.jsonl", [dec(2, good), dec(2, dict(true="Snorlax", drawn="Lapras", same_types=False)),
                                          dict(kind="decision", decider="coach", fly=2)])
    write(tmp_path / "logs/fly00.jsonl", [dec(0)])
    paths = sorted((tmp_path / "logs").glob("fly*.jsonl"))
    assert smoke.os_shadow_errors(paths, {2}) == []
    write(tmp_path / "logs/fly03.jsonl", [dec(3), dec(3, dict(true="Snorlax", drawn="Lapras"))])
    paths = sorted((tmp_path / "logs").glob("fly*.jsonl"))
    assert len(smoke.os_shadow_errors(paths, {2, 3})) == 2
    rec = smoke.os_draw_record(paths, {2})
    assert rec["n"] == 2 and rec["same_types_share"] == 0.5 and rec["by_true"] == {"Snorlax": {"Chansey": 1, "Lapras": 1}}


def test_reproduction_diff_is_byte_level():
    s = [dict(pair=0, side=0, v=[1.0, 2.0], a=[1, 2], p=[3, 4], kc_active=[5, 6], pick=1, tie=False, correct=True)]
    assert smoke.reproduction_diff([dict(fly=0, situations=s)], [dict(fly=0, situations=s)]) == []
    t = [dict(s[0], v=[1.0, 2.0000000000000004])]
    assert smoke.reproduction_diff([dict(fly=0, situations=s)], [dict(fly=0, situations=t)]) == ["fly 0 pair 0 side 0: v"]


def test_same_response():
    a = dict(n_situations=79, n_candidates=200, tie_ratio=0.1, a0_ratio=0.2, p0_ratio=0.0)
    assert smoke.same_response(dict(a), a) and not smoke.same_response(dict(a, tie_ratio=0.11), a)


def test_status_precedence():
    groups = dict(smoke=["a", "b"], reuse=["r"], balance=["k"])
    assert smoke.status(dict(a=True, b=True, r=True, k=True), groups) == "OK"
    assert smoke.status(dict(a=True, b=True, r=True, k=False), groups) == "STOP_BALANCE"
    assert smoke.status(dict(a=True, b=True, r=False, k=False), groups) == "STOP_REUSE"
    assert smoke.status(dict(a=False, b=True, r=False, k=False), groups) == "STOP_SMOKE"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/smoke.py`:

```python
"""AD.5's smoke gates. STOP_SMOKE = a code defect: the full suite; the four safety-constraint tests; worker ==
in-process (KC counts, A, P, V, choice, the weight change after one pulse) and the workers' L_V sha; the stale-pulse
interleaving tests (a)-(d); the OS draw unit tests (key dependence, same-key reproduction, candidate sharing, re-draw
across turns, uniform / independent 16 x 16, global fly numbers, schedule id + battle.turn, byte-identical FLY-encoder
odour) and the global-seed tests; the run-manifest refusal test; the smoke run's log schema, FLY-OS shadow schema,
completion across a stop and a --resume, frozen situation evaluations, one evaluation digest; the OS total strength
|sum - k| <= 1e-9; the naive-L_V POOL odour response (tie / A = 0 / P = 0 shares <= 0.5) equal to stage 1's; the
stage-1 reproduction (flies 0 and 12, point 0: v / a / p / kc_active / pick / tie byte-identical). STOP_REUSE = AD.2
(가) fails. STOP_BALANCE = the committed KC calibration outside 1 +- max(0.15, 3 SD_R). Record only: the drawn species
per true opponent and the same-type share in the smoke battles, the KC > 2 turn share."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

SMOKE = "results/summary/ad_smoke.json"
STALE_TESTS = ("tests/ad/test_ad_stale.py", "tests/battle/test_ad_player.py")
OS_TESTS = ("tests/ad/test_ad_os.py",)
SEED_TESTS = ("tests/ad/test_ad_seeds.py",
              "tests/battle/test_ad_player.py::test_pulse_request_carries_token_generation_and_global_seed",
              "tests/ad/test_ad_hooks.py::test_make_evaluate_uses_os_odours_and_global_seeds")
MANIFEST_TESTS = ("tests/ad/test_ad_run.py::test_resume_refuses_a_changed_run_manifest",
                  "tests/ad/test_ad_runner.py::test_changed_checkpoint_key_is_stop_manifest",
                  "tests/ad/test_ad_run_manifest.py::test_check_detects_changed_budget_code_and_points")
FIELDS = ("v", "a", "p", "kc_active", "pick", "tie")


def _records(paths):
    for p in paths:
        for line in Path(p).read_text().splitlines():
            yield json.loads(line)


def _fly_decisions(paths, os_flies):
    return [r for r in _records(paths) if r.get("kind") == "decision" and r.get("decider") == "fly"
            and r.get("fly") in os_flies]


def os_shadow_errors(paths, os_flies) -> list:
    bad = []
    for r in _fly_decisions(paths, set(os_flies)):
        o = r.get("os")
        if not isinstance(o, dict) or set(o) != {"true", "drawn", "same_types"} or not isinstance(o["same_types"], bool):
            bad.append(f"fly {r.get('fly')} {r.get('battle_id')} turn {r.get('turn')}: os {o!r}")
    return bad


def os_draw_record(paths, os_flies) -> dict:
    by = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(int))
    same = []
    for r in _fly_decisions(paths, set(os_flies)):
        o = r.get("os") or {}
        if "drawn" in o:
            by[o["true"]][o["drawn"]] += 1
            same.append(bool(o["same_types"]))
    return dict(n=len(same), same_types_share=(sum(same) / len(same)) if same else None,
                by_true={t: dict(sorted(d.items())) for t, d in sorted(by.items())})


def reproduction_diff(want: list, got: list) -> list:
    bad = []
    g = {r["fly"]: r for r in got}
    for w in want:
        h = g.get(w["fly"])
        if h is None:
            bad.append(f"fly {w['fly']}: no record")
            continue
        hs = {(s["pair"], s["side"]): s for s in h["situations"]}
        for s in w["situations"]:
            t = hs.get((s["pair"], s["side"]))
            for k in FIELDS:
                if t is None or json.dumps(s[k]) != json.dumps(t[k]):
                    bad.append(f"fly {w['fly']} pair {s['pair']} side {s['side']}: {k}")
    return bad


def same_response(ad: dict, ac: dict) -> bool:
    keys = ("n_situations", "n_candidates", "tie_ratio", "a0_ratio", "p0_ratio")
    return all(ad.get(k) == ac.get(k) for k in keys)


def status(gates: dict, groups: dict) -> str:
    for code, names in (("STOP_SMOKE", groups["smoke"]), ("STOP_REUSE", groups["reuse"]),
                        ("STOP_BALANCE", groups["balance"])):
        if not all(gates[n] for n in names):
            return code
    return "OK"
```

`scripts/check_ad_smoke.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Spec AD.5 / AD.6 2: read the AD smoke run (results/m4-smoke/ad/) and the committed KC calibration, run the gate
test subsets, reproduce stage 1's point-0 records of flies 0 and 12, re-measure the POOL odour response, check the
reuse fields (가), then write results/summary/ad_smoke.json.

    uv run pytest -q -o addopts="" > $L/suite.log 2>&1; echo $? > results/m4-smoke/ad/suite_exit.txt
    uv run python scripts/check_ad_smoke.py --suite-exit results/m4-smoke/ad/suite_exit.txt

Exit 0 OK, 2 STOP_SMOKE, 4 STOP_REUSE, 5 STOP_BALANCE."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

from flymon.ac import smoke as ac_smoke
from flymon.ad import calib, hooks, os_encoder, reuse, smoke, store
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL, SPEC
from flymon.ad.spec import smoke as smoke_spec

EXIT = {"OK": 0, "STOP_SMOKE": 2, "STOP_REUSE": 4, "STOP_BALANCE": 5}


def run_pytest(ids) -> int:
    return subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-m", "pytest", "-q", "-o", "addopts=", *ids]).returncode


def brain_measures(a, pairs) -> dict:
    """Stage-1 reproduction (flies 0 and 12 at point 0, stage-1 seeds) and the naive-L_V POOL odour response."""
    from flymon.ac import confirm, situations
    from flymon.ac.config import load_lv_config
    from flymon.ac.config import lv_codebook
    from flymon.ac.spec import SPEC as AC
    from flymon.agent.encode_grid import GridEncoder
    from flymon.agent.policy import derive_seed
    from flymon.brain.circuits import Populations
    from flymon.brain.connectome import Connectome
    from flymon.brain.fly_pool import FlySpec
    from flymon.brain.h4_jobs import type_cells
    from flymon.brain.lv_pool import LeverFlyPool
    model = json.loads(Path("results/summary/ac_model_manifest.json").read_text())
    cfg = load_lv_config(float(model["recovery_per_pulse"]))
    cb, _ = lv_codebook()
    conn = Connectome.load(a.npz)
    pops = Populations.from_connectome(conn)
    grid = GridEncoder(pops, cb, SPEC.dual_rule)
    cells = type_cells(conn, [cfg.readout["A"], cfg.readout["P"]])
    a_idx, p_idx = cells[cfg.readout["A"]], cells[cfg.readout["P"]]
    idx = np.concatenate([a_idx, p_idx, np.asarray(pops.kc)])
    ods = situations.pair_odours(grid, pairs)
    sits = confirm.situations_of(confirm.candidate_pairs()["pairs"])
    reqs = [(0, [situations.offline_odour(grid, m, opp) for m in cands], derive_seed(AC.tie_seed, "resp", i))
            for i, (_, opp, cands) in enumerate(sits)]
    with LeverFlyPool(a.npz, cfg.params, [FlySpec(), FlySpec()], edit=AC.lever_edit, p_type=AC.p_type,
                      workers=a.workers, timeout_s=7200) as pool:
        got = [dict(situations.evaluate(pool, i, 0, pairs, ods, cfg, a_idx, p_idx, pops.kc, AC.tie_seed, seed_fly=g),
                    fly=g) for i, g in enumerate((0, 12))]
        counts = pool.decide_batch(reqs, cfg.strength, cfg.settle_ms, cfg.read_ms, idx)
    resp = ac_smoke.pool_response(counts, len(a_idx), len(p_idx), cfg.z)
    return dict(reproduced=got, response=resp, strength_error=os_encoder.total_strength_error(grid))


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--suite-exit", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=16)
    ap.add_argument("--npz", default="data/malecns.npz")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    s = smoke_spec()
    root = Path(s.out_root)
    res = json.loads((root / "result.json").read_text()) if (root / "result.json").exists() else {}
    doc = json.loads((root / "inputs.json").read_text()) if (root / "inputs.json").exists() else {}
    sitlog = hooks.ADSituationLog(root / "logs")
    sits = sitlog.records()
    paths = ac_smoke.log_paths(root)
    errs = ac_smoke.schema_errors(paths)
    os_flies = {i for i, (arm, _, _) in enumerate(s.layout()) if arm == "FLYOS"}
    shadow = smoke.os_shadow_errors(paths, os_flies)
    p = reuse.stage1_paths(SPEC)
    ac_inputs = json.loads(Path("results/summary/ac_inputs.json").read_text())
    pairs = ac_inputs["confirm"]["pairs"]
    gates = dict(
        full_suite=Path(a.suite_exit).read_text().strip() == "0",
        safety_tests=run_pytest(ac_smoke.SAFETY_TESTS) == 0,
        worker_equals_in_process=run_pytest(ac_smoke.EQUALITY_TESTS) == 0,
        stale_interleaving_tests=run_pytest(smoke.STALE_TESTS) == 0,
        os_draw_tests=run_pytest(smoke.OS_TESTS) == 0,
        global_fly_tests=run_pytest(smoke.SEED_TESTS) == 0,
        manifest_refusal_tests=run_pytest(smoke.MANIFEST_TESTS) == 0,
        log_schema=bool(paths) and not errs,
        os_shadow_schema=bool(paths) and not shadow,
        complete_after_resume=bool(res.get("complete")) and ac_smoke.resumed(root),
        situation_evals_frozen=bool(sits) and all(r["frozen"] for r in sits),
        eval_digest_shared=bool(res) and res.get("eval_digest") == doc.get("digests", {}).get("eval"))
    try:
        bm = brain_measures(a, pairs)
        want = reuse.coff_records(p["init"], (0, 12))          # point-0 records of stage-1 flies 0 (FLY), 12 (C-off)
        diff = smoke.reproduction_diff(want, bm["reproduced"])
        ac_resp = json.loads(Path(ac_smoke.SMOKE).read_text())["pool_response"]
        gates.update(stage1_reproduction=not diff, os_total_strength=bm["strength_error"] <= SPEC.strength_tol,
                     pool_response_equals_stage1=smoke.same_response(bm["response"], ac_resp),
                     **ac_smoke.response_gates(bm["response"], SPEC))
    except (SystemExit, Exception) as e:
        bm, diff = dict(error=f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"), ["brain measures failed"]
        gates.update(stage1_reproduction=False, os_total_strength=False, pool_response_equals_stage1=False,
                     pool_tie=False, pool_a0=False, pool_p0=False)
    try:
        f = reuse.reuse_fields(ac_inputs["digests"]["confirm"], ac_inputs["digests"]["eval"], SPEC)
        problems = reuse.reuse_problems(f, SPEC)
    except SystemExit as e:
        f, problems = dict(error=str(e)), ["stage-1 files missing"]
    gates["reuse"] = not problems
    cal = json.loads(Path(calib.CALIB).read_text()) if Path(calib.CALIB).exists() else None
    bal = calib.band(cal, SPEC) if cal else dict(status="STOP_BALANCE", error=f"{calib.CALIB} missing")
    gates["kc_balance"] = bal["status"] == "OK"
    groups = dict(smoke=[k for k in gates if k not in ("reuse", "kc_balance")], reuse=["reuse"], balance=["kc_balance"])
    st = smoke.status(gates, groups)
    out = dict(status=st, gates=gates, groups=groups, schema_errors=errs[:20], os_shadow_errors=shadow[:20],
               os_draws=smoke.os_draw_record(paths, os_flies), kc_ratio_gt2=ac_smoke.kc_ratio_frac(paths),
               reproduction_diff=diff[:20], brain={k: v for k, v in bm.items() if k != "reproduced"},
               reuse=f, reuse_problems=problems, kc_band=bal,
               calib_sha256=store.sha256_file(calib.CALIB) if cal else None,
               n_situation_records=len(sits), futility=res.get("futility"), label=LABEL,
               provenance=dict(git=store.git_provenance(), suite_exit=a.suite_exit))
    store.write_json(smoke.SMOKE, out)
    for k, v in gates.items():
        print(f"{'ok ' if v else 'FAILED'} {k}", flush=True)
    print(f"{st}; KC band {bal}; wrote {smoke.SMOKE}", flush=True)
    return EXIT[st]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`brain_measures`의 재현 관문은 1단계와 같은 확인 세트(`results/summary/ac_inputs.json`의 20쌍), 같은 냄새(FLY 인코더), 같은 결정 · 동률 시드(`seed_fly` = 1단계 전역 번호 0 · 12, 키 "sit")를 쓴다. w0는 모든 마리가 같으므로 풀 순번은 결과에 영향을 주지 않는다. `coff_records`는 이름과 달리 주어진 마리들의 0 시점 기록을 돌려주므로 마리 0(FLY)에도 쓸 수 있다.

Run: `uv run pytest -q -o addopts="" tests/ad/test_ad_smoke.py`
Expected: PASS

git commit -m "feat(ad): smoke gates — stale/OS/seed/manifest tests, shadow schema, stage-1 reproduction, reuse, KC band"

### Task 16: 원자료 sha256 목록과 git 밖 보관본 (AD.6 5)

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/raw.py`, `scripts/write_ad_raw_manifest.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_raw.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `store.write_json`, `sha256_file`.
- Produces:
  - `RAW = "results/summary/ad_raw_manifest.json"`
  - `listing(root, extra=()) -> list[dict(path, sha256, bytes)]`(정렬된 상대 경로; `root` 아래 모든 파일 + `extra` 파일)
  - `archive(entries, dest) -> dict(path, sha256, bytes)`(tar.gz)
  - `verify(doc) -> list[str]` — 목록의 sha256과 지금 파일이 다른(또는 없는) 경로
  - `scripts/write_ad_raw_manifest.py [--root results/m4/ad] [--extra LOG ...] [--archive-dir ~/flymon-archive]` — 분석 전에 돌린다

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_raw.py`:

```python
"""AD.6 5: before the analysis, every log / checkpoint / wall_clock.json under results/m4/ad/ and the run load log are
listed with their sha256 (committed with the result) and packed into a tar.gz outside git; verify() names any file that
changed or vanished, and an archive unpacked into results/m4/ad/ verifies."""
import tarfile

from flymon.ad import raw


def tree(root):
    (root / "logs").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "logs/fly00.jsonl").write_text('{"a": 1}\n')
    (root / "checkpoints/learn").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "checkpoints/learn/state_000001.npz").write_bytes(b"\x00\x01")
    (root / "wall_clock.json").write_text("{}")


def test_listing_archive_and_verify(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.chdir(tmp_path)
    root = tmp_path / "results/m4/ad"
    tree(root)
    log = tmp_path / "load.log"
    log.write_text("x\n")
    entries = raw.listing("results/m4/ad", extra=["load.log"])
    assert [e["path"] for e in entries] == ["load.log", "results/m4/ad/checkpoints/learn/state_000001.npz",
                                           "results/m4/ad/logs/fly00.jsonl", "results/m4/ad/wall_clock.json"]
    arc = raw.archive(entries, tmp_path / "arc/ad-raw.tar.gz")
    doc = dict(files=entries, archive=arc)
    assert raw.verify(doc) == []
    (root / "logs/fly00.jsonl").write_text('{"a": 2}\n')
    (root / "wall_clock.json").unlink()
    assert raw.verify(doc) == ["results/m4/ad/logs/fly00.jsonl", "results/m4/ad/wall_clock.json"]
    with tarfile.open(arc["path"]) as t:                 # recovery: unpack the archive, then verify the list
        t.extractall(tmp_path, filter="data")
    assert raw.verify(doc) == []
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 실패를 확인한다**

Run: `uv run pytest -q -o addopts="" tests/ad/test_ad_raw.py`
Expected: FAIL — `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'flymon.ad.raw'`

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/raw.py`:

```python
"""AD.6 5: the raw-data list. Before the analysis every file under results/m4/ad/ (logs, checkpoints, wall_clock.json,
result.json, futility.json, the books, batch_times.json) and the run load log are listed with their sha256 and size,
and packed into ~/flymon-archive/ad-raw-<date>.tar.gz (outside git; its sha256 is in the list). The list
(results/summary/ad_raw_manifest.json) goes into the result commit. Recovery: unpack the archive at the repository root
(paths are stored relative to it) and verify() the list."""
from __future__ import annotations

import tarfile
from pathlib import Path

from .store import sha256_file

RAW = "results/summary/ad_raw_manifest.json"


def listing(root, extra=()) -> list:
    files = [p for p in Path(root).rglob("*") if p.is_file()] + [Path(x) for x in extra]
    return sorted((dict(path=p.as_posix(), sha256=sha256_file(p), bytes=p.stat().st_size) for p in files),
                  key=lambda e: e["path"])


def archive(entries, dest) -> dict:
    dest = Path(dest).expanduser()
    dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tarfile.open(dest, "w:gz") as t:
        for e in entries:
            t.add(e["path"], arcname=e["path"])
    return dict(path=str(dest), sha256=sha256_file(dest), bytes=dest.stat().st_size)


def verify(doc: dict) -> list:
    bad = []
    for e in doc["files"]:
        p = Path(e["path"])
        if not p.is_file() or sha256_file(p) != e["sha256"]:
            bad.append(e["path"])
    return bad
```

`scripts/write_ad_raw_manifest.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Spec AD.6 5: list every raw file of the AD run with its sha256 and archive them outside git, BEFORE the analysis.

    uv run python scripts/write_ad_raw_manifest.py --extra .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs/ad_run_load.log

Writes results/summary/ad_raw_manifest.json and ~/flymon-archive/ad-raw-<YYYYMMDD>.tar.gz."""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime as dt
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ad import raw, store
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--root", default="results/m4/ad")
    ap.add_argument("--extra", nargs="*", default=[])
    ap.add_argument("--archive-dir", default="~/flymon-archive")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if not (Path(a.root) / "result.json").exists():
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {a.root}/result.json does not exist (the run is not finished or stopped)")
    entries = raw.listing(a.root, a.extra)
    day = dt.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d")
    arc = raw.archive(entries, Path(a.archive_dir).expanduser() / f"ad-raw-{day}.tar.gz")
    doc = dict(files=entries, archive=arc, root=a.root, n_files=len(entries),
               restore="tar -xzf <archive> -C <repository root>; then flymon.ad.raw.verify(this list)", label=LABEL)
    store.write_json(raw.RAW, doc)
    print(f"listed {len(entries)} files ({sum(e['bytes'] for e in entries)} bytes); archive {arc['path']} "
          f"sha256 {arc['sha256'][:12]}; wrote {raw.RAW}", flush=True)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```
