### Task 17: AD.3 대조 — 1차 두 개, 기준 행, 차이의 차이, 보조, 짝지은 OS, 민감도, 기준 2 · 3

**Files:**
- Create: `flymon/ad/stats.py`, `flymon/ad/analysis.py`
- Test: `tests/ad/test_ad_analysis.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `flymon.ac.boot`(`two_stage_diff`, `meets`, `fly_units`, `arm_mean`, `_draw`), `flymon.ac.diag.crossed_boot`, `flymon.ac.analysis`(`c2_units`, `c3_units`), Task 8 `validity.records`.
- Produces:
  - `flymon.ad.stats.two_stage_mean(units: dict, draws, seed) -> dict(mean, lo, hi, n, draws, seed)` — 한 팔(마리 → 쌍)의 2단계 백분위 부트스트랩 평균
  - `flymon.ad.analysis`: `switch_units(sits, arm, point, *, set_="confirm", mode="own", exclude=()) -> dict[g, list[int]]`, `did_units(sits, arm, point, exclude=()) -> dict`, `stage1_units(recs) -> dict`, `contrast(name, a, b, ua, ub, spec, *, point=None, rule=False, crossed=True) -> dict(name, a, b, point, n_a, n_b, min_a, min_b, status, diff, lo, hi, crossed{lo, hi})`, `paired_os(sits, spec, point) -> dict`, `situation_readings(sits, spec, *, final, new_points, coff, fly1, retried) -> dict(primary, reference, did, aux, new_set, paired_os, sensitivity)`, `eval_readings(result, eval_records, nobrain_rows, spec) -> list[dict]`
  - status 값: "충족" · "미충족"(1차 두 개와 바닥 접촉 병기 행만), "기술", "측정 불성립"

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_analysis.py`:

```python
"""AD.3's contrasts on synthetic records: the decision rule only on the two primaries, the crossed CI everywhere on the
situation contrasts, point-wise minimum valid flies, the difference of differences within fly, the stage-1 C-off and FLY
columns, the paired OS evaluation, the retry sensitivity rows, and criteria 2-3 unmeasured without an evaluation block."""
import dataclasses

import pytest

from flymon.ad import analysis as an
from flymon.ad import stats
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC

S = dataclasses.replace(SPEC, boot_draws=400)


def sw(n_on):
    return [1] * n_on + [0] * (20 - n_on)


def rec(g, point, n_on, arm=None, set_="confirm", mode="own"):
    return dict(fly=g, point=point, arm=arm or ("FLYL" if g < 36 else "FLYOS"), set=set_, mode=mode, frozen=True,
                switched=sw(n_on))


def arms(point, l_on, os_on, n_l=12, n_os=12, **kw):
    return ([rec(24 + i, point, l_on, **kw) for i in range(n_l)] + [rec(36 + i, point, os_on, **kw) for i in range(n_os)])


def test_two_stage_mean():
    m = stats.two_stage_mean({1: [1, 1, 0, 0], 2: [1, 0, 0, 0]}, 300, 304)
    assert m["mean"] == pytest.approx(0.375) and m["lo"] <= m["mean"] <= m["hi"] and m["n"] == 2


def test_primary_rule_and_crossed_ci():
    sits = arms(0, 4, 4) + arms(40, 6, 4) + arms(120, 12, 2)
    r = an.situation_readings(sits, S, final=120, new_points=[], coff=[], fly1=[], retried=[])
    p1, p2 = r["primary"]
    assert (p1["point"], p1["status"], p1["diff"]) == (120, "충족", pytest.approx(0.5))
    assert p1["crossed"]["lo"] <= p1["diff"] <= p1["crossed"]["hi"]
    assert (p2["point"], p2["status"]) == (40, "미충족")              # 0.1 < 0.15
    assert r["reference"]["status"] == "기술" and r["reference"]["diff"] == 0.0
    assert [d["point"] for d in r["did"]] == [40, 120] and all(d["status"] == "기술" for d in r["did"])


def test_point_wise_minimum_makes_an_item_unmeasured():
    sits = arms(0, 4, 4) + arms(40, 6, 4) + arms(120, 12, 2, n_os=8)
    p1, p2 = an.situation_readings(sits, S, final=120, new_points=[], coff=[], fly1=[], retried=[])["primary"]
    assert p1["status"] == "측정 불성립" and (p1["n_b"], p1["min_b"]) == (8, 9) and p2["status"] == "미충족"


def test_did_units_are_within_fly():
    sits = [rec(24, 0, 2), rec(24, 40, 5), rec(25, 40, 9)]                 # fly 25 has no point-0 record
    u = an.did_units(sits, "FLYL", 40)
    assert list(u) == [24] and sum(u[24]) == 3


def test_aux_uses_the_stage1_columns():
    coff = [dict(fly=12 + i, point=0, arm="COFF", frozen=True, switched=sw(1)) for i in range(6)]
    fly1 = [dict(fly=i, point=40, arm="FLY", frozen=True, switched=sw(5)) for i in range(12)]
    sits = arms(0, 4, 4) + arms(40, 6, 4)
    r = an.situation_readings(sits, S, final=40, new_points=[], coff=coff, fly1=fly1, retried=[])
    names = [a["name"] for a in r["aux"]]
    assert names == ["FLY-L(40) − C-off", "FLY-L(40) − 1단계 FLY(40)"]
    assert r["aux"][0]["diff"] == pytest.approx(0.25) and r["aux"][0]["n_b"] == 6 and r["aux"][0]["status"] == "기술"
    assert r["aux"][1]["diff"] == pytest.approx(0.05)
    assert r["primary"][0]["point"] == r["primary"][1]["point"] == 40          # STOP_FUTILE: 1차 1 = 1차 2


def test_new_set_and_paired_os():
    sits = (arms(0, 4, 4) + arms(40, 6, 4) + arms(0, 3, 3, set_="new") + arms(40, 8, 3, set_="new")
            + [rec(24 + i, 40, 2, mode="os_paired") for i in range(12)] + [rec(24 + i, 0, 4, mode="os_paired")
                                                                           for i in range(12)])
    r = an.situation_readings(sits, S, final=40, new_points=[0, 40], coff=[], fly1=[], retried=[])
    assert [c["point"] for c in r["new_set"]] == [0, 40] and r["new_set"][1]["diff"] == pytest.approx(0.25)
    po = {c["point"]: c for c in r["paired_os"]}
    assert set(po) == {0, 40} and po[40]["diff"] == pytest.approx(0.2) and po[0]["diff"] == pytest.approx(0.0)
    assert po[40]["status"] == "기술" and po[40]["n_a"] == 12 and "crossed" in po[40]


def test_sensitivity_excludes_retried_flies():
    sits = arms(0, 4, 4) + arms(40, 6, 4) + arms(120, 12, 2)
    r = an.situation_readings(sits, S, final=120, new_points=[], coff=[], fly1=[], retried=[24, 25, 26, 27])
    assert [c["status"] for c in r["sensitivity"]] == ["측정 불성립", "측정 불성립"]   # 8 FLY-L flies < 9
    r = an.situation_readings(sits, S, final=120, new_points=[], coff=[], fly1=[], retried=[24])
    assert r["sensitivity"][0]["n_a"] == 11 and r["sensitivity"][0]["status"] == "기술"


def test_budget_stop_marks_criteria_2_3_unmeasured():
    out = an.eval_readings({"status": "STOP_BUDGET", "per_fly": None}, [], [], S)
    assert [c["criterion"] for c in out] == [2, 3, 3, 3]
    assert all(c["status"] == "측정 불성립" and "평가 블록" in c["reason"] for c in out)


def test_eval_readings_on_complete_rows():
    rows = [dict(fly=i, arm="FLYL" if i < 12 else "FLYOS", k=i % 12, invalid=False,
                 eval_battles=[dict(won=(b < (10 if i < 12 else 5))) for b in range(20)]) for i in range(24)]
    nob = [dict(fly=k, arm=arm, k=k, invalid=False, eval_battles=[dict(won=b < 4) for b in range(20)])
           for arm in ("RND", "MAX") for k in range(16)]
    ev = [dict(kind="decision", decider="fly", fly=i, candidates=["a", "b"], multipliers=[2.0, 0.5],
               chosen="a" if i < 12 else "b") for i in range(24)]
    out = {(c["criterion"], c["a"], c["b"]): c for c in an.eval_readings({"status": "COMPLETE", "per_fly": rows}, ev,
                                                                          nob, S)}
    assert out[(2, "FLYL", "FLYOS")]["diff"] == pytest.approx(1.0) and out[(2, "FLYL", "FLYOS")]["status"] == "기술"
    assert out[(3, "FLYL", "FLYOS")]["diff"] == pytest.approx(0.25)
    assert out[(3, "FLYL", "RND")]["diff"] == pytest.approx(0.3) and out[(3, "MAX", "FLYL")]["diff"] == pytest.approx(-0.3)
    assert "crossed" not in out[(3, "FLYL", "RND")]
```

`flymon/ad/stats.py`:

```python
"""A one-arm version of AC.5's two-stage percentile bootstrap (flies with replacement, then each drawn fly's units with
replacement): the paired OS evaluation's within-fly differences (AD.3). Same canonical fly order as boot.two_stage_diff."""
from __future__ import annotations

import numpy as np

from ..ac import boot


def two_stage_mean(units: dict, draws: int, seed: int) -> dict:
    flies = boot.fly_units(units)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    d = np.array([boot._draw(rng, flies) for _ in range(int(draws))])
    lo, hi = np.percentile(d, [2.5, 97.5])
    return dict(mean=boot.arm_mean(flies), lo=float(lo), hi=float(hi), n=len(flies), draws=int(draws), seed=int(seed))
```

`flymon/ad/analysis.py`:

```python
"""AD.3's contrasts (spec AD.3, AD.7). Nothing here is PASS / FAIL.
- Units: per fly the 20 confirmation pairs (switch = both sides picked the best move, AC.5); a fly enters a point iff its
  record at that point passed the frozen check (validity.records - point-wise validity).
- The decision rule (boot.two_stage_diff, B 10 000, seed 304; "충족" iff diff >= 0.15 and lower end > 0) is applied
  only to the two primaries (FLY-L - FLY-OS at the final point and at 40) and to the floor-contact companion row; every
  other row is "기술". Every situation contrast also carries diag.crossed_boot's CI (one pair draw shared by both
  arms; descriptive, never changes a status).
- Reference row FLY-L(0) - FLY-OS(0); difference of differences per fly = switch(t) - switch(0) pair by pair at t = 40
  and the final point; FLY-L - C-off (stage-1 point-0 records, one column used at both points); FLY-L(40) - stage-1
  FLY(40); the new 20 pairs at 0 / 40 / final; the paired OS evaluation FLY-L own - FLY-L on OS odours (within fly,
  stats.two_stage_mean, crossed CI paired); the sensitivity rows = the two primaries without the flies that needed a
  retry in learning.
- Minimum valid flies per item: FLY-L 9, FLY-OS 9, C-off 4, stage-1 FLY 9, RND / MAX 12; fewer is "측정 불성립".
- Criteria 2-3 (descriptive): FLY-L - FLY-OS information-turn type match and win rate in the evaluation battles; FLY-L -
  RND and MAX - FLY-L against stage 1's RND / MAX (same evaluation-schedule digest). No evaluation block (a budget stop)
  -> "측정 불성립" for these only."""
from __future__ import annotations

from ..ac import boot, diag
from ..ac.analysis import c2_units, c3_units
from . import stats, validity


def switch_units(sits, arm: str, point, *, set_: str = "confirm", mode: str = "own", exclude=()) -> dict:
    if point is None:
        return {}
    ex = set(exclude)
    recs = validity.records(sits, point=point, arm=arm, set_=set_, mode=mode)
    return {g: [int(x) for x in r["switched"]] for g, r in recs.items() if g not in ex}


def did_units(sits, arm: str, point, exclude=()) -> dict:
    u0, ut = switch_units(sits, arm, 0, exclude=exclude), switch_units(sits, arm, point, exclude=exclude)
    return {g: [b - a for a, b in zip(u0[g], ut[g])] for g in ut if g in u0}


def stage1_units(recs) -> dict:
    return {r["fly"]: [int(x) for x in r["switched"]] for r in recs if r.get("frozen") is True}


def contrast(name: str, a: str, b: str, ua: dict, ub: dict, spec, *, point=None, rule: bool = False,
             crossed: bool = True) -> dict:
    mins = spec.min_valid_of()
    base = dict(name=name, a=a, b=b, point=point, n_a=len(ua), n_b=len(ub), min_a=mins[a], min_b=mins[b])
    if not ua or not ub or len(ua) < mins[a] or len(ub) < mins[b]:
        return dict(base, status="측정 불성립")
    res = boot.two_stage_diff(ua, ub, spec.boot_draws, spec.boot_seed)
    out = dict(base, diff=res["diff"], lo=res["lo"], hi=res["hi"])
    if crossed:
        cr = diag.crossed_boot(ua, ub, spec.boot_draws, spec.boot_seed)
        out["crossed"] = dict(lo=cr["lo"], hi=cr["hi"])
    out["status"] = ("충족" if boot.meets(res, spec.min_effect) else "미충족") if rule else "기술"
    return out


def paired_os(sits, spec, point: int) -> dict:
    own = switch_units(sits, "FLYL", point)
    os_ = switch_units(sits, "FLYL", point, mode="os_paired")
    common = sorted(set(own) & set(os_))
    n, m = len(common), spec.min_valid_of()["FLYL"]
    base = dict(name=f"짝지은 OS 평가 FLY-L({point}) − FLY-L-OS평가({point})", a="FLYL", b="FLYL_OS", point=point,
                n_a=n, n_b=n, min_a=m, min_b=m)
    if n < m:
        return dict(base, status="측정 불성립")
    own, os_ = {g: own[g] for g in common}, {g: os_[g] for g in common}
    res = stats.two_stage_mean({g: [x - y for x, y in zip(own[g], os_[g])] for g in common}, spec.boot_draws,
                               spec.boot_seed)
    cr = diag.crossed_boot(own, os_, spec.boot_draws, spec.boot_seed, paired=True)
    return dict(base, status="기술", diff=res["mean"], lo=res["lo"], hi=res["hi"], crossed=dict(lo=cr["lo"], hi=cr["hi"]))


def situation_readings(sits, spec, *, final, new_points, coff, fly1, retried) -> dict:
    fp = spec.futility_point
    u = lambda arm, t, **kw: switch_units(sits, arm, t, **kw)
    prim = [contrast("1차 1", "FLYL", "FLYOS", u("FLYL", final), u("FLYOS", final), spec, point=final, rule=True),
            contrast("1차 2", "FLYL", "FLYOS", u("FLYL", fp), u("FLYOS", fp), spec, point=fp, rule=True)]
    ref = contrast("기준 행 FLY-L(0) − FLY-OS(0)", "FLYL", "FLYOS", u("FLYL", 0), u("FLYOS", 0), spec, point=0)
    pts = [p for p in dict.fromkeys((fp, final)) if p is not None]
    did = [contrast(f"차이의 차이 {t}", "FLYL", "FLYOS", did_units(sits, "FLYL", t), did_units(sits, "FLYOS", t), spec,
                    point=t) for t in pts]
    cu, fu = stage1_units(coff), stage1_units(fly1)
    aux = [contrast(f"FLY-L({t}) − C-off", "FLYL", "COFF", u("FLYL", t), cu, spec, point=t) for t in pts]
    aux.append(contrast(f"FLY-L({fp}) − 1단계 FLY({fp})", "FLYL", "FLY1", u("FLYL", fp), fu, spec, point=fp))
    new = [contrast(f"새 20쌍 FLY-L({t}) − FLY-OS({t})", "FLYL", "FLYOS", u("FLYL", t, set_="new"),
                    u("FLYOS", t, set_="new"), spec, point=t) for t in new_points]
    paired = [paired_os(sits, spec, t) for t in sorted({int(r["point"]) for r in sits if r.get("mode") == "os_paired"})]
    ex = set(retried)
    sens = [contrast(f"민감도(재시도 마리 제외) {c['name']}", "FLYL", "FLYOS", u("FLYL", c["point"], exclude=ex),
                     u("FLYOS", c["point"], exclude=ex), spec, point=c["point"]) for c in prim]
    return dict(primary=prim, reference=ref, did=did, aux=aux, new_set=new, paired_os=paired, sensitivity=sens)


EVAL_ITEMS = ((2, "FLYL", "FLYOS"), (3, "FLYL", "FLYOS"), (3, "FLYL", "RND"), (3, "MAX", "FLYL"))
EVAL_TEXT = {2: "정보 있는 턴 일치율", 3: "승률"}


def eval_readings(result: dict, eval_records, nobrain_rows, spec) -> list:
    rows = result.get("per_fly") if result.get("status") == "COMPLETE" else None
    name = lambda c, a, b: f"평가 배틀 기준 {c}({EVAL_TEXT[c]}) {a} − {b}"
    if not rows:
        return [dict(criterion=c, name=name(c, a, b), a=a, b=b, status="측정 불성립",
                     reason="평가 블록 없음(실행 중 예산 소진 또는 미완료)") for c, a, b in EVAL_ITEMS]
    u2 = c2_units(eval_records, rows)
    u3 = c3_units(list(rows) + list(nobrain_rows))
    out = []
    for c, a, b in EVAL_ITEMS:
        units = u2 if c == 2 else u3
        out.append(dict(contrast(name(c, a, b), a, b, units.get(a, {}), units.get(b, {}), spec, crossed=False),
                        criterion=c))
    return out
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 실패하는 테스트를 쓴다**

`tests/ad/test_ad_learncheck.py`:

```python
"""AD.3's learning-check records (record only): right-direction share, centred V by multiplier class, the switch curve,
the self-noise null, reward / punishment totals per interval, cumulative pulses, floor contact and its pre-registered
reading, the power-vs-multiplier conflict share, the changed-weight fraction."""
import pytest

from flymon.ad import learncheck as lc
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC

PAIRS = [dict(cands=["m0", "m1"], best1="m0", best2="m1", mults1=[2.0, 0.5], mults2=[0.5, 2.0]) for _ in range(2)]
MI = {"m0": ("WATER", 90), "m1": ("FIRE", 40)}


def rec(g, point, pos, floor=None, frac=0.0):
    sits = []
    for i, p in enumerate(pos):
        sits.append(dict(pair=i, side=0, v=[1.0, 0.0] if p else [0.0, 1.0], pick=0 if p else 1))
        sits.append(dict(pair=i, side=1, v=[0.0, 1.0] if p else [1.0, 0.0], pick=1 if p else 0))
    return dict(fly=g, point=point, arm="FLYL" if g < 36 else "FLYOS", set="confirm", mode="own", frozen=True,
                situations=sits, switched=[int(p) for p in pos], rate=sum(pos) / len(pos), floor_contact=floor,
                weights=dict(frac_changed=frac))


SITS = [rec(24, 0, [0, 0]), rec(24, 20, [1, 0], 0.2, 0.1), rec(36, 0, [0, 0]), rec(36, 20, [0, 0], 0.7, 0.3)]


def test_curves():
    assert lc.right_share(SITS, PAIRS, (0, 20))["FLYL"] == {0: dict(share=0.0, n=1), 20: dict(share=0.5, n=1)}
    sc = lc.switch_curve(SITS, (0, 20))
    assert sc["FLYL"][20] == dict(mean=0.5, per_fly={24: 0.5}) and sc["FLYOS"][20]["mean"] == 0.0
    assert lc.null_curve(SITS, PAIRS, (20,))["FLYOS"][20] == 0.0
    assert lc.floor_curve(SITS, (20,)) == {"FLYL": {20: 0.2}, "FLYOS": {20: 0.7}}
    assert lc.frac_changed(SITS, (20,))["FLYOS"][20] == pytest.approx(0.3)
    cv = lc.centred_v(SITS, PAIRS, (20,))["FLYL"][20]
    assert cv["super"]["n"] == 4 and cv["not_very"]["n"] == 4
    assert cv["super"]["mean"] == pytest.approx(0.0) and cv["not_very"]["mean"] == pytest.approx(0.0)   # one pair each way
    cs = lc.conflict_share(SITS, PAIRS, MI, (20,))["FLYL"][20]
    assert cs["n"] == 2 and cs["mult_share"] == pytest.approx(0.5)      # power picks m0; side 1's multiplier picks m1


def test_floor_reading():
    r = lc.floor_reading({"FLYL": {0: None, 20: 0.2, 40: 0.6}, "FLYOS": {0: None, 20: 0.4, 40: 0.3}}, 0.5)
    assert r == dict(values={0: None, 20: 0.4, 40: 0.6}, first_over=40, last_ok=20, read=0.5)
    assert lc.floor_reading({"FLYL": {20: 0.1}, "FLYOS": {20: 0.2}}, 0.5)["first_over"] is None


def test_pulse_tables():
    lay = SPEC.layout()
    logs = {0: [dict(kind="reinforce", battle_id="DL-f24-b003", pulses=[["PAM08", 400.0], ["PPL105", 200.0]]),
                dict(kind="reinforce", battle_id="DL-f24-b025", pulses=[["PAM08", 100.0]]),
                dict(kind="decision", battle_id="DL-f24-b025")],
            12: [dict(kind="reinforce", battle_id="DL-f36-b030", pulses=[])]}
    t = lc.pulse_tables(logs, lay, (0, 20, 40))
    assert t["by_fly"][24]["0-20"] == dict(n_reward=1, ms_reward=400.0, n_punish=1, ms_punish=200.0)
    assert t["by_fly"][24]["20-40"] == dict(n_reward=1, ms_reward=100.0, n_punish=0, ms_punish=0.0)
    assert t["by_arm"]["FLYL"]["0-20"]["ms_reward"] == 400.0 and t["by_arm"]["FLYOS"]["20-40"]["n_reward"] == 0
    assert t["cumulative"]["FLYL"][40] == 3.0 and t["cumulative"]["FLYL"][20] == 2.0 and t["cumulative"]["FLYOS"][40] == 0.0
```

`tests/ad/test_ad_report.py`:

```python
"""AD.3's sentence frames and the assembled result: the primary sentence with the chance-switch null, the crossed CI,
the reference row, the valid flies and later drop-outs, the KC tail and the label; the summary by primary 1 with the
40-battle note and the final-point reason; STOP_FUTILE; the learning-check sentence; notes on C-off / descriptive rows;
the forbidden-word check."""
import pytest

from flymon.ad import report
from flymon.ad.spec import LABEL

C = dict(name="1차 1", a="FLYL", b="FLYOS", point=120, n_a=12, n_b=11, min_a=9, min_b=9, status="충족", diff=0.2,
         lo=0.05, hi=0.35, crossed=dict(lo=-0.01, hi=0.41))
REF = dict(name="기준 행", a="FLYL", b="FLYOS", point=0, status="기술", diff=-0.03, lo=-0.1, hi=0.04,
           crossed=dict(lo=-0.15, hi=0.09))


def test_primary_sentence():
    s = report.primary_sentence(C, REF, 0.183, 1, 0.31, notes=("예산 소진 — 최종 판독 시점 120",))
    assert s.startswith("FLY-L − FLY-OS(우연 전환 대조, 120배틀 시점)에서 4.3 기준 1: 0.200 [95% CI 0.050, 0.350] — 탐색 기준 충족.")
    assert "FLY-OS 쪽은 우연 전환이다(자기 잡음 기대치 Σq·q 0.183)." in s
    assert "확인 세트 20쌍에 한함(교차 부트스트랩 95% CI -0.010, 0.410, 기술)." in s
    assert "기준 행 FLY-L(0) − FLY-OS(0): -0.030 [95% CI -0.100, 0.040]." in s
    assert "유효 마리 FLY-L 12 · FLY-OS 11(120배틀 시점, 이후 탈락 1)." in s and "예산 소진 — 최종 판독 시점 120" in s
    assert s.endswith(f"{LABEL} 같은 턴 후보 KC 비율 > 2 턴 비율(FLY-L): 0.310.")
    u = report.primary_sentence(dict(C, status="측정 불성립", n_b=8), REF, None, 0, None)
    assert "유효 마리 FLY-L 12/9 · FLY-OS 8/9 — 탐색 기준 측정 불성립." in u and "기록 없음" in u


def test_row_sentences_and_notes():
    c = dict(name="FLY-L(40) − C-off", a="FLYL", b="COFF", point=40, n_a=12, n_b=6, min_a=9, min_b=4, status="기술",
             diff=0.1, lo=0.0, hi=0.2, crossed=dict(lo=-0.05, hi=0.25))
    s = report.row_sentence(c)
    assert report.C_OFF_NOTE in s and report.MULTI_NOTE in s and s.endswith(LABEL)
    assert report.unmeasured_sentence(4) == f"4.3 기준 4: — 탐색 기준 미측정(AC.2 2단계 팔 보류, 부록 AD). {LABEL}"


def test_summary_sentence():
    p2 = dict(C, point=40, status="미충족")
    s = report.summary_sentence(C, p2, 120, "complete")
    assert s.startswith("상대 타입을 실제 상대와 무관하게 섞고 총 세기를 같게 둔 우연 전환 대조(FLY-OS)와 비교해, 배틀로 120배틀")
    assert "확인 세트 20쌍은 1단계에서 이미 쓰였고, 그 상황은 학습 중에도 나왔을 수 있다(AC.4 · AD.0 3)." in s
    assert "40배틀 시점 대조는 미충족로 최종 시점과 다르다 — 둘을 합쳐 읽지 않는다." in s and s.endswith(LABEL)
    s = report.summary_sentence(dict(C, status="미충족"), dict(p2, status="미충족"), 80, "fallback")
    assert s.startswith("배틀로 80배틀 학습한 마리의 상성 조건부 선택이 상대 타입을 섞은 우연 전환 대조(FLY-OS)와 비교해 탐색 기준에 못 미쳤다")
    assert "최종 판독 시점은 80배틀이다(대체 80)." in s and "합쳐 읽지" not in s
    s = report.summary_sentence(dict(C, status="측정 불성립"), p2, 60, "STOP_BUDGET")
    assert s.startswith("AD 1차 대조는 측정 불성립이다") and "(예산 소진의 마지막 공통 시점)" in s


def test_futility_and_learncheck_sentences():
    f = dict(status="STOP_FUTILE", share_at_point=0.31, share_at_0=0.35, point=40)
    assert report.futility_sentence(f) == ("STOP_FUTILE: 40배틀 시점 FLY-L 맞는 방향 쌍 비율 0.310가 0 시점 0.350보다 크지 "
                                           f"않아 학습을 40배틀에서 멈췄다(판정 아님). 판독은 40배틀 시점이다. {LABEL}")
    lcd = dict(right_share={"FLYL": {0: dict(share=0.2), 20: dict(share=0.3), 40: dict(share=0.4)}},
               switch_curve={"FLYL": {0: dict(mean=0.1), 40: dict(mean=0.2)}}, null={"FLYOS": {40: 0.19}})
    s = report.learncheck_sentence("FLYL", lcd, (0, 20, 40), 40)
    assert s == ("FLY-L 학습 확인(기록, 판정 아님): 맞는 방향 쌍 비율 0 0.200 → 20 0.300 → 40 0.400; 전환율 0 0.100 → 40 0.200; "
                 f"FLY-OS 잡음 기대치 Σq·q 40 0.190. {LABEL}")


def test_check_refuses_forbidden_words():
    report.check(["탐색 기준 충족."])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        report.check(["FLY PASS"])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        report.check(["학습 안 됨"])
```

Run: `uv run pytest -q -o addopts="" tests/ad/test_ad_learncheck.py tests/ad/test_ad_report.py`
Expected: FAIL — `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'flymon.ad.learncheck'`

- [ ] **Step 3: 구현한다**

`flymon/ad/learncheck.py`:

```python
"""AD.3's learning-check records (record only; the only gate is the 40-battle futility check, Task 8), per arm (FLY-L,
FLY-OS) and point, over the valid (confirm, own) records: the right-direction share (diag.best_contrast > 0), centred V
by multiplier class (with n), the switch curve (arm mean and per fly), the self-noise null sum q*q (diag.empirical_null;
outside point 0 a pooled approximation, AC.11), floor contact and the cumulative pulses, reward / punishment totals per
interval between points (count and ms, per arm and per fly; AD.0 6: the same rule, different realised rewards), the
multiplier share where the power and multiplier picks conflict, and the changed plastic-edge fraction. floor_reading:
the larger arm mean per point; the first point above 0.5 and the last point at or below it (AD.3's pre-registered
reading; not a STOP)."""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict

import numpy as np

from ..ac import diag
from . import validity
from .inputs import battle_index
from .spec import LABEL

ARMS = ("FLYL", "FLYOS")


def _mean(xs):
    xs = [float(x) for x in xs if x is not None]
    return float(np.mean(xs)) if xs else None


def _recs(sits, arm, p):
    return validity.records(sits, point=p, arm=arm)


def right_share(sits, pairs, points) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for arm in ARMS:
        out[arm] = {}
        for p in points:
            r = _recs(sits, arm, p)
            out[arm][int(p)] = dict(share=validity.share(r, pairs), n=len(r))
    return out


def centred_v(sits, pairs, points) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for arm in ARMS:
        out[arm] = {}
        for p in points:
            acc = defaultdict(list)
            for r in _recs(sits, arm, p).values():
                for s in r["situations"]:
                    pr = pairs[s["pair"]]
                    mults = pr["mults1"] if s["side"] == 0 else pr["mults2"]
                    for v, m in zip(diag.centred(s["v"]), mults):
                        acc[diag.mult_class(m)].append(float(v))
            out[arm][int(p)] = {k: dict(mean=float(np.mean(v)), n=len(v)) for k, v in sorted(acc.items())}
    return out


def switch_curve(sits, points) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for arm in ARMS:
        out[arm] = {}
        for p in points:
            r = _recs(sits, arm, p)
            per = {g: float(np.mean(x["switched"])) for g, x in sorted(r.items())}
            out[arm][int(p)] = dict(mean=_mean(per.values()), per_fly=per)
    return out


def null_curve(sits, pairs, points) -> dict:
    return {arm: {int(p): (diag.empirical_null(list(r.values()), pairs)["mean"] if (r := _recs(sits, arm, p)) else None)
                  for p in points} for arm in ARMS}


def conflict_share(sits, pairs, move_info, points) -> dict:
    out = {}
    for arm in ARMS:
        out[arm] = {}
        for p in points:
            items = []
            for r in _recs(sits, arm, p).values():
                for s in r["situations"]:
                    pr = pairs[s["pair"]]
                    mults = pr["mults1"] if s["side"] == 0 else pr["mults2"]
                    items.append((s["pick"], diag.rule_picks([move_info[c][1] for c in pr["cands"]], mults)))
            c = diag.rule_agreement(items)["conflict"]
            out[arm][int(p)] = dict(n=c["n"], mult_share=c["mult_share"], power_share=c["power_share"])
    return out


def frac_changed(sits, points) -> dict:
    return {arm: {int(p): _mean((r.get("weights") or {}).get("frac_changed") for r in _recs(sits, arm, p).values())
                  for p in points} for arm in ARMS}


def floor_curve(sits, points) -> dict:
    return {arm: {int(p): _mean(r.get("floor_contact") for r in _recs(sits, arm, p).values()) for p in points}
            for arm in ARMS}


def _zero() -> dict:
    return dict(n_reward=0, ms_reward=0.0, n_punish=0, ms_punish=0.0)


def pulse_tables(learn_logs: dict, lay, points, reward: str = "PAM08", punish: str = "PPL105") -> dict:
    pts = sorted(int(p) for p in points)
    names = [f"{a}-{b}" for a, b in zip(pts, pts[1:])]
    by_fly, cum = {}, {}
    for i, recs in learn_logs.items():
        arm, _, g = lay[int(i)]
        by_fly[g] = {n: _zero() for n in names}
        idx = []
        for r in recs:
            if r.get("kind") != "reinforce":
                continue
            b = battle_index(r["battle_id"])
            idx += [b] * len(r.get("pulses") or [])
            for a_, b_, n in zip(pts, pts[1:], names):
                if a_ <= b < b_:
                    for dan, ms in r.get("pulses") or []:
                        key = "reward" if dan == reward else "punish" if dan == punish else None
                        if key:
                            by_fly[g][n][f"n_{key}"] += 1
                            by_fly[g][n][f"ms_{key}"] += float(ms)
        cum[g] = {p: float(sum(1 for b in idx if b < p)) for p in pts}
    by_arm = {}
    cumulative = {}
    for arm in ARMS:
        flies = [g for a, _, g in lay if a == arm and g in by_fly]
        by_arm[arm] = {n: {k: sum(by_fly[g][n][k] for g in flies) for k in _zero()} for n in names}
        cumulative[arm] = {p: _mean(cum[g][p] for g in flies) if flies else None for p in pts}
    return dict(by_arm=by_arm, by_fly=by_fly, cumulative=cumulative)


def floor_reading(floor: dict, read: float) -> dict:
    pts = sorted({int(p) for v in floor.values() for p in v})
    values = {}
    for p in pts:
        xs = [floor[a].get(p) for a in floor if floor[a].get(p) is not None]
        values[p] = max(xs) if xs else None
    over = [p for p in pts if values[p] is not None and values[p] > read]
    first = over[0] if over else None
    ok = [p for p in pts if values[p] is not None and values[p] <= read and (first is None or p < first)]
    return dict(values=values, first_over=first, last_ok=ok[-1] if ok else None, read=float(read))


def learncheck(sits, pairs, move_info, learn_logs, lay, points, read: float) -> dict:
    floor = floor_curve(sits, points)
    return dict(right_share=right_share(sits, pairs, points), centred_v=centred_v(sits, pairs, points),
                switch_curve=switch_curve(sits, points), null=null_curve(sits, pairs, points),
                conflict=conflict_share(sits, pairs, move_info, points), frac_changed=frac_changed(sits, points),
                floor=floor, floor_reading=floor_reading(floor, read), pulses=pulse_tables(learn_logs, lay, points),
                label=LABEL)
```

(`test_floor_reading`에서 0.6 > 0.5인 첫 시점은 40, 그 앞의 0.5 이하 마지막 시점은 20. `test_pulse_tables`의 누적 펄스: 마리 24는 배틀 3에서 2개, 배틀 25에서 1개 → 20 시점 2, 40 시점 3.)

`flymon/ad/report.py`:

```python
"""AD.3's sentence frames (AC.8 with AD's arm names) and the assembled AD result. Every sentence ends with AC.5's LABEL;
check() refuses AC.8's forbidden words in every sentence. Notes: C-off rows carry C_OFF_NOTE, every non-primary row
MULTI_NOTE; under STOP_BUDGET the primaries carry "예산 소진 — 최종 판독 시점 <t>"; at or after the first point whose
floor contact exceeds 0.5 every reading carries "회복 r 보정 범위(1 000펄스) 밖 — 바닥 접촉 <x>" and the result adds the
primary contrast at the last point at or below 0.5."""
from __future__ import annotations

from . import analysis, learncheck as lcm, validity
from .spec import ARM_TEXT, FORBIDDEN, LABEL

RESULT = "results/summary/ad_m4.json"
C_OFF_NOTE = "(C-off는 1단계 0 시점 기록; 6마리 팔의 CI는 불안정할 수 있다)"
MULTI_NOTE = "(기술 통계, 다중 비교 미보정)"
REASON = {"fallback": "대체 80", "STOP_FUTILE": "STOP_FUTILE", "STOP_BUDGET": "예산 소진의 마지막 공통 시점"}


def f3(x) -> str:
    return "기록 없음" if x is None else f"{x:.3f}"


def _ci(c) -> str:
    return f"{c['diff']:.3f} [95% CI {c['lo']:.3f}, {c['hi']:.3f}]"


def primary_sentence(c, ref, null_os, n_later_drop, kc_flyl, notes=()) -> str:
    t = "—" if c["point"] is None else c["point"]
    head = f"FLY-L − FLY-OS(우연 전환 대조, {t}배틀 시점)에서 4.3 기준 1: "
    if c["status"] == "측정 불성립":
        body = head + f"유효 마리 FLY-L {c['n_a']}/{c['min_a']} · FLY-OS {c['n_b']}/{c['min_b']} — 탐색 기준 측정 불성립."
    else:
        body = (head + f"{_ci(c)} — 탐색 기준 {c['status']}. FLY-OS 쪽은 우연 전환이다(자기 잡음 기대치 Σq·q {f3(null_os)}). "
                f"확인 세트 20쌍에 한함(교차 부트스트랩 95% CI {c['crossed']['lo']:.3f}, {c['crossed']['hi']:.3f}, 기술).")
    body += " 기준 행 FLY-L(0) − FLY-OS(0): " + (f"{_ci(ref)}." if "diff" in ref else "측정 불성립.")
    body += f" 유효 마리 FLY-L {c['n_a']} · FLY-OS {c['n_b']}({t}배틀 시점, 이후 탈락 {n_later_drop})."
    for n in notes:
        body += f" {n}"
    return f"{body} {LABEL} 같은 턴 후보 KC 비율 > 2 턴 비율(FLY-L): {f3(kc_flyl)}."


def row_sentence(c, notes=()) -> str:
    if c["status"] == "측정 불성립":
        why = f" — {c['reason']}" if c.get("reason") else ""
        body = (f"{c['name']}: 유효 마리 {ARM_TEXT[c['a']]} {c.get('n_a', 0)}/{c.get('min_a', '—')} · "
                f"{ARM_TEXT[c['b']]} {c.get('n_b', 0)}/{c.get('min_b', '—')}{why} — 측정 불성립.")
    else:
        body = f"{c['name']}: {_ci(c)}"
        if "crossed" in c:
            body += f" (교차 부트스트랩 95% CI {c['crossed']['lo']:.3f}, {c['crossed']['hi']:.3f})"
        body += f" — {c['status']}."
    if "COFF" in (c["a"], c["b"]):
        body += f" {C_OFF_NOTE}"
    body += f" {MULTI_NOTE}"
    for n in notes:
        body += f" {n}"
    return f"{body} {LABEL}"


def unmeasured_sentence(n: int) -> str:
    return f"4.3 기준 {n}: — 탐색 기준 미측정(AC.2 2단계 팔 보류, 부록 AD). {LABEL}"


def summary_sentence(p1, p2, final, reason) -> str:
    if p1["status"] == "충족":
        s = (f"상대 타입을 실제 상대와 무관하게 섞고 총 세기를 같게 둔 우연 전환 대조(FLY-OS)와 비교해, 배틀로 {final}배틀 "
             f"학습한 마리에서 상성 조건부 선택이 더 많이 관찰됐다(탐색, 확인 세트 20쌍에 한함, 학습 중 같은 냄새를 봤을 수 있음).")
    elif p1["status"] == "미충족":
        s = (f"배틀로 {final}배틀 학습한 마리의 상성 조건부 선택이 상대 타입을 섞은 우연 전환 대조(FLY-OS)와 비교해 탐색 기준에 "
             f"못 미쳤다(확인 세트 20쌍에 한함).")
    else:
        s = "AD 1차 대조는 측정 불성립이다(최소 유효 마리 미달 또는 팔 STOP_INFRA)."
    s += " 확인 세트 20쌍은 1단계에서 이미 쓰였고, 그 상황은 학습 중에도 나왔을 수 있다(AC.4 · AD.0 3)."
    if p2["status"] != p1["status"]:
        s += f" 40배틀 시점 대조는 {p2['status']}로 최종 시점과 다르다 — 둘을 합쳐 읽지 않는다."
    if reason != "complete":
        s += f" 최종 판독 시점은 {final}배틀이다({REASON[reason]})."
    return f"{s} {LABEL}"


def futility_sentence(fut) -> str:
    return (f"STOP_FUTILE: {fut['point']}배틀 시점 FLY-L 맞는 방향 쌍 비율 {f3(fut['share_at_point'])}가 0 시점 "
            f"{f3(fut['share_at_0'])}보다 크지 않아 학습을 {fut['point']}배틀에서 멈췄다(판정 아님). 판독은 "
            f"{fut['point']}배틀 시점이다. {LABEL}")


def learncheck_sentence(arm, lc, points, final) -> str:
    rs = " → ".join(f"{p} {f3(lc['right_share'][arm].get(p, {}).get('share'))}" for p in points)
    sc = " → ".join(f"{p} {f3(lc['switch_curve'][arm][p]['mean'])}" for p in sorted(lc["switch_curve"][arm])
                    if p in (points[0], final))
    nul = (lc["null"].get("FLYOS") or {}).get(final)
    return (f"{ARM_TEXT[arm]} 학습 확인(기록, 판정 아님): 맞는 방향 쌍 비율 {rs}; 전환율 {sc}; FLY-OS 잡음 기대치 Σq·q "
            f"{final} {f3(nul)}. {LABEL}")


def check(sentences) -> None:
    for s in sentences:
        bad = [w for w in FORBIDDEN if w in s]
        if bad:
            raise ValueError(f"forbidden words {bad} in a result sentence (AC.8): {s}")


def analyse(*, spec, T, result, sits, pairs, new_pairs, coff, fly1, battles_L, eval_records, all_records,
            learn_logs, nobrain_rows, move_info, kc_by_arm, load_summary) -> dict:
    lay = spec.layout()
    invalid_L = {int(k): v for k, v in result["books"]["L"]["invalid"].items()}
    fut = result.get("futility") or {}
    fp = validity.final_point(spec, T, fut.get("status"), result["status"], sits, lay, invalid_L)
    final, reason = fp["point"], fp["reason"]
    points = [p for p in spec.points(T) if final is not None and p <= final]
    retried = sorted({lay[int(r["fly"])][2] for r in battles_L if int(r.get("retries", 0)) > 0})
    new_pts = [p for p in dict.fromkeys((0, spec.futility_point, final))
               if p is not None and any(r.get("set") == "new" and r.get("point") == p for r in sits)]
    rd = analysis.situation_readings(sits, spec, final=final, new_points=new_pts, coff=coff, fly1=fly1,
                                     retried=retried)
    ev = analysis.eval_readings(result, eval_records, nobrain_rows, spec)
    lc = lcm.learncheck(sits, pairs, move_info, learn_logs, lay, points, spec.floor_read)
    fr = lc["floor_reading"]
    floor_row = None
    if fr["first_over"] is not None and fr["last_ok"] is not None:
        t = fr["last_ok"]
        floor_row = analysis.contrast("1차 1(바닥 접촉 ≤ 0.5 마지막 시점)", "FLYL", "FLYOS",
                                      analysis.switch_units(sits, "FLYL", t), analysis.switch_units(sits, "FLYOS", t),
                                      spec, point=t, rule=True)
    drops = validity.dropouts(invalid_L, lay, sits)
    later = lambda t: sum(1 for d in drops if t is not None and d["last_point"] is not None and d["last_point"] >= t)

    def notes(t) -> list:
        out = []
        if reason == "STOP_BUDGET" and t == final:
            out.append(f"예산 소진 — 최종 판독 시점 {final}")
        if fr["first_over"] is not None and t is not None and t >= fr["first_over"]:
            out.append(f"회복 r 보정 범위(1 000펄스) 밖 — 바닥 접촉 {f3(fr['values'].get(t))}")
        return out

    nul = lc["null"]["FLYOS"]
    p1, p2 = rd["primary"]
    sentences = [primary_sentence(c, rd["reference"], nul.get(c["point"]), later(c["point"]), kc_by_arm.get("FLYL"),
                                  notes(c["point"])) for c in (p1, p2)]
    if floor_row is not None:
        sentences.append(primary_sentence(floor_row, rd["reference"], nul.get(floor_row["point"]), later(floor_row["point"]),
                                          kc_by_arm.get("FLYL")))
    for key in ("did", "aux", "new_set", "paired_os", "sensitivity"):
        sentences += [row_sentence(c, notes(c.get("point"))) for c in rd[key]]
    sentences += [row_sentence(c) for c in ev] + [unmeasured_sentence(4), unmeasured_sentence(5)]
    summary = summary_sentence(p1, p2, final, reason)
    extra = [futility_sentence(fut)] if fut.get("status") == "STOP_FUTILE" else []
    extra += [learncheck_sentence(arm, lc, points, final) for arm in ("FLYL", "FLYOS")] if points else []
    check(sentences + [summary] + extra)
    return dict(appendix="AD", label=LABEL, T=T, final_point=fp, futility=fut or None, run_status=result["status"],
                primary=rd["primary"], reference=rd["reference"], did=rd["did"], aux=rd["aux"], new_set=rd["new_set"],
                paired_os=rd["paired_os"], sensitivity=rd["sensitivity"], floor_row=floor_row, criteria_2_3=ev,
                criteria_4_5=[dict(criterion=n, status="미측정") for n in (4, 5)], learncheck=lc,
                kc_ratio_gt2=kc_by_arm, n_valid_by_point=validity.n_valid_by_point(sits, lay, points),
                dropouts=drops, retried_flies=retried, load=load_summary, sentences=sentences, summary=summary,
                futility_and_learncheck_sentences=extra, boot=dict(draws=spec.boot_draws, seed=spec.boot_seed),
                notes=["다중 비교 보정 없음(AD.0 8): 1차는 최종 시점과 40배틀 시점 두 대조, 나머지는 기술 · 기록이다.",
                       "1차의 추론은 확인 세트 20쌍에 한한다; 교차 부트스트랩 CI는 기술이다(AD.0 3).",
                       "FLY-L − FLY-OS에는 실현된 보상 노출의 차이가 섞인다(AD.0 6).",
                       "기준 4 · 5와 AC.2 2단계 팔은 미측정(보류)이다(AD.0 10).",
                       "AD 결과는 AC.10 문장을 바꾸지 않으며 두 단계의 기준 1을 합치지 않는다(AD.7)."])
```

(`null_curve`의 `:=`는 Python 3.8+ 문법이다 — 이 저장소는 3.13.)

`scripts/analyze_ad.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Spec AD.6 5, last step: AD's reading. Refuses unless the raw-data list verifies (AD.6 5) and the stage-1 reuse fields
are unchanged (STOP_REUSE). Reads results/m4/ad/ (result.json, situation records, learning / evaluation logs,
battles.jsonl), the stage-1 C-off and FLY records, stage 1's RND / MAX results (same evaluation digest), the run load
log; writes results/summary/ad_m4.json with AD.3's sentences and AC.5's label. No PASS / FAIL.

    uv run python scripts/analyze_ad.py --load-log .superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs/ad_run_load.log"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import dataclasses
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

from flymon.ac.analysis import kc_ratio_by_arm
from flymon.ad import hooks, load, raw, report, reuse, run_manifest as rm, store
from flymon.ad.spec import SPEC
from flymon.rescope import blocks


def own_pid(path) -> int:
    for line in Path(path).read_text().splitlines():
        rec = json.loads(line)
        if rec.get("kind") == "start":
            return int(rec.get("own_pid") or 0)
    return 0


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("--root", default=SPEC.out_root)
    ap.add_argument("--load-log", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default=report.RESULT)
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    root = Path(a.root)
    rdoc = json.loads(Path(raw.RAW).read_text()) if Path(raw.RAW).exists() else None
    if rdoc is None:
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {raw.RAW} does not exist (scripts/write_ad_raw_manifest.py first, AD.6 5)")
    bad = raw.verify(rdoc)
    if bad:
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: raw files changed since {raw.RAW}: {bad[:5]}")
    man = json.loads(Path(rm.RUN_MANIFEST).read_text())
    T = int(man["T"])
    spec = dataclasses.replace(SPEC, learn_battles=T)
    result = json.loads((root / "result.json").read_text())
    if result.get("status") not in ("COMPLETE", "STOP_BUDGET"):
        raise SystemExit(f"refusing: {root / 'result.json'} status {result.get('status')!r}")
    doc = json.loads(Path("results/summary/ad_inputs.json").read_text())
    iman = json.loads(Path("results/summary/ad_inputs_manifest.json").read_text())
    now = reuse.reuse_fields(doc["digests"]["confirm"], doc["digests"]["eval"], spec)
    changed = reuse.changed_since(iman.get("reuse"), now)
    if changed:
        raise SystemExit(f"STOP_REUSE: stage-1 fields changed since the input manifest: {changed}")
    p = reuse.stage1_paths(spec)
    s1 = json.loads(p["result"].read_text())
    fly1_valid = [r["fly"] for r in s1["per_fly"] if r["arm"] == "FLY" and not r["invalid"]]
    coff = reuse.coff_records(p["init"], spec.coff_flies)
    fly1 = reuse.stage1_fly_records(p["points"], fly1_valid, spec.stage1_point)
    nob = []
    for arm in ("RND", "MAX"):
        r = json.loads((Path(spec.stage1_root) / arm / "result.json").read_text())
        if r.get("eval_digest") != result["eval_digest"]:
            raise SystemExit(f"refusing: stage-1 {arm} ran another evaluation schedule (AC.4 digest gate)")
        nob += [row for row in r["per_fly"]]
    sits = hooks.ADSituationLog(root / "logs").records()
    learn_logs = {int(q.stem[3:]): blocks.read_jsonl(q) for q in sorted((root / "logs").glob("fly*.jsonl"))}
    eval_recs = [x for q in sorted((root / "logs" / "eval").glob("fly*.jsonl")) for x in blocks.read_jsonl(q)]
    all_recs = [x for v in learn_logs.values() for x in v] + eval_recs
    lay = spec.layout()
    rows = [dict(fly=i, arm=arm, invalid=False) for i, (arm, _, _) in enumerate(lay)]
    from flymon.brain.h4_pairs import pool_vocabulary
    _, mi, _, _ = pool_vocabulary()
    sess = json.loads((root / "wall_clock.json").read_text())["sessions"]
    import datetime as dt
    ts = lambda s: dt.datetime.fromisoformat(s).timestamp()
    start, end = ts(sess[0]["started_utc"]), max(ts(x["ended_utc"]) for x in sess if x.get("ended_utc"))
    lsum = load.summary(load.read(a.load_log), start, end, own_pid(a.load_log), SPEC.busy_samples)
    out = report.analyse(spec=spec, T=T, result=result, sits=sits, pairs=doc["confirm"]["pairs"],
                         new_pairs=doc["new"]["pairs"], coff=coff, fly1=fly1,
                         battles_L=blocks.read_jsonl(root / "logs" / "battles.jsonl"), eval_records=eval_recs,
                         all_records=all_recs, learn_logs=learn_logs, nobrain_rows=nob, move_info=mi,
                         kc_by_arm=kc_ratio_by_arm(all_recs, rows), load_summary=lsum)
    out["sources"] = dict(result=dict(path=str(root / "result.json"), sha256=store.sha256_file(root / "result.json")),
                          raw_manifest=dict(path=raw.RAW, sha256=store.sha256_file(raw.RAW)),
                          run_manifest=dict(path=rm.RUN_MANIFEST, sha256=store.sha256_file(rm.RUN_MANIFEST)))
    store.write_json(a.out, out)
    for s in out["sentences"] + [out["summary"]] + out["futility_and_learncheck_sentences"]:
        print(s, flush=True)
    print(f"wrote {a.out}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`kc_ratio_by_arm(all_recs, rows)`는 레코드의 `fly`(풀 순번)를 `rows`의 팔로 묶는다 — 여기 `rows`는 KC 비율 집계 전용이라 모든 마리를 넣는다(AC.0 6의 "재고"는 기록이다).

- [ ] **Step 4: 통과를 확인한다**

Run: `uv run pytest -q -o addopts="" tests/ad/test_ad_learncheck.py tests/ad/test_ad_report.py tests/ad/test_ad_analysis.py`
Expected: PASS

- [ ] **Step 5: 커밋한다**

```bash
git add flymon/ad/learncheck.py flymon/ad/report.py scripts/analyze_ad.py tests/ad/test_ad_learncheck.py tests/ad/test_ad_report.py
git commit -m "feat(ad): learning-check records, AD.3 sentences with the AC.5 label, analyze_ad.py (raw list and reuse verified first)"
```

## 실행 런북 — 컨트롤러 백그라운드 실행(서브에이전트 작업이 아니다)

아래 R1–R7은 길게 도는 실행이다. 서브에이전트는 출력 없이 600초가 지나면 죽으므로, 컨트롤러가 Bash `run_in_background: true`로 돌리고 끝나면 종료 코드와 마지막 줄 · 오류 줄만 읽는다. 로그는 `L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs`(`mkdir -p $L` 먼저). 각 단계 결과는 다음 단계 전에 커밋한다(AD.6). 커밋 메시지에 트레일러를 넣지 않는다.

**의존:** R1은 Task 1–4 · 14가 커밋된 뒤(나머지 구현과 병행 가능). R2는 Task 1–18 전부와 R1 커밋 뒤. R3은 R2가 `OK`일 때. R4는 R3이 `OK`일 때. R5는 R4의 실행 manifest 커밋 뒤. R6은 R5가 끝났거나(`AD: complete`) `STOP_BUDGET`일 때. R7은 R6 커밋 뒤.

**STOP 처리(AD.7):** `STOP_SMOKE` · `STOP_BALANCE` · `STOP_REUSE`(R2), `STOP_REUSE`(R3), `STOP_BENCH` · `STOP_BUDGET`(R4), 실행 중 `SafetyStop` 트레이스백(= `STOP_SAFETY`), `STOP_MANIFEST`(R5의 거부 메시지), 동결 검사 실패(`STOP_INFRA` 메시지)는 결과 파일이 있으면 커밋하고 멈춰 사용자에게 보고한다. `STOP_FUTILE`과 실행 중 `STOP_BUDGET`은 멈춤이 아니라 판독 경로다(R6으로 간다). 팔 하나의 `STOP_INFRA`(장부의 `stopped`)는 그 팔을 멈춘 채 나머지를 끝내고 분석에서 시점별로 "측정 불성립"이 된다.

### R1 (AD.6 2, 앞부분): KC 활성 보정 측정 → 커밋 (관문 판단 전)

```bash
L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs; mkdir -p $L
uv run python scripts/write_ad_os_calib.py > $L/calib.log 2>&1
git add results/summary/ad_os_kc_calib.json
git commit -m "results(ad): FLY-OS KC-activity calibration on w0 (R <R>, SD_R <sd>, KS <ks>) — measured before the gate"
```

- 출력 마지막 줄의 R · SD_R · KS를 커밋 메시지에 그대로 넣는다. 이 단계는 관문을 판단하지 않는다(판단은 R2의 `check_ad_smoke.py`).

### R2 (AD.6 2): 스모크 · 마이크로 스모크

```bash
L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs
uv run python scripts/write_ad_inputs.py --smoke > $L/smoke_inputs.log 2>&1
uv run python scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py --smoke > $L/smoke_manifest.log 2>&1
uv run python scripts/run_ad.py --smoke --stop-after 3 > $L/smoke_1.log 2>&1
uv run python scripts/run_ad.py --smoke --resume > $L/smoke_2.log 2>&1
uv run pytest -q -o addopts="" > $L/suite.log 2>&1; echo $? > results/m4-smoke/ad/suite_exit.txt
uv run python scripts/check_ad_smoke.py --suite-exit results/m4-smoke/ad/suite_exit.txt > $L/smoke_check.log 2>&1
git add results/summary/ad_smoke.json
git commit -m "results(ad): AD.5 smoke — gates OK (suite, safety 4, stale interleaving, OS draws, global seeds, manifest refusal, stage-1 reproduction, reuse, KC band)"
```

- 첫 스모크 실행은 "stopped before completion … rerun with --resume"로 끝나야 하고(3배틀, 무익 점검 시점 1 전), 둘째가 `result.json`을 쓴다(관문 `complete_after_resume`). 무익 점검 줄(`futility check at 1: …`)은 스모크에서 어느 쪽이든 된다.
- 전체 스위트는 20분 이상 걸린다. 다른 명령과 겹쳐 돌려도 된다(경로가 겹치지 않는다).
- `check_ad_smoke.py` 종료 2(`STOP_SMOKE`) · 4(`STOP_REUSE`) · 5(`STOP_BALANCE`)면 커밋 메시지를 실패한 관문 이름과 코드로 바꿔 커밋하고 멈춘다. `full_suite`가 알려진 간헐 실패(`tests/test_run_m0d_h3.py`) 하나로만 실패했으면 스위트를 한 번 다시 돌리고 둘 다 원장에 적는다. 그래도 실패하면 `STOP_SMOKE`다.

### R3 (AD.6 3): AD 입력 manifest

```bash
L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs
uv run python scripts/write_ad_inputs.py > $L/inputs.log 2>&1
git add results/summary/ad_inputs.json results/summary/ad_inputs_manifest.json
git commit -m "results(ad): inputs — learning schedule 306 (120/80/40 digests), new 20 pairs 308, reuse fields (가) OK"
```

- 종료 2면(`STOP_REUSE` 또는 `STOP_SET`) 두 파일을 커밋하고 멈춘다.

### R4 (AD.6 4): 혼자 재벤치(유효 3회, 시도 ≤ 6) → 외삽 → T → 실행 manifest

부하 기록기를 한 번 띄우고(백그라운드, 모든 시도 동안 유지), 시도마다 조용함을 기다린 뒤 벤치를 돌린다.

```bash
L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs
uv run python scripts/ad_load_log.py --out $L/ad_bench_load.log      # run_in_background: true; R4 끝에 종료
# 시도 N = 1, 2, …(≤ 6). 매번:
uv run python scripts/ad_wait_quiet.py --log $L/ad_bench_load.log > $L/wait_N.log 2>&1   # 0 = 조용함; 3 = 4시간 넘게 바쁨 → 사용자에게 보고
uv run python scripts/write_ad_inputs.py --bench N > $L/bench_inputs_N.log 2>&1
uv run python scripts/run_ad.py --bench N > $L/bench_N.log 2>&1
uv run python scripts/write_ad_budget.py --load-log $L/ad_bench_load.log > $L/budget_N.log 2>&1
#   종료 3 → 다음 N; 종료 0 → 아래 커밋; 종료 2 → STOP_BENCH 또는 STOP_BUDGET (커밋하고 멈춤)
pkill -f scripts/ad_load_log.py
git add results/summary/ad_budget.json
git commit -m "results(ad): re-bench alone (attempts <used>, medians <b> s/batch-battle, <e> s/evaluation) — T <T>, new set <yes/no>, <h> h of 47.418 h"
uv run python scripts/write_ad_run_manifest.py > $L/run_manifest.log 2>&1
git add results/summary/ad_run_manifest.json
git commit -m "results(ad): run manifest — T <T>, points <…>, code <commit12>"
```

- "혼자": 다른 세션의 뇌 · 배틀 실행이 없을 때 시작한다. `ad_wait_quiet.py`가 대기 동안 분마다 `uptime`과 50% 넘는 프로세스를 같은 로그에 적는다.
- 벤치는 시도마다 새 디렉터리(`results/m4-bench/ad-N/`)다. 끝까지 못 간 시도(`bench.json` 없음)도 시도 수에 든다.
- 벤치 무효 판정은 `write_ad_budget.py`가 로그로 한다(벤치 밖 같은 pid가 연속 3표본 50% 초과). 무효 시도의 이유와 프로세스는 `ad_budget.json`의 `attempts`에 남는다.
- 실행 manifest는 코드가 모두 커밋된 상태에서만 써진다. 이 뒤로 `flymon/` · `scripts/run_ad.py`를 고치면 재개가 `STOP_MANIFEST`로 거부된다.

### R5 (AD.6 5): 실행 — 재개 스크립트, caffeinate, 실행 중 부하 기록

`$L/ad_run_resume.sh`(실행 권한, 1단계의 `r4_brain_resume.sh` 형식):

```zsh
#!/bin/zsh
# AD run: the first session, then --resume until complete / STOP_BUDGET (session cap or reboot). One process at a time;
# a log per session; the load logger follows this script's pid ($$: the run's processes are its descendants).
cd /Users/jeonsehyeon/orca/workspaces/fruit-fly/guillemot
L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs
uv run python scripts/ad_load_log.py --out $L/ad_run_load.log --own-pid $$ --until-pid $$ &
for i in 1 2 3 4 5 6; do
  f=$L/ad_run_$(date '+%m%d_%H%M').log
  echo "$(date '+%F %T') session start -> $f" >> $L/ad_progress.log
  if [ -d results/m4/ad/checkpoints ]; then R=(--resume); else R=(); fi
  caffeinate -i uv run python scripts/run_ad.py $R > $f 2>&1
  rc=$?
  last=$(tail -1 $f | cut -c1-200)
  fut=$(grep -m1 "^futility check" $f)
  echo "$(date '+%F %T') rc=$rc $last ${fut:+| $fut}" >> $L/ad_progress.log
  [ $rc -ne 0 ] && { echo "ad rc=$rc"; exit $rc; }
  echo "$last" | grep -q "AD: complete\|STOP_BUDGET" && { echo "ad finished: $last"; exit 0; }
done
echo "ad: 6 sessions without completion"; exit 3
```

```bash
L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs
chmod +x $L/ad_run_resume.sh
$L/ad_run_resume.sh > $L/ad_resume_driver.log 2>&1      # run_in_background: true
```

- 세션은 `min(24 h, 47.418 h − AD 세션 합)`에서 배치 경계로 스스로 닫히고("rerun with --resume"), 스크립트가 다음 세션을 연다. Mac 재부팅 뒤에는 같은 스크립트를 다시 띄운다 — 끊긴 세션은 다음 세션 시작까지 청구된다(AD.4).
- 컨트롤러가 실행 중 읽는 것은 `$L/ad_progress.log`(세션 마지막 줄, 무익 점검 줄)뿐이다. **실행이 끝나기 전에는 무익 점검의 두 수 밖의 상황 쌍 기록 · 전환율을 보지 않는다(AD.3).**
- 마지막 줄 `AD: complete, valid flies …` 또는 `AD: STOP_BUDGET (in run) …`이면 R6으로 간다. `SafetyStop` · `STOP_INFRA` · `STOP_MANIFEST` 트레이스백이면 멈추고 보고한다.

### R6 (AD.6 5): 원자료 목록 → 분석 → 커밋

```bash
L=.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-10-ad-stage2/runs
uv run python scripts/write_ad_raw_manifest.py --extra $L/ad_run_load.log > $L/raw.log 2>&1
uv run python scripts/analyze_ad.py --load-log $L/ad_run_load.log > $L/analysis.log 2>&1
git add results/summary/ad_raw_manifest.json results/summary/ad_m4.json
git commit -m "results(ad): AD stage 2 (exploration, no judgement) — <summary sentence, first clause>"
```

- 원자료 목록은 분석 전에 만든다. 보관본은 `~/flymon-archive/ad-raw-<YYYYMMDD>.tar.gz`(git 밖)이고 그 sha256이 목록에 있다. 복구: 저장소 루트에서 `tar -xzf <보관본>` 뒤 `flymon.ad.raw.verify`.
- 분석 커밋 메시지에는 `summary` 문장의 첫 문장을 그대로 넣는다("PASS"/"FAIL"을 쓰지 않는다).

### R7 (AD.6 6): AD.10 결과 절 · README 장부(ko/en)

컨트롤러가 스펙 문서 끝(AD.9 뒤)에 `### AD.10 결과 (<날짜>, AC 2단계 — 탐색, 판정 없음)`을 쓴다. 넣는 것(모두 `results/summary/ad_*.json`에서 옮긴다, 새 계산 없음):
1. 실행 경로: KC 보정(R, SD_R, 범위, `ad_os_kc_calib.json` 커밋), 스모크 상태, 재벤치(유효 시도, 중앙값 세 값, 무효 시도와 이유), T · 새 세트 여부 · 외삽 시간, 실행 manifest 커밋, 세션 수 · 벽시계 · 끊긴 세션 청구, 실행 중 부하(load1 중앙 · 최대, 실행 밖 50% 초과 프로세스), 낡은 펄스 거부 수(`stale_pulses`), 재시도 마리.
2. 무익 점검 결과(두 수와 결정; `STOP_FUTILE`이면 그 문장).
3. 1차 두 문장(그대로), 고정 기준 행, 요약 문장, 최종 판독 시점과 그 이유.
4. 차이의 차이 · 보조(C-off, 1단계 FLY) · 새 20쌍 · 짝지은 OS · 민감도 · 기준 2 · 3 문장(그대로), 기준 4 · 5 미측정 문장.
5. 학습 확인 문장(FLY-L · FLY-OS), 바닥 접촉 읽기(넘은 시점이 있으면 주석과 병기 행), 보상 · 처벌 합 요약, KC 비율 > 2(두 팔).
6. 원자료 목록 sha256 · 보관본 경로 · sha256, 결과 커밋 해시.
7. AD.0 10의 1단계 끊긴 세션 꼬리(≤ 약 13분, 1 h 예비 안) 공개는 그대로 둔다(고치지 않는다).
모든 문장에 AC.5 라벨, 금지어 없음. README의 한국어 장부(236–240행 근처, "M3 · M4 탐색 단계" 항목 뒤)와 영어 장부(497행 근처)에 같은 내용을 요약해 한 항목씩 더한다(1단계 항목은 고치지 않는다).

```bash
git add docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-14-flymon-design.md README.md
git commit -m "docs(spec,readme): AD.10 result — AC stage 2 (exploration, no judgement)"
```

- push(AD.6 6)는 컨트롤러가 사용자 사전 승인(2026-10-06)과 저장소 메모(`gh auth switch --user lyutvs`, origin main)에 따라 한다. 이 계획의 구현자는 push하지 않는다.

## Self-review (계획 작성 시점)

- **스펙 대응(AD 절마다):**
  - AD 머리말 · AD.0(공개, 판정 아님, 우선순위): Global Constraints, Task 18 문장 · notes, R7.
  - AD.0 5 · AD.1 FLY-OS(16종 전체 독립 균등, 제시마다, 후보 공유, 키 = 일정 battle_id + `battle.turn` / (fly, pair, side), 시드 307, 섀도 기록, 인코더 속성으로 숨기지 않음, 펄스는 본 냄새의 KC에): Task 4, Task 2(`_log`의 `os`, `_on_outcome`은 결정 때 냄새 그대로), Task 7(`os_pair_odours`, 기록의 `os`), Task 13(마리별 `OSEncoder`, `set_battle`).
  - AD.1 팔 · 전역 번호 24–47 · 태그 DL/DE · 같은 학습 행 · C-off 재사용 · 돌리지 않는 팔: Task 1, 5, 6, 13; 모든 시드의 전역 번호: Task 2(강화), 3(결정 · 동률 · 상황), 4(OS).
